### [1] [Struggle as Flow: Challenge, Design, and Experience in Soulslike Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15318)
*Zhehao Sun,Yuanyuan Xu,Chi Zhen,Yin-Shan Lin,Miles Thorogood,Patricia Lasserre,Aleksandra Dulic,Megan Smith*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文挑战传统游戏设计的享乐主义范式，研究"失败悖论"的心理机制，提出"韧性心流"概念，并通过分析《艾尔登法环》《只狼》《黑暗之魂3》的玩家评论验证了这一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统游戏设计强调无障碍体验，但魂类游戏却通过高难度和频繁失败取得了商业成功。论文旨在挑战传统的享乐主义游戏范式，探究"失败悖论"背后的心理机制，理解玩家为何从看似惩罚性的游戏体验中获得满足。

Method: 整合Csikszentmihalyi的心流理论和Juul的游戏学框架，提出"韧性心流"概念。采用定性文本分析方法，从Steam社区平台收集《艾尔登法环》《只狼：影逝二度》《黑暗之魂3》的600条有用用户评论进行分析。

Result: 研究发现长期玩家在语言上将死亡重构为教学而非惩罚，使用与节奏同步和冥想专注相关的词汇。当难度设计具有清晰性和公平性时，能够培养"注意力伦理"，将数字斗争转化为深刻的掌控感和正念体验。

Conclusion: 论文得出结论：精心设计的难度（清晰且公平）能够促进韧性心流状态，使玩家将挫折有意义地框架化，从而将数字斗争转化为掌控感和正念的深刻体验，挑战了传统游戏设计的享乐主义假设。

Abstract: While traditional game design prioritizes friction-free accessibility, the Soulslike subgenre has achieved commercial dominance through punishing difficulty and frequent failure. This paper challenges the conventional hedonistic paradigm of gaming to investigate the psychological mechanisms behind the Paradox of Failure. By integrating Csikszentmihalyi's Flow Theory with Juul's ludological framework, we propose the concept of Resilient Flow. We define this as a cognitive state wherein absorption is maintained not despite frustration but through the meaningful framing of it. To validate this model without invasive laboratory constraints, we conducted a qualitative text analysis of 600 helpful user reviews from Elden Ring, Sekiro: Shadows Die Twice, and Dark Souls III via the Steam Community platform. Findings reveal that long-term players linguistically reframe death as pedagogy rather than punishment and utilize vocabulary associated with rhythmic synchronization and meditative focus. We conclude that when difficulty is designed with clarity and fairness, it fosters an Ethics of Attention and transforms digital struggle into a profound experience of mastery and mindfulness.

</details>


### [2] [Explainable Iterative Data Visualisation Refinement via an LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15319)
*Burak Susam,Tingting Mu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出基于大语言模型的自动化高维数据可视化优化系统，通过语义化评估和超参数优化生成高质量可视化图表


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据可视化中，寻找合适的算法配置（特别是超参数设置）以产生忠实反映数据真实结构并促进模式发现的可视化图表仍然具有挑战性

Method: 提出基于大语言模型的智能AI管道，将可视化评估和超参数优化作为语义任务处理，生成结合定量评估和定性洞察的多方面报告，通过迭代优化循环实现全自动化

Result: 系统能够快速生成高质量的可视化图表，将严格的定量评估与定性的人类洞察相结合，提供可操作的算法配置建议

Conclusion: 该智能AI管道通过语义化方法有效解决了高维数据可视化中的超参数优化挑战，实现了从定量评估到定性洞察的桥梁，能够自动化生成高质量的可视化结果

Abstract: Exploratory analysis of high-dimensional data relies on embedding the data into a low-dimensional space (typically 2D or 3D), based on which visualization plot is produced to uncover meaningful structures and to communicate geometric and distributional data characteristics. However, finding a suitable algorithm configuration, particularly hyperparameter setting, to produce a visualization plot that faithfully represents the underlying reality and encourages pattern discovery remains challenging. To address this challenge, we propose an agentic AI pipleline that leverages a large language model (LLM) to bridge the gap between rigorous quantitative assessment and qualitative human insight. By treating visualization evaluation and hyperparameter optimization as a semantic task, our system generates a multi-faceted report that contextualizes hard metrics with descriptive summaries, and suggests actionable recommendation of algorithm configuration for refining data visualization. By implementing an iterative optimization loop of this process, the system is able to produce rapidly a high-quality visualization plot, in full automation.

</details>


### [3] [Towards Measuring Interactive Visualization Abilities: Connecting With Existing Literacies and Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15320)
*Gabriela Molina León,Benjamin Bach,Matheus Valentim,Niklas Elmqvist*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该立场论文讨论了当前可视化素养测试的局限性，并提出评估交互式数据可视化能力的方法框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的可视化素养测试（如VLAT及其衍生工具）仅涉及静态可视化，缺乏评估人们与数据可视化交互能力的正式方法。随着交互式可视化在数据分析和决策中的重要性日益增加，需要开发能够评估交互能力的评估工具。

Method: 作者通过讨论相关的素养概念和评估方法，提出并比较了不同的评估方法，用于评估人们在交互式感知任务中使用可视化时所依赖的能力。

Result: 本文提出了一个评估交互式可视化能力的框架，并比较了不同的评估方法，为未来开发正式的交互式可视化素养测试奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 需要开发新的评估方法来衡量人们在交互式数据可视化中的能力，这有助于更好地理解和提升用户在交互式数据分析任务中的表现。

Abstract: How do we assess people's abilities to interact with data visualizations? The current state-of-the-art visualization literacy tests -- such as VLAT and its derivatives -- only involve the use of static visualizations. Despite advances in investigating multiple visualization abilities, we do not yet have formal methods to assess the ability of a person to interact with a data visualization effectively. In this position paper, we discuss related literacy concepts and assessments to propose and compare different approaches for assessing the abilities that people leverage to use visualizations in interactive sensemaking tasks.

</details>


### [4] [NEFFY 2.0: A Breathing Companion Robot: User-Centered Design and Findings from a Study with Ukrainian Refugees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15325)
*Ilona Buchem,Jessica Kazubski,Charly Goerke*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: NEFFY 2.0是一款用于压力缓解的触觉慢呼吸社交机器人，针对乌克兰难民的研究显示其在主观压力降低方面优于纯音频条件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为经历长期焦虑的个体（特别是难民等脆弱人群）提供低门槛、易获取的慢呼吸指导，通过具身化和多感官交互增强压力缓解效果

Method: 采用用户中心设计开发NEFFY 2.0机器人，通过实验比较机器人辅助呼吸干预与纯音频条件，测量主观评分和生理指标（心率、心率变异性、呼吸率、皮电反应），结合访谈收集定性数据，使用k-means聚类识别呼吸模式

Result: 定性结果显示NEFFY 2.0被感知为直观、镇静且支持性强；调查显示机器人条件下感知压力显著降低的效果远大于纯音频条件；生理数据结果混合且个体间差异大；识别出三种呼吸实践模式

Conclusion: NEFFY 2.0是一种有前景的低门槛压力缓解工具，为脆弱人群提供了机器人辅助与非机器人条件对比的实证证据，支持人机交互赋能社会的愿景

Abstract: This paper presents the design of NEFFY 2.0, a social robot designed as a haptic slow-paced breathing companion for stress reduction, and reports findings from a mixed-methods user study with 14 refugees from Ukraine. Developed through a user-centered design process, NEFFY 2.0 builds on NEFFY 1.0 and integrates embodiment and multi-sensory interaction to provide low-threshold, accessible guidance of slow-paced breathing for stress relief, which may be particularly valuable for individuals experiencing prolonged periods of anxiety. To evaluate effectiveness, an experimental comparison of a robot-assisted breathing intervention versus an audio-only condition was conducted. Measures included subjective ratings and physiological indicators, such as heart rate (HR), heart rate variability (HRV) using RMSSD parameter, respiratory rate (RR), and galvanic skin response (GSR), alongside qualitative data from interviews exploring user experience and perceived support. Qualitative findings showed that NEFFY 2.0 was perceived as intuitive, calming and supportive. Survey results showed a substantially larger effect in significant reduction of perceived stress in the NEFFY 2.0 condition compared to audio-only. Physiological data reveled mixed results combined with large inter-personal variability. Three patterns of breathing practice with NEFFY 2.0 were identified using k-means clustering. Despite the small sample size, this study makes a novel contribution by providing empirical evidence of stress reduction in a vulnerable population through a direct comparison of robot-assisted and non-robot conditions. The findings position NEFFY 2.0 as a promising low-threshold tool that supports stress relief and contributes to the vision of HRI empowering society.

</details>


### [5] [Beyond Content Exposure: Systemic Factors Driving Moderators' Mental Health Crisis in Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15321)
*Nuredin Ali Abdelkadir,Tianling Yang,Shivani Kapania,Kauna Ibrahim Malgwi,Fasica Berhane Gebrekidan,Adio-Adet Dinika,Elaine O. Nsoesie,Milagros Miceli,Stevie Chancellor*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 非洲内容审核员面临严重的心理健康问题，研究发现其心理困扰程度高于其他地区审核员，且离职后影响持续存在，系统性劳动条件是主要影响因素，企业健康计划效果有限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲内容审核员的心理健康状况被报告为特别严重，研究旨在探究除了审核内容本身之外，还有哪些因素导致这些工作者心理健康恶化

Method: 对非洲134名内容审核员进行问卷调查以了解其心理健康状况，并对15名审核员进行访谈以深入了解其经历和体验

Result: 非洲审核员相比其他地区审核员表现出更高的心理困扰和更低的心理幸福感；离职审核员的困扰程度显著更高，表明影响具有长期性；访谈显示系统性劳动条件是导致严重心理困扰和幸福感降低的主要因素；平台推广的企业健康计划被证明无效且不足

Conclusion: 需要所有相关方采取整体关注和结构性解决方案来改善审核员的心理健康，单纯依靠企业健康计划是不够的

Abstract: Content moderators review disturbing content to protect social media users, often at significant cost to their mental health. Recent reports document the mental health conditions of African moderators as notably problematic. Beyond the content itself, what factors contribute to the deteriorating mental health of these workers? We surveyed 134 moderators across Africa to understand their mental health and interviewed 15 moderators to contextualize their experiences. We found that African moderators suffer from high psychological distress and lower well-being compared to moderators in other areas. Former moderators showed significantly higher distress levels, demonstrating long term impact that extends beyond their moderation work. Our interviews showed that systemic and structural labor conditions contribute to moderators' severe psychological distress and diminished mental well-being. Corporate wellness programs promoted by platforms were found ineffective and inadequate. We discuss how this requires holistic attention and structural solutions by all involved parties to improve moderators' mental health.

</details>


### [6] [Acoustic and Facial Markers of Perceived Conversational Success in Spontaneous Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15322)
*Thanushi Withanage,Elizabeth Redcay,Carol Espy-Wilson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究分析了Zoom自然对话中多模态特征与感知对话质量的关系，发现语音趋同现象与更高感知成功相关


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然任务导向对话中的语音趋同现象已有充分研究，但对自然、非任务导向的虚拟环境中趋同现象了解较少，需要探索对话动态与感知交互质量的关系

Method: 分析大规模自发Zoom对话语料库，提取包括话轮转换、停顿、面部动作、音高和强度等声学特征的多模态特征，通过事后评分因子分析量化感知对话成功

Result: 在自发语音中可靠检测到趋同现象，且趋同与更高的感知对话成功相关，识别出对话质量的关键交互标记

Conclusion: 研究揭示了自然虚拟对话中趋同现象的重要性，为针对性干预促进更有效和吸引人的沟通提供了机会

Abstract: Individuals often align their speaking patterns with their interlocutors, a phenomenon linked to engagement and rapport. While well documented in task-oriented dialogues, less is known about entrainment in naturalistic, non-task and virtual settings. In this study, we analyze a large corpus of spontaneous dyadic Zoom conversations to examine how conversational dynamics relate to perceived interaction quality. We extract multimodal features encompassing turn-taking, pauses, facial movements, and acoustic measures such as pitch and intensity. Perceived conversational success was quantified via factor analysis of post-conversation ratings. Results demonstrate that entrainment reliably detected in spontaneous speech and correlates with higher perceived success. These findings identify key interactional markers of conversational quality and highlight opportunities for targeted interventions to foster more effective and engaging communication.

</details>


### [7] [A Systematic Review of User Experiments Measuring the Effects of Dark Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15323)
*Brennan Schaffner,Luis Heysen,Marshini Chetty*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该综述论文系统分析了欺骗性/操纵性界面设计（暗黑模式）的实验研究，发现这些设计确实显著影响用户行为，但干预措施大多无效，且影响在不同人群中差异不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管欺骗性/操纵性界面设计（暗黑模式）的伦理和法律影响已受到广泛关注，但关于其实际效果的经验证据仍然分散。本研究旨在综合最新的实验研究，量化分析这些设计如何（或是否）影响用户行为。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，综合分析了最新的实验研究，重点关注量化欺骗性/操纵性界面设计对用户影响的研究，并汇总了旨在减少这些设计效果的干预措施的研究结果。

Result: 实验研究一致表明：1）欺骗性/操纵性界面设计确实显著改变用户行为（效应大小差异较大）；2）外部干预措施大多未能有效减轻这些设计的影响；3）设计效果与个人特征（如年龄或政治倾向）之间的显著相关性不常见，表明这些设计几乎对所有测试人群产生相似影响。

Conclusion: 通过总结实验证据，本研究阐明了欺骗性/操纵性界面设计的影响，突出了现有实验文献中的空白和矛盾，为正在进行的研究和政策制定提供了信息支持。

Abstract: Deceptive/Manipulative Patterns (DMP) are interface designs, also known as ``dark patterns,'' that manipulate user behavior. While considerable attention has been paid to their ethical and legal implications, empirical evidence about their real-world effects remains diffuse. This review synthesizes up-to-date experimental studies, focusing on works that quantify how (or whether) DMPs influence users. We also aggregate findings on interventions aimed at reducing DMP effects. Our synthesis highlights the experimental agreement that DMPs do significantly alter user behavior (with large variance in effect size) and that external interventions have been mostly unsuccessful in mitigating their effects. Lastly, we show that significant correlations between DMP effects and personal characteristics (e.g., age or political affiliation) are uncommon, indicating DMPs similarly affected nearly all populations tested. By summarizing the experimental evidence, we clarify the effects of DMPs, highlight gaps and tensions in the existing experimental literature, and help inform ongoing research and policy directions.

</details>


### [8] [Struggle Premium : How Human Effort and Imperfection Drive Perceived Value in the Age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15324)
*Nazneen Sultana,Mst Rafia Islam,Md. Tanvir Hossain,Azmine Toushik Wasi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨"奋斗溢价"现象，即感知到的人类努力为创意作品带来的附加价值，分析可见努力线索如何影响人类与AI生成作品的评价


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI进入创意实践，观众在判断作品真实性和价值时面临日益增长的不确定性，需要研究人类努力线索如何影响对创意作品的评价

Method: 对70名大学生进行问卷调查，重点关注过程视频、时间记录、书面解释和瑕疵等可见努力线索，分析这些线索如何影响对人类和AI生成创意作品的真实性、价值判断和支付意愿

Result: 过程导向线索（特别是视频和时间记录）对真实性和价值判断影响最大，瑕疵影响有限；72.9%参与者愿意为人类作品支付更多；努力线索也能改善AI生成内容的感知，表明过程透明度可以部分弥补真实性差距

Conclusion: 研究将努力启发式扩展到算法创造力领域，为设计透明的人机创意系统提供启示，表明过程透明度有助于弥合人类与AI生成作品之间的真实性差距

Abstract: As AI enters creative practice, audiences face growing uncertainty in judging authenticity and value. This study examines the Struggle Premium, the added value attributed to perceived human effort, by analyzing how visible effort cues influence evaluations of human- and AI-generated creative works. We surveyed 70 university students, focusing on process videos, time documentation, written explanations, and imperfections. Process-oriented cues, especially videos and time spent, most strongly shaped authenticity and value judgments, while imperfections had limited impact. Participants showed a clear preference for human-made works, with 72.9% willing to pay more. Notably, effort cues also improved perceptions of AI-generated content, suggesting that process transparency can partially bridge authenticity gaps. These findings extend the effort heuristic to algorithmic creativity and inform the design of transparent human-AI creative systems.

</details>


### [9] [Analyzing the Presentation, Content, and Utilization of References in LLM-powered Conversational AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15326)
*Jianheng Ouyang,Arpit Narechania*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了9个对话AI系统的参考文献呈现方式与质量，发现不同系统在参考文献数量、质量及用户交互行为上存在显著差异，强调需要改进界面设计以提升用户对参考文献的信任与使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话AI系统在信息检索和问答中日益普及，其引用的参考文献对于确保回答的可靠性和可信度至关重要。然而，此前没有研究系统分析这些参考文献的呈现方式或质量，因此需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 研究分析了9个对话AI系统的30个问答对中的1,517个参考文献，重点关注两个方面：(1) 用户界面中的参考文献呈现方式；(2) 使用CRAAP标准（货币性、相关性、权威性、准确性、目的性）评估参考文献质量。此外，还进行了初步用户研究，观察用户与参考文献的交互行为。

Result: 研究发现不同系统在参考文献呈现、质量和数量上存在显著差异。例如，ChatGPT平均每个回答提供9.5个参考文献，CRAAP得分为15.48/20（质量较高）；而Hunyuan-TurboS平均提供4.0个参考文献，CRAAP得分为11.65/20（质量较低）。用户研究表明，用户很少与这些参考文献互动，且互动行为在不同系统间存在差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了需要改进对话AI系统的界面设计，以帮助用户更有效地参与和使用参考文献，从而增强对AI回答的信任。参考文献的呈现方式和质量直接影响用户对AI系统可靠性的认知。

Abstract: As conversational AI systems become popular for information retrieval and question-answering, the references they cite are key to ensuring their answers are reliable and trustworthy. Yet, no prior work systematically analyzes how these references are presented or their quality. We examine 1,517 references from 30 question-answer pairs across nine systems, focusing on their (1) presentation in the user interface and (2) quality using the CRAAP criteria. We find notable variations in the presentation, quality, and quantity of references across systems. For instance, ChatGPT provides more references (9.5 per response on average) with higher quality (15.48/20 CRAAP score), while Hunyuan-TurboS provides fewer references (4.0) and lower quality (11.65/20). Additionally, a preliminary user study shows that people rarely interact with these references and that their behavior differs across systems. These findings highlight the need for better interface designs that help users engage with and trust references more effectively.

</details>


### [10] [Interpupillary Distance Constraints in Pediatric VR: Implications for Psychology and Psychotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15328)
*Grzegorz Pochwatko*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 消费级VR头显的瞳距限制在儿童心理学研究和治疗中是一个被忽视的方法学和临床问题，可能导致儿童被排除或获得劣质感知体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR在心理学研究和治疗中的应用日益增多，但大多数消费级头显主要基于成人人体测量学设计，包括成人瞳距范围。这种设计可能导致儿童被排除在研究之外，或获得系统性的劣质感知体验，从而影响研究参与度、治疗耐受性和结果推广性。

Method: 使用已发表的发育瞳距数据、官方头显规格说明以及儿科和青少年VR研究案例，分析人体测量学不匹配问题。以Meta Quest 3为例进行研究，展示瞳距限制如何不成比例地影响年幼儿童和瞳距分布较低的个体。

Result: 人体测量学不匹配问题可能不成比例地影响年幼儿童和瞳距分布较低的个体。头显适配问题会影响视觉舒适度、深度感知、注意力负荷、晕动症、模拟停留意愿和临场感，进而影响参与度、情绪处理、退出率和治疗反应。

Conclusion: 儿科VR研究和治疗应将头显兼容性视为心理学方法的一部分，而不仅仅是背景技术细节。头显可能作为选择机制，影响研究参与者的纳入、干预耐受性和结果推广性。

Abstract: Virtual reality (VR) is increasingly used across psychology, from research and assessment to counseling, psychological treatment, and psychotherapy, with growing applications for children and adolescents. In these contexts, VR is often treated as a relatively neutral delivery medium. This assumption may be misleading. Most consumer head-mounted displays (HMDs) have been designed primarily for adult anthropometry, including adult interpupillary distance (IPD) ranges. As a result, some children may be excluded from participation or may receive a systematically degraded perceptual experience because the device cannot be adequately aligned to their visual anatomy. This paper argues that IPD constraints in consumer VR headsets represent an underrecognized methodological and clinical problem in pediatric psychology and psychotherapy. If headset fit affects visual comfort, depth perception, attentional load, cybersickness, willingness to remain in the simulation, and sense of presence, it may also influence engagement, emotional processing, dropout, and treatment response. The headset may therefore function as a selection mechanism, shaping who is included in studies, who can tolerate intervention, and to whom findings can be generalized. Using published developmental IPD data, official headset specifications, and examples from pediatric and youth-oriented VR studies, we show that anthropometric mismatch is likely to disproportionately affect younger children and those at the lower end of the IPD distribution. Using Meta Quest 3 as a case study, we argue that pediatric VR research and therapy should treat headset compatibility as part of psychological method rather than as background technical detail.

</details>


### [11] [Evaluating LLMs as Human Surrogates in Controlled Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15329)
*Adnan Hoq,Tim Weninger*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: LLM生成数据与人类实验数据的比较研究：发现LLM能复现人类方向性效应，但效应大小和调节模式存在差异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地用于模拟人类行为研究中的响应，需要明确LLM生成数据何时能支持与人类数据相同的实验推断

Method: 将人类观察转换为结构化提示，让现成的LLM生成0-10结果变量，不进行任务特定训练，对人工和合成响应应用相同的统计分析

Result: LLM能复现人类观察到的多个方向性效应，但效应大小和调节模式在不同模型间存在差异；现成LLM在受控条件下能捕捉总体信念更新模式，但不能一致匹配人类尺度效应

Conclusion: 现成LLM生成的数据可作为行为替代品，但需要明确其适用范围，澄清了LLM生成数据何时能作为行为替代品

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to simulate human responses in behavioral research, yet it remains unclear when LLM-generated data support the same experimental inferences as human data. We evaluate this by directly comparing off-the-shelf LLM-generated responses with human responses from a canonical survey experiment on accuracy perception. Each human observation is converted into a structured prompt, and models generate a single 0--10 outcome variable without task-specific training; identical statistical analyses are applied to human and synthetic responses. We find that LLMs reproduce several directional effects observed in humans, but effect magnitudes and moderation patterns vary across models. Off-the-shelf LLMs therefore capture aggregate belief-updating patterns under controlled conditions but do not consistently match human-scale effects, clarifying when LLM-generated data can function as behavioral surrogates.

</details>


### [12] [Making It Work Is the Work: Engineering Maturity as Epistemic Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15330)
*Danny Leen,Stig Konings,Raf Ramakers,Kris Luyten*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文指出HCI/制造系统原型难以重用和推广的根本原因是缺乏系统行为知识，提出将工程成熟度重构为认知工作，并引入Fab-ilities六个维度来描述制造系统的可转移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多HCI/制造系统作为原型很有吸引力，但难以重用、扩展或超越原始出版物传播。常见解释是需要时间，但作者认为问题更根本：系统在不同材料、机器和用户中的行为知识在发表时通常不存在，因为生成这些知识的工作很少得到激励或奖励。

Method: 基于工程认识论和系统导向HCI的先前辩论，将工程成熟度重构为认知工作：持续工程努力产生原型无法揭示的知识。提出Fab-ilities六个维度作为词汇来描述制造系统哪些方面已确立、哪些知识仍隐性：(1)可构建性、(2)可执行性、(3)可靠性、(4)可维护性、(5)可转移性、(6)可扩展性。通过五个自身项目(JigFab, StoryStick++, Silicone Devices, LamiFold, PaperPulse)的传播尝试(商业化、衍生公司、市场探索)来验证。

Result: 五个项目的传播尝试各自暴露了发表内容与实际转移需求之间的不同差距，验证了Fab-ilities框架的有效性。这些差距揭示了原型系统向可转移系统转化所需的额外认知工作。

Conclusion: HCI/制造系统的可转移性问题根源在于缺乏系统行为知识，而非单纯时间问题。Fab-ilities框架提供了描述和评估制造系统成熟度的词汇，将工程成熟度视为认知工作有助于激励和奖励产生可转移知识所需的持续工程努力。

Abstract: Many HCIxfabrication systems are compelling as prototypes but remain difficult to reuse, extend, or transfer beyond their original publication. A common explanation is that adoption simply takes time. We argue that the issue is more fundamental. The knowledge needed to make fabrication systems transferable, namely how they behave across different materials, machines, and users, usually does not exist at the time of publication because the work required to generate this knowledge is rarely incentivized or rewarded. Drawing on engineering epistemology and prior debates in systems-oriented HCI, we reframe engineering maturity as epistemic work: sustained engineering effort that produces knowledge which prototyping alone cannot reveal. We propose six dimensions, Fab-ilities, as a vocabulary to describe what aspects of fabrication artifacts have become established and what knowledge remains tacit: (1) buildability, (2) executability, (3) reliability, (4) maintainability, (5) transferability, and (6) scalability. We describe five of our own projects (JigFab, StoryStick++, Silicone Devices, LamiFold, and PaperPulse), where varied attempts at dissemination, such as commercialization, spin-offs, and market exploration, each exposed different gaps between what we published and what transfer actually required.

</details>


### [13] [How people use Copilot for Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15331)
*Beatriz Costa-Gomes,Pavel Tolmachev,Eloise Taysom,Viknesh Sounderajah,Hannah Richardson,Philipp Schoenegger,Xiaoxuan Liu,Matthew M Nour,Seth Spielman,Samuel F. Way,Yash Shah,Michael Bhaskar,Harsha Nori,Christopher Kelly,Peter Hames,Bay Gross,Mustafa Suleyman,Dominic King*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究分析了2026年1月超过50万次与Microsoft Copilot的健康相关对话，开发了包含12个主要类别的分层意图分类法，揭示了用户向对话式AI咨询健康问题的五大关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解人们如何使用对话式AI进行健康咨询，包括查询意图、主题分布、使用模式差异，为平台设计、安全考虑和健康AI的负责任开发提供依据。

Method: 使用隐私保护的基于LLM的分类方法开发了12个主要类别的分层意图分类法，并通过专家人工标注验证；应用LLM驱动的主题聚类分析每个意图内的流行主题；分析查询意图、对象、设备类型和时间模式。

Result: 五大关键发现：1）近五分之一对话涉及个人症状评估或疾病讨论；2）七分之一的个人健康查询涉及他人（如子女、父母、伴侣）；3）症状和情绪健康查询在夜间显著增加；4）移动设备集中于个人健康问题，桌面设备主要用于专业和学术工作；5）大量查询关注医疗系统导航（如寻找医生、理解保险）。

Conclusion: 对话式AI在健康咨询中扮演重要角色，特别是在传统医疗服务受限时段和作为护理工具方面。使用模式因设备和时间而异，大量查询关注医疗系统导航问题。这些发现对平台特定设计、安全考虑和健康AI的负责任开发具有直接意义。

Abstract: We analyze over 500,000 de-identified health-related conversations with Microsoft Copilot from January 2026 to characterize what people ask conversational AI about health. We develop a hierarchical intent taxonomy of 12 primary categories using privacy-preserving LLM-based classification validated against expert human annotation, and apply LLM-driven topic-clustering for prevalent themes within each intent. Using this taxonomy, we characterize the intents and topics behind health queries, identify who these queries are about, and analyze how usage varies by device and time of day. Five findings stand out. First, nearly one in five conversations involve personal symptom assessment or condition discussion, and even the dominant general information category (40%) is concentrated on specific treatments and conditions, suggesting that this is a lower bound on personal health intent. Second, one in seven of these personal health queries concern someone other than the user, such as a child, a parent, a partner, suggesting that conversational AI can be a caregiving tool, not just a personal one. Third, personal queries about symptoms and emotional health queries increase markedly in the evening and nighttime hours, when traditional healthcare is most limited. Fourth, usage diverges sharply by device: mobile concentrates on personal health concerns, while desktop is dominated by professional and academic work. Fifth, a substantial share of queries focuses on navigating healthcare systems such as finding providers, and understanding insurance, highlighting friction in the delivery of existing healthcare. These patterns have direct implications for platform-specific design, safety considerations, and the responsible development of health AI.

</details>


### [14] [Automating Crash Diagram Generation Using Vision-Language Models: A Case Study on Multi-Lane Roundabouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15332)
*Xiao Lu,Hao Zhen,Jidong J. Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 使用视觉语言模型自动从事故报告中生成事故示意图，在复杂多车道环岛场景测试，GPT-4o表现最佳


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统事故示意图手动制作耗时且存在人为差异，需要自动化解决方案提高交通安全分析的效率和一致性

Method: 开发三部分结构化提示框架引导模型推理（解释、提取、视觉合成），设计10指标评估系统，测试GPT-4o、Gemini-1.5-Flash和Janus-4o三个模型在79份事故报告上的表现

Result: GPT-4o平均得分最高（6.29/10），其次是Gemini-1.5-Flash（5.28）和Janus-4o（3.64），GPT-4o在空间推理和提取数据与可视化对齐方面表现最优

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型在工程可视化任务中具有潜力但仍有限制，为将生成式AI集成到事故分析工作流程以提高效率、一致性和可解释性奠定了基础

Abstract: Crash diagrams are essential tools in transportation safety analysis, yet their manual preparation remains time-consuming and prone to human variability. This study investigates the use of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to automate crash diagram generation from police crash reports, focusing on multilane roundabouts as a challenging test case. A three-part structured prompt framework was developed to guide model reasoning through interpretation, extraction, and visual synthesis, while a 10-metric evaluation system was designed to assess diagram quality in terms of semantic accuracy, spatial fidelity, and visual clarity. Three popular models, including GPT-4o, Gemini-1.5-Flash, and Janus-4o, were tested on 79 crash reports. GPT-4o achieved the highest average performance (6.29 out of 10), followed by Gemini-1.5-Flash (5.28) and Janus-4o (3.64). The analysis revealed GPT-4o's superior spatial reasoning and alignment between extracted and visualized crash data. These results highlight both the promise and current limitations of VLMs in engineering visualization tasks. The study lays the groundwork for integrating generative AI into crash analysis workflows to improve efficiency, consistency, and interpretability.

</details>


### [15] [Technically Love: The Evolution of Human-AI Romance Discourse on Reddit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15333)
*Tyler Chang,Jina Huh-Yoo,Afsaneh Razi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析Reddit上2017-2025年3383个自曝与AI浪漫关系的帖子，发现公众讨论从积极的亲密关系转向平台治理、技术问题和现实后果，揭示了人机浪漫关系从私人体验到技术中介与监管的框架转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人机浪漫关系日益普遍，但缺乏对公众讨论如何随时间演变的长时分析。先前研究关注用户体验和伦理问题，但缺少对用户发起的公共讨论的纵向分析。

Method: 使用高精度数据集（3383个Reddit自曝帖子，2017-2025年），采用主题建模和时间统计分析来识别主导主题及其随时间演变。

Result: 发现显著的主题漂移：讨论从积极的亲密关系转向平台治理、技术问题和现实后果。这些转变表明人机浪漫关系的框架从私人体验转向技术中介与监管。

Conclusion: 人机浪漫关系的公众讨论框架正在转变，从私人体验转向技术中介与监管，这对伴侣AI系统的设计和治理具有重要启示。

Abstract: Human-AI romantic relationships are increasingly common, yet little is understood about how public discourse around them emerges and shifts over time. Prior research has examined user experiences and ethical concerns, but lacks longitudinal analyses of user-initiated public discussions. We address this gap by analyzing a high-precision dataset of 3,383 self-disclosed romantic companion AI posts from Reddit (2017-2025), using topic modeling and temporal statistical analysis to identify dominant themes and their evolution over time. We find significant topic drift, with discussions moving away from positive intimate relationships toward platform governance, technical issues, and real-world consequences. These shifts highlight a transition in how human-AI romance is framed-moving from private experiences to technical mediation and regulation-with implications for the design and governance of companion AI systems.

</details>


### [16] [Beyond Passive Viewing: A Pilot Study of a Hybrid Learning Platform Augmenting Video Lectures with Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15334)
*Mohammed Abraar,Raj Abhijit Dandekar,Rajat Dandekar,Sreedath Panat*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AI增强视频平台通过集成对话式AI导师，相比传统视频教学显著提升了学习效果和参与度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线AI教育规模快速增长，但传统视频教学在维持学习者参与度和促进深度概念掌握方面存在系统性不足，需要探索更有效的教学方法

Method: 采用顺序被试内设计，58名参与者先完成传统视频教学，再使用AI增强视频平台；测量即时后测和2周延迟保留测试；使用行为分析评估参与度

Result: AI增强教学后即时测试成绩显著提高（91.8 vs 83.5，p<.001，效应量d=1.505）；参与时长增加71.1%；提供了对话式AI导师可能增强传统教育交付的初步证据

Conclusion: 对话式AI导师与传统视频教学结合可能提升学习效果，为开发可扩展的适应性学习系统提供了潜在途径，但需要更大规模研究验证

Abstract: The exponential growth of AI education has brought millions of learners to online platforms, yet this massive scale has simultaneously exposed critical pedagogical shortcomings. Traditional video-based instruction, while cost-effective and scalable, demonstrates systematic failures in both sustaining learner engagement and facilitating the deep conceptual mastery essential for AI literacy. We present a pilot study evaluating a novel hybrid learning platform that integrates real-time conversational AI tutors with traditional video lectures. Our controlled experiment (N = 58, mean age M = 21.4, SD = 2.8) compared traditional video-based instruction with our AI-augmented video platform. This study employed a sequential within-subjects design where all participants first completed the traditional video condition followed by the AI-augmented condition, providing direct comparisons of learning outcomes. We measured learning effectiveness through immediate post-tests and delayed retention assessments (2-week delay). Results suggest improvements in learning performance: immediate post-test performance showed a large effect size (d = 1.505) with participants scoring 8.3 points higher after AI-augmented instruction (91.8 vs 83.5 out of 100, p < .001). Behavioral analytics revealed increased engagement duration (71.1% improvement with AI tutoring) in the experimental group. This pilot study provides preliminary evidence that conversational AI tutors may enhance traditional educational delivery, suggesting a potential avenue for developing scalable, adaptive learning systems.

</details>


### [17] [A Comparative Study on the Impact of Traditional Learning and Interactive Learning on Students' Academic Performance and Emotional Well-Being](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15335)
*Siva Raja Sindiramutty*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 比较传统与互动学习方法对大学生学术表现、参与度、动机和情感健康的影响，发现互动学习组在成绩、参与度和情感健康方面显著优于传统组，但存在认知负荷问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着互动学习工具在高等教育中的日益普及，本研究旨在比较传统学习方法和互动学习方法对学生学术表现、参与度、动机和情感健康的影响，探索互动学习工具的实际效果。

Method: 研究采用随机分组实验设计，将100名计算机入侵检测课程的大学生分为传统学习组（讲座和笔记）和互动学习组（使用Kahoot、Panopto、Slido、Quizizz、Padlet和教学视频等工具）。通过前测、后测、期末考试和作业评估学术成就，使用经过验证的李克特量表问卷评估参与度和情感状态。

Result: 互动学习组在后测（67.48% vs. 53.36%）和期末考试（80.8% vs. 61.44%）中显著优于传统组。互动学习者在行为参与度（+67.01%）和情感参与度（+75.32%）方面也更高，情感健康改善表现为积极情绪增加（+66.67%）和挫败感减少。但认知参与度显著下降（-39.8%），表明可能存在认知超负荷。

Conclusion: 研究证实了互动学习的教学潜力，但强调了需要平衡刺激和认知负荷。建议未来研究使用更大、更多样化的样本来推广和最大化学习成果。

Abstract: The growing adoption of interactive learning tools in higher education offers new opportunities to enhance student performance and well-being. This study compares the effects of traditional and interactive learning methods on academic performance, engagement, motivation, and emotional well-being among 100 university students enrolled in a computer intrusion detection course. Participants were randomly assigned to either a traditional learning group (lectures and notes) or an interactive learning group utilising tools such as Kahoot, Panopto, Slido, Quizizz, Padlet, and educational videos. Academic achievement was measured through pre-tests, post-tests, final exams, and assignments, while engagement and emotional states were assessed using validated Likert-scale questionnaires. Results showed that students in the interactive group significantly outperformed their peers in both post-tests (67.48% vs. 53.36%) and final exams (80.8% vs. 61.44%). Interactive learners also demonstrated greater behavioural (+67.01%) and emotional engagement (+75.32%), along with enhanced emotional well-being marked by increased positive emotions (+66.67%) and reduced frustration. A significant drop in cognitive involvement (-39.8%) indicates possible cognitive overload. The pedagogical potential of interactive learning is reaffirmed by this result while reinforcing the need for balancing stimulation and cognitive level. Future research with larger, diverse samples is suggested for generalising and maximising outcomes.

</details>


### [18] [Radical Gender Neutrality: Agender Euphoria in Gaming and Play Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15337)
*Katie Seaborn,Shano Liang,Rua M. Williams,Phoebe O. Toups Dugas*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探索了"无性别欣快感"这一新概念，即人们在无性别体现、空间和活动中获得的快乐、喜悦和满足感，并研究了视频游戏如何创造或抑制这种体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视频游戏可以为性别少数群体提供性别欣快体验的安全空间，但无性别欣快体验的可能性尚未被探索。研究者希望填补这一空白，关注被忽视的无性别自我实现框架。

Method: 采用关键事件技术（CIT），对142名认同拥有或渴望无性别欣快体验的人进行研究，分析游戏和游戏体验如何创造（或抑制）无性别欣快感。

Result: 研究揭示了无性别欣快体验的现象学特征，并提供了基于实证的游戏设计标准，旨在为所有人（特别是无性别及无性别相关身份玩家）激发无性别欣快体验。

Conclusion: 这项工作为游戏研究和人机交互领域关于边缘化体验的批判性文献做出了贡献，强调了无性别欣快体验在设计中的重要性，并为包容性游戏设计提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Agender euphoria is a new term representing the powerful feelings of happiness, joy, and contentment derived from experiences in gender-free embodiments, spaces, and activities. People with and without agender and adjacent identities (e.g., genderless, gender-free, non-binary, gender-apathetic) may have such experiences under the right circumstances. Video games can offer gender minorities a safe haven for gender euphoric experiences. However, the possibility of agender euphoric experiences was unexplored. We considered this overlooked frame of self-actualization with 142 people who identified as having or desiring agender euphoric experiences. Using the critical incident technique (CIT), we uncovered how games and play experiences create (and inhibit) agender euphoria. We surface this experiential phenomenon and provide empirically-grounded criteria for the design of games to elicit agender euphoric experiences for everyone, but especially agender and agender adjacent players. This work adds to the growing critical literatures on marginalized experiences in games research and human-computer interaction.

</details>


### [19] [Access Over Deception: Fighting Deceptive Patterns through Accessibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15338)
*Tobias Pellkvist,Katie Seaborn,Miu Kojima*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了Web内容可访问性指南(WCAG)和相关立法能否作为对抗欺骗性设计模式(黑暗模式)的工具，通过启发式评估发现三种模式(倒计时计时器、自动播放、隐藏信息)与WCAG指南相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺骗性设计模式(黑暗模式)被广泛用于操纵用户做出违背自身利益的行为，尤其影响教育程度较低、视力障碍和老年人群体。虽然可访问性是用户体验、开发标准和法律的关键特征，但需要研究现有可访问性指南和立法能否作为对抗这些欺骗性模式的有效工具。

Method: 采用启发式评估方法，分析欺骗性设计模式是否违反或符合Web内容可访问性指南(WCAG)和相关立法(如欧洲可访问性法案EAA)。通过系统评估确定哪些欺骗性模式与可访问性指南存在冲突。

Result: 统计分析显示不同模式类型之间没有显著差异，但识别出三种与WCAG指南相关的欺骗性模式：倒计时计时器、自动播放和隐藏信息。这些模式在可访问性方面存在问题，可能违反相关指南。

Conclusion: WCAG和相关立法可以作为对抗UI欺骗性设计的工具之一，支持包容性设计。该方法为打击基于用户界面的欺骗行为提供了新的途径，强调了可访问性指南在保护用户免受操纵性设计影响方面的潜在作用。

Abstract: Deceptive patterns, dark patterns, and manipulative user interfaces (UI) are a widely used design strategy that manipulates users to act against their own interests in pursuit of shareholder aims. These patterns may particularly affect people with less education, visual impairments, and older adults. Yet, access is a critical feature of the user experience (UX), development standards, and law. We considered whether and how the Web Content Accessibility Guidelines (WCAG) and related legislation, like the European Accessibility Act (EAA), could act as a tool against deceptive patterns. We used heuristic evaluation to analyze whether and how deceptive patterns violate or conform to these guidelines and legal statutes. Although statistical analysis revealed no significant differences by pattern type, we identified three patterns implicated by the WCAG guidelines: Countdown Timer, Auto-Play, and Hidden Information. We offer this approach as one tool in the fight against UI-based deception and in support of inclusive design.

</details>


### [20] [Uncertainty, Vagueness, and Ambiguity in Human-Robot Interaction: Why Conceptualization Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15339)
*Xiaowen Sun,Cornelius Weber,Matthias Kerzel,Josua Spisak,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文为HRI中的不确定性、模糊性和歧义性提出了统一的概念基础，澄清了这些常被混淆的概念的定义、区别和相互关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在HRI研究中，不确定性、模糊性和歧义性这些概念经常被混淆，存在矛盾的定义和不一致的术语使用。这种概念混乱和术语不一致削弱了实证研究的可比性，阻碍了理论积累。因此需要建立清晰一致的概念框架来阐明这些挑战。

Method: 首先查阅字典分析三个术语的含义，然后在HRI背景下分析它们的区别和相互关系，通过实例说明这些特征，最后展示这一概念基础如何促进新方法的设计和现有方法的评估。

Result: 提出了一个一致的概念基础，澄清了不确定性、模糊性和歧义性在HRI中的定义、区别和相互关系，为相关方法的设计和评估提供了理论框架。

Conclusion: 建立清晰一致的概念基础对于HRI研究至关重要，能够促进理论积累、方法创新和实证研究的可比性，从而推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Uncertainty, vagueness, and ambiguity are closely related and often confused concepts in human-robot interaction (HRI). In earlier studies, these concepts have been defined in contradictory ways and described using inconsistent terminology. This conceptual confusion and lack of terminological consistency undermine empirical comparability, thereby slowing the accumulation of theory. Consequently, consistent concepts that clarify these challenges, including their definitions, distinctions, and interrelationships, are needed in HRI. To address this lack of clarity, this paper proposes a consistent conceptual foundation for the challenges of uncertainty, vagueness, and ambiguity in HRI. First, we examine the meanings of these three terms in dictionaries. We then analyze the nature of their distinctions and interrelationships within the context of HRI. We further illustrate these characteristics through examples. Finally, we demonstrate how this consistent conceptual foundation facilitates the design of novel methods and the evaluation of existing methodologies for these phenomena.

</details>


### [21] [Restoration, Exploration and Transformation: How Youth Engage Character.AI Chatbots for Feels, Fun and Finding themselves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15340)
*Annabel Blake,Marcus Carter,Eduardo Velloso*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 青少年在Character.AI Discord社区中高度参与AI互动，主要用于情感调节、创意探索和身份发展，创造了七种角色原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注成人设计的AI工具和实验，忽视了青少年自发的、游戏化的AI使用方式。本研究旨在探索青少年在Character.AI平台上的实际使用模式，揭示他们如何创造性地利用AI满足自身需求。

Method: 分析了Character.AI官方Discord社区中4,172名用户的讨论内容，识别出高度参与的用户群体特征（主要为13-17岁青少年，女性或非二元性别占61.9%），并基于话语分析构建理论框架。

Result: 发现高度参与的青少年用户主要通过AI进行三种意图的活动：恢复（情感调节）、探索（创意实验）和转变（身份发展）。同时识别出七种青少年创建的角色原型，揭示了青少年如何为AI发明新颖角色，以及当前AI体验与青少年使用需求之间的关键错位。

Conclusion: 青少年在AI使用中展现出独特的创造性实践，挑战了现有AI设计的成人中心视角。研究结果为研究人员和从业者提供了设计以青少年为中心的AI未来的框架，强调了需要更好地理解和支持青少年的情感、创意和身份发展需求。

Abstract: Young people are among the fastest adopters of generative AI, yet research emphasises adult-designed tools and experiments rather than playful, self-directed youth use. We analysed discourse from 4,172 users in Character.AI's official Discord, finding that the most engaged users were predominantly adolescents (50% aged 13-17), female or non-binary (61.9%), with most (59%) creating their own characters. We contribute (1) a descriptive account of how highly-engaged youth on Character.AI's Discord use AI for playful, emotional, and creative practices that push the platform limits; (2) a framework of three engagement intents -- Restoration (emotional regulation), Exploration (creative experimentation), and Transformation (identity development); and (3) a taxonomy of seven youth-created character archetypes. Together, these findings reveal how youth invent novel roles for AI, expose critical misalignments between youth use and current AI experiences, and provide frameworks for researchers and practitioners to design youth-centred AI futures.

</details>


### [22] [MRGEN: A Conceptual Framework for LLM-Powered Mixed Reality Authoring Tools for Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15341)
*Mohammed Oussama Seddini,Mohamed Ez-Zaouia,Ngoc Luyen Le,Iza Marfisi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MRGEN框架利用LLM帮助教师创建移动设备MR学习活动，无需技术专长，原型测试显示任务时间减少36%，90%以上参与者认为AI支持有帮助


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合现实为教育提供沉浸式多模态机会，但教师缺乏技术专长难以创作MR学习活动，需要降低创作门槛

Method: 提出MRGEN概念框架，基于三个轴：学习目标、MR模态和GAI辅助；在开源MIXAP创作平台实现原型，对24名参与者进行用户研究

Result: LLM驱动的创作使任务持续时间平均减少36%，超过90%参与者认为AI支持对头脑风暴、结构化和内容与学习目标对齐有帮助

Conclusion: MRGEN框架验证了AI辅助MR创作工具的可行性，为未来AI增强的MR教育创作工具提供了有前景的方向

Abstract: Mixed Reality (MR) offers immersive and multimodal opportunities for education but remains difficult for teachers to author without technical expertise. We propose MRGEN, a conceptual framework for LLM-powered authoring tools to support teachers in creating MR learning activities that work on mobile devices (tablets and smartphones). MRGEN articulates three axes: Learning Objectives, MR Modality, and GAI Assistance. To validate our framework, we implemented a prototype based on the open-source MIXAP authoring platform and conducted a user study with 24 participants. Results show that LLM-powered authoring reduced task duration by 36% on average, and that over 90% of participants found the AI support helpful for brainstorming, structuring, and aligning content with their learning goals. These findings yielded very promising results for future AI-assisted MR authoring tools.

</details>


### [23] [SuperProvenanceWidgets: Tracking and Visualizing Analytic Provenance Across UI Control Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15342)
*Antariksh Verma,Kaustubh Odak,Arpit Narechania*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SuperProvenanceWidgets扩展了ProvenanceWidgets库，新增了SuperWidget来跨多个UI控件追踪和可视化用户交互的溯源信息，支持工作流审计、探索偏差缓解和界面设计优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ProvenanceWidgets库只能追踪单个UI控件的交互溯源，缺乏对跨控件交互关系的理解。需要扩展功能来追踪多个控件之间的交互关系，以支持更复杂的分析工作流审计、探索偏差识别和界面设计优化。

Method: 开发SuperProvenanceWidgets作为ProvenanceWidgets的扩展，引入新的SuperWidget组件。该组件能够跨多个UI控件追踪用户交互的溯源信息（包括时间、频率和使用情况），并通过可视化方式呈现这些信息。通过三个使用场景展示功能，并使用认知维度符号框架进行技术自评估。

Result: SuperProvenanceWidgets成功实现了跨控件溯源追踪功能，能够帮助用户理解不同UI控件如何、何时以及是否被使用。通过三个场景展示了其在工作流审计、探索偏差缓解和界面设计方面的应用价值。技术评估表明该库对开发者具有良好可用性。

Conclusion: SuperProvenanceWidgets扩展了交互溯源能力，从单个控件扩展到跨控件层面，为开发者提供了构建高级溯源应用的工具。该开源库支持工作流审计、偏差识别和界面优化等多种应用场景，具有实用价值。

Abstract: ProvenanceWidgets is an existing JavaScript library that tracks the recency and frequency of user interactions with individual UI controls (e.g., range sliders and dropdowns) and dynamically overlays this provenance onto them. In this work, we introduce SuperProvenanceWidgets, an extension to ProvenanceWidgets featuring a new SuperWidget that similarly tracks and visualizes provenance but across multiple UI controls, enabling users to understand how, when, and whether different UI controls were used. Through three example usage scenarios, we demonstrate how this cross-control SuperWidget helps (a) audit and share analysis workflows, (b) surface and mitigate exploration biases, and (c) facilitate user interface design and personalization. We also perform a technical self-assessment using the Cognitive Dimensions of Notations to evaluate the library's usability for developers. SuperProvenanceWidgets is integrated into the ProvenanceWidgets library and is available as open-source software at ProvenanceWidgets.github.io, empowering developers to build advanced provenance applications.

</details>


### [24] [Improving Recycling Accuracy across UK Local Authorities: A Prototype for Citizen Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15345)
*Chloé Greenstreet,Anastasia Vayona,Jane Henriksen-Bulmer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究开发交互式应用原型，提供位置特定指导，使家庭回收准确率提高60%，但需配合地方当局系统改进


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管公众有回收动机，但英国分散的地方当局废物管理系统导致公民困惑和"愿望回收"（将不可回收物品放入回收箱）。新的"更简单回收政策"增加了识别、分类和清洁要求，需要公民指导以理解对回收实践的影响。

Method: 混合方法研究（50份调查、专家访谈、设计活动），使用价值主张画布识别公民痛点：标识困惑、物流限制、地方要求信息缺口。然后开发交互式应用原型，提供位置特定指导、视觉分类辅助和材料特定信息。

Result: 焦点小组评估显示原型将回收准确率提高了60%，包装评估有明显改善。以用户为中心设计的技术解决方案可显著改善回收行为并减少污染。

Conclusion: 基于用户中心设计的技术解决方案可有效改善回收行为，但最有效的方式是补充（而非替代）地方当局沟通和服务设计的系统性改进。

Abstract: Despite public motivation to recycle, significant barriers hinder effective household recycling in the UK. Decentralised local authority waste management creates citizen confusion and "wishcycling" (disposing of non-recyclable items in recycling bins). The recent Simpler Recycling Policy further complicates this landscape by mandating new identification, sorting, and cleaning requirements that will require citizen guidance to ensure they understand how these will impact their recycling practices.
  This mixed methods study (surveys n=50, expert interviews, design activities) used the Value Proposition Canvas to identify citizen pain points: confusion about logos, logistical constraints, and information gaps about local requirements. We then developed an interactive prototype application providing location-specific guidance, visual sorting aids, and material-specific information to address these painpoints.
  Focus group evaluation showed the prototype improved recycling accuracy by 60 percent, with marked improvements in packaging assessment. Technology-enabled solutions grounded in user-centred design can measurably improve recycling behaviours and reduce contamination. However, such solutions are most effective when complementing (rather than substituting for) systemic improvements in local authority communication and service design.

</details>


### [25] [GazeSync: A Mobile Eye-Tracking Tool for Analyzing Visual Attention on Dynamically Manipulated Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15348)
*Yaxiong Lei,Rishab Talwar,Shijing He,Xinya Gong,Yuheng Wang,Xudong Cai,Zhongliang Guo,Juan Ye*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GazeSync是一个移动眼动追踪系统，通过同步设备上的注视估计与实时图像变换矩阵，解决了动态内容中静态坐标失效的问题，能够准确重建图像相关的注意力模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统移动眼动追踪将注视映射到静态屏幕坐标，当用户进行捏合、缩放和旋转图像等操作时，静态坐标相对于底层视觉内容失去了语义意义，无法捕捉用户在动态内容上的注意力。

Method: 提出了GazeSync系统，通过同步设备上的注视估计与实时图像变换矩阵（缩放、旋转和平移），记录注视坐标与精确UI状态，从而能够准确重建图像相关的注意力模式，将视觉注意力与设备交互解耦。

Result: 通过包含引导操作、阅读和视觉搜索任务的实证研究验证，结果表明GazeSync能够恢复变换内容上的真实注视位置，明显优于静态基线方法，同时也揭示了校准漂移和复合操作下重建脆弱性的关键边界。

Conclusion: GazeSync提供了一个可重用的移动系统，解决了动态内容中眼动追踪的方法学缺陷，能够准确捕捉用户在变换内容上的视觉注意力模式，为移动交互研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Conventional mobile eye-tracking maps gaze to static screen coordinates, failing to capture user attention when content is dynamic. As users pinch, zoom, and rotate images, static coordinates lose their semantic meaning relative to the underlying visual content. To address this methodological gap, we present \textit{GazeSync}, a reusable mobile system that synchronizes on-device gaze estimation with real-time image transformation matrices (scale, rotation, and translation). By logging gaze coordinates alongside precise UI states, GazeSync enables the accurate reconstruction of \textit{image-relative} attention patterns, decoupling visual attention from device interaction. We validate our end-to-end toolchain through a formative study involving guided manipulation, reading, and visual search tasks. Our results demonstrate GazeSync's ability to recover ground-truth gaze locations on transforming content, explicitly showing how it outperforms static baselines, while also surfacing critical boundaries regarding calibration drift and reconstruction fragility under compound manipulations.

</details>


### [26] [The Crutch or the Ceiling? How Different Generations of LLMs Shape EFL Student Writings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15460)
*Hengky Susanto,David James Woo,Chingyi Yeung,Stephanie Wing Yan Lo-Philip,Chi Ho Yeung*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型在辅助EFL学生写作中的双重作用：既能提升低水平学生的评估分数和词汇多样性，但也可能掩盖真实能力，且过度依赖会降低写作的深层连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs的快速发展，它们已成为提升学生写作能力的有力工具。然而，现有研究多关注输出质量，本研究旨在探究LLMs在辅助EFL学生写作中的实际作用范围与局限性，特别是更智能的模型究竟是真正的脚手架还是单纯的补偿性拐杖。

Method: 分析ChatGPT发布前后LLMs辅助下的学生作文，采用专家定性评分和多种定量指标相结合的方法，包括可读性测试、皮尔逊相关系数、MTLD（词汇多样性测量）等。

Result: 高级LLMs能提升低水平学习者的评估分数和词汇多样性，但可能掩盖其真实能力。关键发现是：LLM辅助程度的增加与人类专家评分呈负相关，表明表面流畅性可能缺乏深层连贯性。

Conclusion: 要将AI辅助实践转化为真正的学习，教学法必须从关注输出质量转向验证学习过程。教育者应将AI功能（特别是区分概念脚手架与文本生成）与学习者的最近发展区对齐。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Large Language Models (LLMs) has made them powerful tools for enhancing student writing. This study explores the extent and limitations of LLMs in assisting secondary-level English as a Foreign Language (EFL) students with their writing tasks. While existing studies focus on output quality, our research examines the developmental shift in LLMs and their impact on EFL students, assessing whether smarter models act as true scaffolds or mere compensatory crutches. To achieve this, we analyse student compositions assisted by LLMs before and after ChatGPT's release, using both expert qualitative scoring and quantitative metrics (readability tests, Pearson's correlation coefficient, MTLD, and others). Our results indicate that advanced LLMs boost assessment scores and lexical diversity for lower-proficiency learners, potentially masking their true ability. Crucially, increased LLM assistance correlated negatively with human expert ratings, suggesting surface fluency without deep coherence. To transform AI-assisted practice into genuine learning, pedagogy must shift from focusing on output quality to verifying the learning process. Educators should align AI functions, specifically differentiating ideational scaffolding from textual production, within the learner's Zone of Proximal Development.

</details>


### [27] [Designing More Engaging Serious Games to Support Students' Mental Health: A Pilot Study Based on A CBT-Informed Design Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15662)
*Ting-Chen Hsu,Zheyuan Zhang,Ziyi Chen,Yuwen Liu,Yanjia Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种新颖的以体验为中心的游戏设计框架TPR-MMF，并基于此开发了横版卷轴严肃游戏"World + You - World"。通过小样本随机对照试验证明，该框架设计的游戏在内在动机方面显著优于传统显性教育严肃游戏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前数字心理健康教育和严肃游戏存在的枯燥、依从性低、缺乏吸引力等问题，特别是针对学生和青少年群体，需要一种更有效的游戏设计方法来提升参与度和教育效果。

Method: 提出了治疗性程序修辞与机制映射框架（TPR-MMF），并基于该框架开发了横版卷轴严肃游戏原型"World + You - World"。通过小样本随机对照试验（N=28）比较了TPR-MMF游戏与传统显性教育严肃游戏的效果，使用内在动机量表（IMI）进行评估，并结合定性调查分析。

Result: IMI结果显示，实验组（玩"World + You - World"）在四个方面显著优于对照组。定性调查表明，玩家能够感知游戏机制中的心理隐喻，并自发地与现实生活经验产生共鸣。

Conclusion: TPR-MMF框架为面向学生和青少年的游戏化心理健康教育提供了一种高度吸引人的新型开发范式，能够有效提升参与度和教育效果。

Abstract: Addressing the issues of dullness, low compliance, and lack of appeal in current digital mental health education and serious games for students and adolescents, this study proposes a novel, experience-centered framework for serious game design: the Therapeutic Procedural Rhetoric and Mechanism Mapping Framework (TPR-MMF). Based on this framework, a side-scrolling serious game prototype, "World + You - World," was developed. This study compared the effectiveness of TPR-MMF-based games with traditional explicit educational serious games through a small-sample randomized controlled trial (N=28). The results of the Intrinsic Motivation Inventory (IMI) showed that the experimental group (playing "World + You - World") significantly outperformed the control group in four aspects. Furthermore, qualitative survey results indicated that players could perceive the psychological metaphors within the game mechanics and spontaneously resonated with real-life experiences. This study provides a highly engaging new development paradigm for gamified mental health education for students and adolescents.

</details>


### [28] [ReVis: Towards Reusable Image-Based Visualizations with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15781)
*Xiaolin Wen,Changlin Li,Manusha Karunathilaka,Can Liu,Fangzhuo Jin,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ReVis：一种人机协作方法，通过领域特定语言和MLLM管道实现基于图像的可视化灵活重用，支持可视化分解、复制和定制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线共享的许多可视化仅以位图图像形式存在，难以重新设计或适应新数据。现有方法依赖结构化SVG或规范，支持的可视化类型有限，定制灵活性不足。

Method: 1) 提出通用领域特定语言(DSL)建模复杂可视化，支持分解和复制；2) 采用MLLM管道将图像可视化解析为DSL，识别视觉结构和数据编码映射；3) 提供交互界面供用户上传图像、检查结果、更新数据和定制编码。

Result: 1) 40个可视化图库展示DSL的表达能力；2) 定量研究评估ReVis在这些示例上的复制质量；3) 两个使用场景和16位可视化从业者的用户访谈验证了ReVis的有效性。

Conclusion: ReVis通过人机协作方法成功解决了图像可视化重用难题，其DSL和MLLM管道能够有效解析和复制复杂可视化，为可视化从业者提供了灵活的重用工具。

Abstract: Many expressive visualizations are shared online only as bitmap images, making them difficult to redesign or adapt to new data. Reusing such image-based visualizations requires substantial expertise and is often time-consuming, even for experienced visualization practitioners. Existing work on reproducing visualizations often relies on structured SVG or specifications, supports limited visualization types, and offers limited flexibility for customization. To address these challenges, we present ReVis, a human-AI collaboration approach that enables flexible reuse of image-based visualizations. First, a generic Domain-Specific language (DSL) is proposed to model complex visualizations and support both visualization decomposition and reproduction. Then, ReVis employs an MLLM-based pipeline to parse an image-based visualization into the DSL, delineating its core visual structures and data-to-encoding mappings, and further reproduces the visualization from the DSL. Finally, ReVis includes an interactive interface to allow users to upload visualization images, inspect reproduced results, update the underlying data, and customize visual encodings. A gallery of 40 visualizations demonstrates the expressiveness of the DSL, and a quantitative study evaluates the reproduction quality of ReVis on these examples. Two usage scenarios and user interviews with 16 visualization practitioners demonstrate the effectiveness of ReVis.

</details>


### [29] [Filter Babel: The Challenge of Synthetic Media to Authenticity and Common Ground in AI-Mediated Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15786)
*Advait Sarkar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文探讨了"过滤巴别塔"思想实验：AI为每个人生成个性化内容，可能导致私人语言出现，挑战共同基础和沟通完整性，但也可能促进身份认同发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI为每个人生成个性化内容（过滤巴别塔）可能带来的挑战和机遇，特别是对人际沟通和身份认同的影响，为AI中介沟通研究提供方向。

Method: 采用思想实验方法，分析"过滤巴别塔"场景下AI中介沟通的理论影响，结合语言哲学（维特根斯坦私人语言）和身份认同理论进行概念分析。

Result: 识别出AI个性化内容可能导致私人语言出现，挑战沟通的共同基础，但私人体验也是身份认同发展的必要引擎，存在沟通与身份认同的张力。

Conclusion: 需要开展AI中介沟通研究，平衡个性化体验与共享现实，确保AI技术既能支持身份发展又能维护沟通完整性，避免完全私人化的沟通困境。

Abstract: Filter Babel is a thought experiment about a near future in which everything we read, watch, and even whom we "meet" is privately generated for each of us. If we each recede into a world of purely private experience, we may each develop a Wittgensteinian private language that remains intelligible to others only because an AI translator sits in the middle. This intermediation challenges the integrity of common ground and therefore of communication. On the other hand, private experience is an essential engine of identity and selfhood: as Lanier warns, one must be somebody before one can share oneself. This paper opens a discussion of the challenges and opportunities that Filter Babel might present to human communication and identity, and what constructive directions for research in AI-mediated communication might ensue.

</details>


### [30] [From Intention to Text: AI-Supported Goal Setting in Academic Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15800)
*Yueling Fan,Richard Lee Davis,Olga Viberg*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: WriteFlow是一个基于AI语音的写作助手，通过目标导向的交互支持反思性学术写作，帮助作者进行目标表达、监控和协商，提升元认知调节能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术写作需要迭代反思和动态目标调节，但作者常常难以清晰表达和管理变化的目标。现有AI写作工具过于强调效率，缺乏对元认知和作者主体性的支持。

Method: 设计WriteFlow系统，将AI交互构建为对话空间，支持持续的目标表达、监控和协商。通过17人形成性研究和12人Wizard-of-Oz研究进行评估。

Result: WriteFlow能够支持迭代目标细化、保持目标与文本对齐、提示目标完成度评估，从而有效支撑元认知调节和行动中反思。

Conclusion: AI写作系统应优先考虑反思性对话、灵活的目标结构和多视角反馈，以支持有意图和主体性的写作过程。

Abstract: This study presents WriteFlow, an AI voice-based writing assistant designed to support reflective academic writing through goal-oriented interaction. Academic writing involves iterative reflection and evolving goal regulation, yet prior research and a formative study with 17 participants show that writers often struggle to articulate and manage changing goals. While commonly used AI writing tools emphasize efficiency, they offer limited support for metacognition and writer agency. WriteFlow frames AI interaction as a dialogic space for ongoing goal articulation, monitoring, and negotiation grounded in writers' intentions. Findings from a Wizard-of-Oz study with 12 expert users show that WriteFlow scaffolds metacognitive regulation and reflection-in-action by supporting iterative goal refinement, maintaining goal-text alignment during drafting, and prompting evaluation of goal fulfillment. We discuss design implications for AI writing systems that prioritize reflective dialogue, flexible goal structures, and multi-perspective feedback to support intentional and agentic writing.

</details>


### [31] [Shaping Plant-Like Shape-Changing Interfaces as Vertical Charts: Maximizing Readability, Aesthetics, and Naturalness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15902)
*Elodie Bouzekri,Guillaume Riviere*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了如何设计有效的植物状图表来传达环境数据，通过研究-设计方法比较了四种原型，发现物理植物状图表在自然感知和公共展示方面具有优势，特别是当使用可持续材料且折叠形状能编码0到最大值范围的数据时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过数据驱动策略鼓励环保生活方式的需求日益增长，需要能代表环境目的的数据可视化。虽然已有自然启发的计数器、仪表和位图，但数据序列可视化仍有待探索。因此，研究能否设计有效的植物状图表来传达环境数据。

Method: 采用研究-设计方法，探索设计空间以最大化可读性和美学。通过比较四种原型图表（涉及模态和材料维度），在可再生能源预测场景中询问用户反馈。特别关注物理图表与图形图表的对比，以及使用有意义材料（唤起可持续性和增强自然感）的优势。

Result: 物理植物状图表值得实施，因为它们展现出有前景的性能和最佳的自然感知，特别是当材料允许感知低技术方面时。物理图表在公共场所无需解释即可安装，前提是折叠形状能编码从0到最大值的比率。结果还重新验证了先前关于图形信息图的发现：物理图表在清晰度和可读性上略低，但在装饰美学上更高。同时研究了通过折叠形状编码比率的学习能力。

Conclusion: 物理植物状图表在传达环境数据方面具有价值，特别是在使用可持续材料且设计能直观编码数据范围的情况下。它们结合了良好的性能、自然感知和公共可访问性，为环境数据可视化提供了有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Conveying environmental data has grown interest in encouraging the adoption of eco-friendly lifestyles through data-driven strategies. This scope appeals to data visualizations representing the environmental purpose. For example, previous work has already proposed nature-inspired counters, gauges, and bitmaps, but data series remains to be explored. Therefore, could we design and implement effective plant-like charts? This paper brings answers through a research-through-design approach that explores a design space to maximize readability and aesthetics. It then compares four prototypes of charts over modality and material dimensions by asking users about scenarios involving renewable energy forecasts. The results examine whether implementing physical charts is worth it instead of graphical charts and the advantages of using meaningful materials that evocate sustainability and enhance naturalness. The results also reexamine, with physical charts, the previous results on graphical infographics of slightly lower clarity and readability but higher aesthetics of embellishment. In addition, learnability is examined for encoding rates through folded shapes. This paper shows that physical plant-like charts are worthwhile because of promising performance and best-of-breed naturalness when materials allow low-tech aspects' perception and because being installable in public places without explanations if folded shapes encode rates ranging from 0 to a maximum value.

</details>


### [32] [Stochastic wage suppression on gig platforms and how to organize against it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15962)
*Ana-Andreea Stoica,Celestine Mendler-Duenner,Moritz Hardt*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个数字劳动力平台的定价模型，展示了平台如何利用工人对劳动力成本的不确定性来压低工资，并探讨了集体行动作为提高工资和促进福利的杠杆作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究数字劳动力平台中平台通过利用低成本劳动力的丰富性来压低工资的剥削模式，并探索集体行动作为提高工人工资和促进市场福利的潜在机制。

Method: 引入了一个具有覆盖目标的挂牌价格采购模型，平台通过向顺序到达的工人发布价格来完成任务，工人接受任务当价格超过其私人成本。分析了平台的定价策略和集体行动的影响。

Result: 平台存在简单的定价策略，可以在O(M)等待时间内完成所有M个任务，同时仅支付劳动力总成本的O(log(M)/M)比例。而一小部分有针对性的低成本工人组成的联盟通过设定价格底线，能将平台总支出从对数增长强制变为线性增长。

Conclusion: 数字劳动力平台可以利用工人对劳动力成本的不确定性来有效压低工资，但有针对性的集体行动能够显著提高工人工资和促进市场福利，而随机抽样的联盟则效果有限。

Abstract: Digital labor platforms are increasingly used to procure human input, ranging from annotating data and red-teaming AI models, to ride-sharing and food delivery. A central concern in such markets is the ability of platforms to suppress wages by exploiting the abundance of low-cost labor. To study this exploitation pattern, we introduce a novel posted-price procurement model with coverage objectives. A platform seeks to complete M tasks by posting prices to sequentially arriving workers, each of whom accepts a task if it exceeds their private cost. First, we show that under natural assumptions on the workers' estimated cost, there exists a simple pricing strategy for the platform to cover all M tasks with wait time O(M), while paying only a O(log(M)/M) fraction of the total cost of labor. This result highlights how platforms can exploit workers' uncertainty about the cost of labor to effectively suppress wages. Then, we study collective action as a lever to increase wages and promote welfare in digital labor markets. In particular, we show how a small coalition of targeted low-cost workers who commit to a price floor forces the platform's total spending from logarithmic to linear in M. In contrast, a randomly sampled coalition of equal size remains largely ineffective. We complement our theory with synthetic experiments, showcasing the benefits of collective action across different market regimes.

</details>


### [33] ["Taking Stock at FAccT": Using Participatory Design to Co-Create a Vision for the Fairness, Accountability and Transparency Community](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16224)
*Shiran Dudy,Jan Simson,Yanan Long*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文描述了在ACM FAccT会议上实施的大规模参与式设计过程，用于反思性会议治理，结合了现场CRAFT会议、异步Polis投票和治理报告合成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACM FAccT作为新兴论坛，已成为活动家和学者批判性审视新兴AI和ML技术的关键空间。需要建立反思性会议治理机制，让多元参与者共同塑造议程，为批判性学者提供表达关切的平台

Method: 采用大规模参与式设计过程，结合：1) 现场CRAFT会议；2) 异步Polis投票系统；3) 为FAccT领导层合成治理报告。参与者通过撰写种子声明、添加新声明以及投票展示同意、不同意和不确定的模式来塑造实质性议程

Result: 成功实施了首批将参与式设计应用于批判性AI社会影响研究场所的案例之一，创建了批判性学者自由表达关切的生态位。提供了可扩展的协同设计范式案例研究，在时间和认识论维度上都具有可扩展性

Conclusion: 这项工作推进了大规模参与式设计理论，为批判性AI研究场所的治理提供了有效的协同设计范式，展示了如何通过参与式方法促进多元利益相关者在技术社会影响讨论中的深度参与

Abstract: As a relatively new forum, ACM FAccT has become a key space for activists and scholars to critically examine emerging AI and ML technologies. It brings together academics, civil society members, and government representatives from diverse fields to explore the broader societal impacts of both deployed and proposed technologies. We report a large-scale participatory design (PD) process for reflexive conference governance, which combined an in-person CRAFT session, an asynchronous Polis poll and the synthesis of a governance-facing report for the FAccT leadership. Participants shaped the substantive agenda by authoring seed statements, adding new statements and making patterns of agreement, disagreement and uncertainty made visible through voting.Our endeavors represent one of the the first instances of applying PD to a venue that critically interrogates the societal impacts of AI, fostering a niche in which critical scholars are free to voice their concerns. Finally, this work advances large-scale PD theory by providing an effective case study of a co-design paradigm that can readily scale temporally and epistemologically.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [34] [Prompt-Driven Code Summarization: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15385)
*Afia Farjana,Zaiyu Cheng,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这篇系统文献综述分析了LLM在软件文档生成中的提示工程方法，总结了现有研究碎片化、评估指标不完善的问题，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件文档对于程序理解、开发者入职、代码审查和长期维护至关重要，但手动编写质量文档耗时且结果往往不完整或不一致。LLM为自动生成代码描述提供了有前景的解决方案，但其效果严重依赖于提示设计，而当前研究对此缺乏系统理解。

Method: 采用系统文献综述方法，整合现有证据，对提示范式进行分类，考察其有效性，并识别研究空白。具体包括分析少样本提示、思维链推理、检索增强生成和零样本学习等不同提示策略。

Result: 研究发现当前研究碎片化，缺乏对哪些提示策略在何种条件下对哪些模型最有效的系统理解。评估实践差异很大，多数研究依赖基于重叠的指标，这些指标可能无法捕捉语义质量。

Conclusion: 需要更系统的研究来理解不同提示策略的有效性，开发更全面的评估指标，并指导LLM在软件工程文档任务中的实际应用。该综述为未来研究和实践采用提供了指导框架。

Abstract: Software documentation is essential for program comprehension, developer onboarding, code review, and long-term maintenance. Yet producing quality documentation manually is time-consuming and frequently yields incomplete or inconsistent results. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising solution by automatically generating natural language descriptions from source code, helping developers understand code more efficiently, facilitating maintenance, and supporting downstream activities such as defect localization and commit message generation. However, the effectiveness of LLMs in documentation tasks critically depends on how they are prompted. Properly structured instructions can substantially improve model performance, making prompt engineering-the design of input prompts to guide model behavior-a foundational technique in LLM-based software engineering. Approaches such as few-shot prompting, chain-of-thought reasoning, retrieval-augmented generation, and zero-shot learning show promise for code summarization, yet current research remains fragmented. There is limited understanding of which prompting strategies work best, for which models, and under what conditions. Moreover, evaluation practices vary widely, with most studies relying on overlap-based metrics that may not capture semantic quality. This systematic literature review consolidates existing evidence, categorizes prompting paradigms, examines their effectiveness, and identifies gaps to guide future research and practical adoption.

</details>


### [35] [Analyzing Chain of Thought (CoT) Approaches in Control Flow Code Deobfuscation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15390)
*Seyedreza Mohseni,Sarvesh Baskar,Edward Raff,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文探索使用思维链提示引导大语言模型进行代码反混淆，显著提升了控制流图重建和语义保持效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代码反混淆需要大量手动工作和复杂分析工具，耗时耗力，需要探索更高效的自动化方法

Method: 采用思维链提示策略，引导大语言模型进行逐步推理的代码分析，针对控制流平坦化、不透明谓词及其组合等混淆技术

Result: CoT提示显著提升反混淆质量，GPT5表现最佳，控制流图重建平均提升16%，语义保持平均提升20.5%，性能受混淆程度和原始控制流图复杂度影响

Conclusion: 思维链引导的大语言模型可作为有效的代码反混淆辅助工具，提高代码可解释性、控制流图重建准确性和程序行为保持，减少逆向工程人工工作量

Abstract: Code deobfuscation is the task of recovering a readable version of a program while preserving its original behavior. In practice, this often requires days or even months of manual work with complex and expensive analysis tools. In this paper, we explore an alternative approach based on Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting, where a large language model is guided through explicit, step-by-step reasoning tailored for code analysis. We focus on control flow obfuscation, including Control Flow Flattening (CFF), Opaque Predicates, and their combination, and we measure both structural recovery of the control flow graph and preservation of program semantics. We evaluate five state-of-the-art large language models and show that CoT prompting significantly improves deobfuscation quality compared with simple prompting. We validate our approach on a diverse set of standard C benchmarks and report results using both structural metrics for control flow graphs and semantic metrics based on output similarity. Among the tested models and by applying CoT, GPT5 achieves the strongest overall performance, with an average gain of about 16% in control-flow graph reconstruction and about 20.5% in semantic preservation across our benchmarks compared to zero-shot prompting. Our results also show that model performance depends not only on the obfuscation level and the chosen obfuscator but also on the intrinsic complexity of the original control flow graph. Collectively, these findings suggest that CoT-guided large language models can serve as effective assistants for code deobfuscation, providing improved code explainability, more faithful control flow graph reconstruction, and better preservation of program behavior while potentially reducing the manual effort needed for reverse engineering.

</details>


### [36] [The Semi-Executable Stack: Agentic Software Engineering and the Expanding Scope of SE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15468)
*Robert Feldt,Per Lenberg,Julian Frattini,Dhasarathy Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"半可执行栈"模型，认为AI驱动的软件工程转型不是威胁，而是工程对象从可执行代码扩展到包含自然语言、工具、工作流等的半可执行制品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对AI（特别是LLM和智能体）对软件工程构成威胁的普遍担忧，作者提出不同观点：真正的转变不是软件工程失去相关性，而是工程对象从纯可执行代码扩展到需要人类或概率解释的半可执行制品。

Method: 引入"半可执行栈"作为六环诊断参考模型，包含可执行制品、指导性制品、编排执行、控制机制、操作逻辑、社会与制度适配。通过三个案例研究展开论证，将常见反对意见重构为工程目标而非拒绝转型的理由。

Result: 提出了一个概念性框架，用于定位贡献、瓶颈或组织转型在栈中的位置及其依赖关系。提供了"保留vs净化"启发式方法，帮助决定哪些传统软件工程流程、控制和协调例程应保留，哪些应简化或重新设计。

Conclusion: 软件工程正在经历根本性转变，工程对象扩展到半可执行制品。论文提供了诊断框架和议程设置，将转型挑战重构为工程机会，而非威胁。

Abstract: AI-based systems, currently driven largely by LLMs and tool-using agentic harnesses, are increasingly discussed as a possible threat to software engineering. Foundation models get stronger, agents can plan and act across multiple steps, and tasks such as scaffolding, routine test generation, straightforward bug fixing, and small integration work look more exposed than they did only a few years ago. The result is visible unease not only among students and junior developers, but also among experienced practitioners who worry that hard-won expertise may lose value. This paper argues for a different reading. The important shift is not that software engineering loses relevance. It is that the thing being engineered expands beyond executable code to semi-executable artifacts; combinations of natural language, tools, workflows, control mechanisms, and organizational routines whose enactment depends on human or probabilistic interpretation rather than deterministic execution. The Semi-Executable Stack is introduced as a six-ring diagnostic reference model for reasoning about that expansion, spanning executable artifacts, instructional artifacts, orchestrated execution, controls, operating logic, and societal and institutional fit. The model helps locate where a contribution, bottleneck, or organizational transition primarily sits, and which adjacent rings it depends on. The paper develops the argument through three worked cases, reframes familiar objections as engineering targets rather than reasons to dismiss the transition, and closes with a preserve-versus-purify heuristic for deciding which legacy software engineering processes, controls, and coordination routines should be kept and which should be simplified or redesigned. This paper is a conceptual keynote companion: diagnostic and agenda-setting rather than empirical.

</details>


### [37] [LLM4C2Rust: Large Language Models for Automated Memory-Safe Code Transpilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15485)
*Sarah Bedell,Nazanin Siavash,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于检索增强生成(RAG)的框架，结合LLM与SLM实现C/C++到Rust的转译，重点关注内存安全性提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内存安全是软件工程中的关键挑战，特别是用C/C++等内存不安全语言编写的遗留系统。Rust提供内置内存安全保证，但手动转译耗时且易错，基于规则的方法不够灵活高效

Method: 提出RAG辅助框架，集成LLM与SLM进行C/C++到Rust转译。采用分段策略处理代码块，通过检索Rust文档和编译器错误参考来指导LLM

Result: 使用三种OpenAI模型(GPT-4o、GPT-4-Turbo、o3-Mini)的实验表明，RAG增强管道通常能提高C到Rust代码转译的正确性和安全性。多个Coreutils程序在最终Rust输出中完全消除了原始指针解引用(RPDs)和不安全类型转换(UTCs)

Conclusion: LLM基于转译在推进自动化软件现代化、修复以及内存安全代码生成方面具有潜力，RAG增强方法能有效提升转译质量

Abstract: Memory safety has long been a critical challenge in software engineering, particularly for legacy systems written in memory-unsafe languages such as C and C++. Rust, one of the youngest modern programming languages, offers built-in memory-safety guarantees that make it a strong candidate for secure systems development. Consequently, transpiling C/C++ code into memory-safe Rust code has become a growing area of research. However, manual transpilation is often time-consuming and error-prone. Additionally, rule-based automated approaches are not as flexible or cost-effective as methods enabled by state-of-the-art AI models, techniques, and methods, such as those that deploy Large Language Models (LLMs), for example, Generative Pretrained Transformers (GPT). In this paper, we propose a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)-assisted framework that integrates an LLM with a Small Language Model (SLM) to perform C/C++-to-Rust transpilation with a focus on enhancing memory safety. The framework deploys a segmentation strategy that processes C/C++ code in balanced blocks, guiding the LLM with retrieved context from Rust documentation and compiler error references. Our experiments using three OpenAI models (GPT-4o, GPT-4-Turbo, and o3-Mini) demonstrate that the RAG-enhanced pipeline generally improves both code correctness and security for C-to-Rust code transpilation. Several Coreutils programs achieve complete elimination of Raw Pointer Dereferences (RPDs) and Unsafe Type Casts (UTCs) in the final Rust output, indicating the potential of LLM-based transpilation for advancing automated software modernization and repair, as well as memory-safe code generation.

</details>


### [38] [QMutBench: A Dataset of Quantum Circuit Mutants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15870)
*Eñaut Mendiluze Usandizaga,Thomas Laurent,Paolo Arcaini,Shaukat Ali*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QMutBench是一个包含超过70万个量子电路变异体的数据集，用于评估量子软件测试技术的有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件测试需要评估测试用例检测故障的有效性，这需要包含故障的基准程序。然而，目前缺乏包含故障的基准数据集

Method: 开发了QMutBench数据集，包含超过700,000个量子电路变异体，代表不同类型的故障。数据集通过在线接口提供，支持按原始量子电路、期望的变异体存活率、故障量子门类型等标准进行选择

Result: 创建了一个可访问的在线数据集，为量子软件开发者和测试者提供必要的变异体基准，用于评估测试用例质量或比较不同测试技术

Conclusion: QMutBench填补了量子软件测试领域缺乏故障基准的空白，支持测试技术评估和新颖的变异引导测试技术开发

Abstract: Quantum software testing has attracted interest in recent years, prompting the development of various techniques to automate the testing of quantum software. These techniques generate test cases that must be assessed for their effectiveness in detecting faults. Such an assessment requires benchmarks of faulty programs. However, there is a lack of benchmarks containing faults. In this data showcase, we propose QMutBench, a dataset that contains over 700,000 quantum circuit mutants representing different faults. The dataset is accessible via an online interface with selection criteria, such as the original quantum circuit(s) from which mutants are generated, the desired survival rate of the selected mutants, and other mutation characteristics (e.g., the type of faulty quantum gate). QMutBench provides quantum software developers and testers with an accessible online dataset to obtain benchmarks of mutants necessary to assess either the quality of the test cases generated by their testing technique or to compare different testing techniques. It also enables the development of new mutation-guided quantum software testing techniques.

</details>


### [39] [Empirical Investigation of Quantum Computing Toolchains and Algorithms : Mining Stack Overflow Repository](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15512)
*Maryam Tavassoli Sabzevari,Arif Ali Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析Stack Overflow上1404个量子计算相关帖子，揭示了开发者社区讨论的主要话题、工具偏好、算法引用情况以及问题难度差异，为量子软件工程提供实用见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算向实际应用和工业采用过渡，需要了解开发者如何与量子技术互动。研究旨在通过分析真实开发者讨论来理解量子计算社区的实际需求和挑战。

Method: 研究分析了1404个Stack Overflow量子计算相关帖子，采用主题建模和定量分析方法。识别主要讨论主题、流行度、工具、编程语言和量子算法引用情况。使用两个指标评估问题难度：(i)无接受答案的问题百分比，(ii)获得接受答案的中位时间。

Result: 识别出7个主要讨论主题，其中混合量子-经典计算和量子电路实现最为普遍。Qiskit和Q-sharp主导开发者讨论，Grover和Shor算法最常被引用。分析揭示了不同主题、工具和算法在参与度和难度上的差异，表明成熟度和社区支持水平不同。

Conclusion: 研究结果为研究人员、工具开发者和教育工作者提供了可操作的见解，支持量子软件工程在可用性、文档和学习资源方面的改进。开源数据集已公开，支持透明性和可重复性。

Abstract: Quantum computing (QC) is increasingly transitioning toward practical and industrial adoption, highlighting the need to understand how developers engage with quantum technologies. In this study, we analyze 1,404 Stack Overflow posts related to quantum computing topics, including quantum programming, tools, and algorithms, to investigate real-world developer discussions. Using topic modeling and quantitative analysis, we identify the main discussion topics, their popularity, and the tools, programming languages, and quantum algorithms referenced by practitioners. We further assess the difficulty of developer questions using two metrics: (i) the percentage of questions without accepted answers and (ii) the median time required to receive an accepted answer. Our findings reveal seven main topics, with hybrid quantum--classical computing and quantum circuit implementation emerging as the most prevalent. We observe that Qiskit and Q-sharp dominate developer discussions, while Grover's and Shor's algorithms are the most frequently referenced. Moreover, our analysis highlights differences in engagement and difficulty across topics, tools, and algorithms, indicating varying levels of maturity and community support. These findings provide actionable insights for researchers, tool developers, and educators, supporting improvements in usability, documentation, and learning resources in quantum software engineering. To support transparency and reproducibility, the open-source dataset used in this study is publicly available at Zenodo.

</details>


### [40] [Symbolic Guardrails for Domain-Specific Agents: Stronger Safety and Security Guarantees Without Sacrificing Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15579)
*Yining Hong,Yining She,Eunsuk Kang,Christopher S. Timperley,Christian Kästner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文研究了符号护栏作为为AI智能体提供强安全性和安全性保证的实用途径，通过系统分析现有基准测试、评估符号护栏的政策执行能力，并在多个基准上验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险业务场景中，AI智能体通过工具与环境交互可能导致隐私泄露和财务损失等不可接受的危害。现有缓解措施（如基于训练的方法和神经护栏）提高了智能体可靠性，但无法提供保证。需要寻找能够提供强安全性和安全性保证的实用方法。

Method: 三部分研究：1）系统回顾80个最先进的智能体安全和安全性基准，识别其评估的政策；2）分析哪些政策要求可以通过符号护栏保证；3）在τ²-Bench、CAR-bench和MedAgentBench上评估符号护栏对安全性、安全性和智能体成功率的影响。

Result: 发现85%的基准缺乏具体政策，依赖未明确说明的高层目标或常识。在已指定的政策中，74%的政策要求可以通过符号护栏强制执行，通常使用简单、低成本的机制。这些护栏在不牺牲智能体效用的前提下提高了安全性和安全性。

Conclusion: 符号护栏是保证某些安全性和安全性要求的实用有效方法，特别适用于领域特定的AI智能体。研究结果支持符号护栏作为实现AI智能体强安全保证的可行途径。

Abstract: AI agents that interact with their environments through tools enable powerful applications, but in high-stakes business settings, unintended actions can cause unacceptable harm, such as privacy breaches and financial loss. Existing mitigations, such as training-based methods and neural guardrails, improve agent reliability but cannot provide guarantees. We study symbolic guardrails as a practical path toward strong safety and security guarantees for AI agents. Our three-part study includes a systematic review of 80 state-of-the-art agent safety and security benchmarks to identify the policies they evaluate, an analysis of which policy requirements can be guaranteed by symbolic guardrails, and an evaluation of how symbolic guardrails affect safety, security, and agent success on $τ^2$-Bench, CAR-bench, and MedAgentBench. We find that 85\% of benchmarks lack concrete policies, relying instead on underspecified high-level goals or common sense.
