Among the specified policies, 74\% of policy requirements can be enforced by symbolic guardrails, often using simple, low-cost mechanisms. These guardrails improve safety and security without sacrificing agent utility. Overall, our results suggest that symbolic guardrails are a practical and effective way to guarantee some safety and security requirements, especially for domain-specific AI agents. We release all codes and artifacts at https://github.com/hyn0027/agent-symbolic-guardrails.

</details>


### [41] [CodeMMR: Bridging Natural Language, Code, and Image for Unified Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15663)
*Jiahui Geng,Qing Li,Fengyu Cai,Fakhri Karray*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出首个多模态代码检索基准MMCoIR和统一检索模型CodeMMR，通过指令对齐将自然语言、代码和图像嵌入共享语义空间，显著提升多模态代码检索性能并增强RAG的代码生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索模型主要基于文本，忽略了编程工件中的视觉和结构信息（如Web界面、数据可视化、SVG、示意图、UML等），限制了代码发现、重用和LLM编码的可靠性。

Method: 首先构建MMCoIR基准，涵盖5个视觉领域、8种编程语言和11个库；然后提出CodeMMR模型，通过指令驱动的多模态对齐将自然语言、代码和图像共同嵌入共享语义空间。

Result: CodeMMR在nDCG@10指标上平均超越竞争基线（如UniIR、GME、VLM2Vec）10个百分点，展现出跨模态和跨语言的强泛化能力；集成到RAG中能提升代码生成保真度和视觉基础能力。

Conclusion: 多模态检索是下一代智能编程系统的核心使能技术，CodeMMR通过统一的多模态表示学习有效解决了代码检索中的视觉信息缺失问题，显著提升了检索性能和代码生成质量。

Abstract: Code search, framed as information retrieval (IR), underpins modern software engineering and increasingly powers retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), improving code discovery, reuse, and the reliability of LLM-based coding. Yet existing code IR models remain largely text-centric and often overlook the visual and structural aspects inherent in programming artifacts such as web interfaces, data visualizations, SVGs, schematic diagrams, and UML. To bridge this gap, we introduce MMCoIR, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating multimodal code IR across five visual domains, eight programming languages, eleven libraries, and show the challenge of the task through extensive evaluation. Therefore, we then propose CodeMMR, a unified retrieval model that jointly embeds natural language, code, and images into a shared semantic space through instruction-based multimodal alignment. CodeMMR achieves strong generalization across modalities and languages, outperforming competitive baselines (e.g., UniIR, GME, VLM2Vec) by an average of 10 points on nDCG@10. Moreover, integrating CodeMMR into RAG enhances code generation fidelity and visual grounding on unseen code generation tasks, underscoring the potential of multimodal retrieval as a core enabler for next-generation intelligent programming systems. Datasets are available at HuggingFace.

</details>


### [42] [Feature Toggle Dynamics in Large-Scale Systems: Prevalence, Growth, Lifespan, and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15872)
*Xhevahire Tërnava*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对Kubernetes和GitLab中4000多个功能开关事件的纵向分析显示，功能开关移除滞后于添加，导致库存增长，且不同系统的开关寿命差异显著，部分开关成为事实上的永久开关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能开关虽然支持渐进式部署和实验，但常常超出预期生命周期而持续存在，积累为技术债务。先前研究关注功能开关交互和复杂性，但缺乏跨不同组织环境的纵向量化研究来追踪功能开关随时间的演变。

Method: 分析Kubernetes（1000万行代码，8.5年）和GitLab（500万行代码，5年）中超过4000个功能开关事件。研究功能开关的添加、移除模式及寿命分布，并基于发现提出包含五个关键指标的基准框架及其经验阈值。

Result: 1. 功能开关移除滞后于添加：Kubernetes滞后约35%，GitLab滞后约13%，导致开关库存持续增长。
2. 寿命差异显著：Kubernetes开关中位寿命734天，GitLab仅185天。
3. 部分开关成为永久开关：Kubernetes 1.33%和GitLab 0.73%的开关超过所有先前观察到的移除时长，成为事实上的永久开关。

Conclusion: 功能开关管理实践在不同系统中存在显著差异，开关移除滞后导致库存积累。提出的基准框架和五个关键指标的经验阈值使实践者能够评估和比较跨项目的开关管理实践，所有脚本和数据已公开。

Abstract: Feature toggles enable gradual rollouts and experimentation in software systems, yet often persist beyond their intended lifecycle, accumulating as technical debt. Prior research has examined feature toggle interactions and complexity, but no longitudinal study has quantified how toggles evolve over time across different organizational contexts. We analyse over 4,000 toggle events in Kubernetes (10 MLoC, 8.5 years) and GitLab (5 MLoC, 5 years). We find that feature toggle removals lags behind additions in both systems (by roughly 35% and 13%, respectively), leading to growing toggle inventories. Their lifespan patterns also differ notably, with Kubernetes toggles lasting a median of 734 days versus 185 in GitLab. Then, some feature toggles (1.33% and 0.73%, respectively) exceed all previously observed removal durations, becoming de facto permanent. Building on these findings, we propose a benchmarking framework with five key metrics and their empirically derived threshold zones, enabling practitioners to assess and compare toggle management practices across projects. All scripts and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [43] [Small Yet Configurable: Unveiling Null Variability in Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15957)
*Xhevahire Tërnava,Georges Aaron Randrianaina,Luc Lesoil,Mathieu Acher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对108个GNU coreutils程序进行实证研究，发现即使是小型软件系统也具有显著的可配置性（最多76个选项），运行时可配置性与代码库大小高度相关（0.78），且可配置性随时间增长，主要源于编译时选项增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然小型软件系统在日常任务中广泛使用，但其可配置性尚未得到充分研究。现代软件系统呈现可配置性增加的趋势，同时人们对构建不可变、专用和可复现软件的兴趣也在增长。本研究旨在填补小型软件系统可配置性实证研究的空白。

Method: 对GNU coreutils中的108个程序进行实证分析，测量每个程序的编译时和运行时可变性选项数量。分析运行时可配置性与代码库大小的相关性。对20个最小程序在85个版本中的演变进行纵向研究，追踪可配置性随时间的变化趋势。

Result: 小型程序可具有显著可配置性（最多76个选项）。运行时可配置性与代码库大小高度相关（相关系数0.78）。可配置性随时间增加，主要源于编译时选项增长。首次提出"零可变性软件系统"概念，即除必需功能外无可配置性。

Conclusion: 高可配置性并非大型系统专属特征。减少不必要的可变性（通过选项在运行时和编译时之间转移、移除不必要的运行时可变性或早期解决编译时可变性）可降低代码库复杂性和大小，从而创建更轻量、更小、更易维护的软件。

Abstract: Many small-scale software systems, that is, with limited codebase or binary size, are widely used in everyday tasks, yet their configurability remains largely unexplored. At the same time, studies on modern software systems show a trend toward increasing configurability, alongside growing interest in building immutable, specialized, and reproducible software. In this paper, we present the first empirical study on the extent of configurability in small-scale software systems. By analyzing 108 programs from GNU coreutils, we show that even small programs can exhibit significant compile-time and run-time variability, with up to 76 options per program. Then, there is a high correlation (0.78) between run-time variability and codebase size. Furthermore, an analysis of the 20 smallest programs across 85 releases reveals that variability tends to increase over time, primarily due to the added compile-time variability. This suggests that shifting options between run-time and compile-time, removing unnecessary run-time variability, or resolving compile-time variability early, can help reduce codebase complexity and size. We also introduce, for the first time, the concept of null-variable software system, one with no configurability beyond mandatory features. Our findings show that high configurability is not exclusive to large-scale systems and that reducing unnecessary variability can lead to lightweight, smaller, and more maintainable software. We hope this effort contributes to designing new software by understanding how to balance its configurability with codebase size.

</details>


### [44] [Supporting the Comprehension of Data Analysis Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15963)
*Florian Sihler,Oliver Gerstl,Lars Pfrenger,Julian Schubert,Matthias Tichy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: flowR是一个用于R语言数据分析IDE的扩展工具，通过数据流和控制流分析构建可视化图表，提供代码审查和值注释功能，支持实时反馈和插件扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分析脚本通常难以理解和维护，阻碍了研究的可重复性和重用性，同时缺乏处理此类脚本的工具支持。针对R语言环境，需要提供更好的工具来改善数据分析脚本的可理解性和可维护性。

Method: 开发flowR作为Positron和VS Code IDE的扩展，结合先前提出的静态后向程序切片器，通过交织过程间数据流和控制流分析构建全面的数据流图。系统增量分析R项目，支持交互式图形可视化、代码审查和内联值注释，并提供插件系统和接口以集成更多分析功能。

Result: flowR在真实项目中计算完整数据流图平均仅需576ms，实现近实时反馈。工具支持R的动态和探索性特性，提供全面的数据分析脚本概览和交互式可视化功能。

Conclusion: flowR通过提供数据流可视化、代码审查和值注释等工具支持，显著改善了R数据分析脚本的可理解性和可维护性，有助于提高研究的可重复性和代码重用性。其插件架构确保了系统的可扩展性。

Abstract: A lot of research relies on data analysis scripts to process, clean, and visualize data. However, recent studies show that these scripts are often hard to comprehend and maintain, hindering reproducibility and reuse, accompanied by a lack of tool support for handling such scripts. In this work, we focus on the R programming language, addressing this problem by presenting flowR as an extension for the common data analysis IDEs Positron and VS Code. Alongside a previously presented static backward program slicer, flowR provides an overview of data analysis scripts, interactive graph visualizations, linting, and inline value annotations to support data analysts. FlowR incrementally analyzes R projects by intertwining interprocedural data- and control-flow analyses to build a comprehensive dataflow graph, incorporating R's dynamic and explorative features. Additionally, flowR offers a plugin system and interfaces, allowing the integration of further analyses, such as new linting rules or custom visualizations. Requiring an average of 576ms to calculate the full dataflow graph of real-world projects, this enables near real-time feedback. The demonstration video is available at  https://youtu.be/hJzr-r-NmMg . For the full source code and extensive documentation, refer to https://github.com/flowr-analysis/flowr . To try the docker image, use `docker run --rm -it eagleoutice/flowr`.

</details>


### [45] [LLMSniffer: Detecting LLM-Generated Code via GraphCodeBERT and Supervised Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16058)
*Mahir Labib Dihan,Abir Muhtasim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMSniffer：基于两阶段监督对比学习的AI生成代码检测框架，通过微调GraphCodeBERT实现，在多个基准数据集上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在软件开发中的快速普及，区分AI生成代码与人类编写代码成为关键挑战，涉及学术诚信、代码质量保证和软件安全等多个重要领域。

Method: 提出LLMSniffer检测框架，采用两阶段监督对比学习流程微调GraphCodeBERT，包含注释移除预处理和多层感知机分类器，通过对比学习优化代码表示。

Result: 在两个基准数据集GPTSniffer和Whodunit上取得显著改进：GPTSniffer准确率从70%提升至78%（F1从68%到78%），Whodunit准确率从91%提升至94.65%（F1从91%到94.64%）。t-SNE可视化证实对比学习微调产生分离良好、紧凑的嵌入表示。

Conclusion: LLMSniffer框架有效提升了AI生成代码的检测性能，通过对比学习优化代码表示，为相关研究提供了模型检查点、数据集、代码和交互式演示等资源。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Large Language Models (LLMs) in software development has made distinguishing AI-generated code from human-written code a critical challenge with implications for academic integrity, code quality assurance, and software security. We present LLMSniffer, a detection framework that fine-tunes GraphCodeBERT using a two-stage supervised contrastive learning pipeline augmented with comment removal preprocessing and an MLP classifier. Evaluated on two benchmark datasets - GPTSniffer and Whodunit - LLMSniffer achieves substantial improvements over prior baselines: accuracy increases from 70% to 78% on GPTSniffer (F1: 68% to 78%) and from 91% to 94.65% on Whodunit (F1: 91% to 94.64%). t-SNE visualizations confirm that contrastive fine-tuning yields well-separated, compact embeddings. We release our model checkpoints, datasets, codes and a live interactive demo to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [46] [Bridging the Gap between User Intent and LLM: A Requirement Alignment Approach for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16198)
*Jia Li,Ruiqi Bai,Yangkang Luo,Yiran Zhang,Wentao Yang,Zeyu Sun,Tiankuo Zhao,Dongming Jin,Lei Li,Zhi Jin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: REA-Coder：一种通过需求对齐提升大语言模型代码生成性能的方法，通过识别LLM不理解的需求内容、对齐需求、生成代码并验证对齐，迭代进行直至生成正确代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成方法都假设LLM能正确理解给定需求，但这一假设并不总是成立。当LLM无法正确理解需求时，即使采用先进的推理策略或后优化方法，生成的代码也可能不正确。因此需要解决LLM与需求之间的对齐问题。

Method: REA-Coder采用需求对齐方法：1) 识别LLM不理解的需求内容；2) 对齐需求；3) 基于对齐后的需求生成代码；4) 验证生成的代码是否与需求对齐。这是一个迭代过程，直到生成正确代码或达到最大迭代次数。

Result: 在4个LLM和5个编程基准测试上，REA-Coder均优于所有先进基线方法。具体来说，在五个基准数据集上平均提升了7.93%、30.25%、26.75%、8.59%和8.64%，证明了需求对齐对提升LLM代码生成性能的有效性。

Conclusion: 需求对齐是提升LLM代码生成性能的关键因素。REA-Coder通过迭代的需求对齐和代码生成验证过程，有效解决了LLM可能误解需求的问题，显著提高了代码生成的正确性。

Abstract: Code generation refers to automatically producing executable programs from user requirements. Recently, researchers have explored approaches to enhance the correctness of generated code with advanced large language models. Although achieving improvements, existing approaches focus on designing reasoning strategies or post-refinement methods to enhance code generation performance. Despite their differences, all these methods share a common assumption: the LLM can correctly understand the given requirement. However, this assumption does not always hold. To fill this gap, we propose REA-Coder, a requirement alignment approach to enhance the code generation performance of LLMs. REA-Coder involves first identifying the requirement content that does not align with LLMs and aligning the requirements. Then, based on the aligned requirements, LLMs generate code and further verify whether the generated code aligns with the requirements, iterating this process of requirement alignment and code generation until generating correct code or achieving the maximum number of iterations. Experimental results show that REA-Coder outperforms all advanced baselines on four LLMs across five programming benchmarks. Concretely, REA-Coder achieves average improvements of 7.93%, 30.25%, 26.75%, 8.59%, and 8.64% on the five benchmark datasets, demonstrating the effectiveness of requirement alignment for improving the code generation performance of LLMs.

</details>


### [47] [From Papers to Progress: Rethinking Knowledge Accumulation in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16208)
*Jason Cusati,Chris Brown*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文分析软件工程研究领域知识积累与整合的挑战，基于ICSE 2026调查数据诊断四个结构性障碍，并提出未来研究产物的四项设计原则以支持累积性科学进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程研究产出快速增长，但领域内知识积累、整合和重用的能力存在持续担忧。研究者希望了解社区自身如何认知这些挑战，通过分析ICSE 2026调查数据来理解社区对长期进步障碍的看法。

Method: 分析ICSE 2026未来软件工程预调查的280份全球分布、经验丰富研究者的回应数据，识别研究生产力与知识整合机制之间的张力，诊断结构性障碍。

Result: 研究发现研究生产力增长与知识整合机制有限之间存在张力，诊断出四个相互关联的结构性障碍：论文作为孤立知识单元、上下文和来源信息丢失、主张演变缺乏系统追踪、激励机制偏向新颖性而非整合。

Conclusion: 需要重新思考研究产物的基本属性，提出四项技术无关的设计原则：结构化可解释的主张与证据表示、可检查的方法决策文档、超越出版的长寿命可重用基底、协调个体激励与集体知识构建的治理机制。FOSE可作为实验替代性产物设计的平台。

Abstract: Software engineering research has experienced rapid growth in both output and participation over the past decades. Yet concerns persist about the field's ability to accumulate, integrate, and reuse knowledge in ways that support long-term progress. To better understand how the community itself perceives these challenges, we analyze responses from the ICSE 2026 Future of Software Engineering pre-survey, which captures perspectives from 280 globally distributed and highly experienced researchers. Our analysis reveals a tension between increasing research productivity and the limited mechanisms available for synthesizing results, tracking evolving claims, and supporting cumulative understanding over time.
  Building on these observations, we diagnose four interrelated structural breakdowns: papers function as isolated knowledge units with claims embedded in prose; context and provenance are lost as knowledge moves through the publication pipeline; claims evolve without systematic tracking; and incentive structures favor novelty over consolidation. We argue that addressing these barriers requires rethinking the fundamental properties of research artifacts.
  We articulate four technology-agnostic principles for future research artifacts: structured and interpretable representations of claims and evidence; inspectable and provenance-aware documentation of methodological decisions; long-lived and reusable substrates that evolve beyond publication; and governance mechanisms that align individual incentives with collective knowledge-building goals. We discuss implications for research practice, publication norms, and community infrastructure, positioning FOSE as a venue for experimenting with alternative artifact designs that support cumulative scientific progress.

</details>

### [48] [An Agentic Workflow for Detecting Personally Identifiable Information in Crash Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15369)
*Junyi Ma,Pei Li,Rui Gan,Kai Cheng,Steven T. Parker,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 开发了一个本地可部署的智能工作流，利用大语言模型检测交通事故报告中的个人身份信息，通过混合提取器和验证器实现高精度PII检测，支持隐私敏感环境下的可扩展数据处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通事故报告中的叙述包含重要的安全分析信息，但其中存在稀疏且不一致的个人身份信息（PII），如姓名、家庭地址和车牌号。手动检测不可扩展，现有基于规则的方法难以捕捉上下文相关的PII，这限制了数据的广泛使用。

Method: 开发了一个本地可部署的智能工作流，包含混合提取器和验证器。混合提取器将结构化PII（如电话号码、电子邮件地址）路由到基于规则的模型（Presidio），将上下文相关的PII（如姓名、家庭地址、字母数字标识符）路由到领域适应、微调的大语言模型。针对具有挑战性的类别，工作流集成了集成LLM提取和智能验证步骤，通过基于证据的推理过滤误检。

Result: 在真实交通事故数据集上的评估显示，智能工作流实现了精确率0.82、召回率0.94、F1分数0.87和准确率0.96的强性能，优于多个基线方法。消融结果表明，集成LLM提取和验证器对家庭地址和字母数字标识符的检测有改进作用。

Conclusion: 该工作流可在本地运行，支持外部API受限的隐私敏感操作环境，为可扩展、隐私保护的交通事故数据处理提供了实用且稳健的路径，能够在保护个人隐私的同时支持更广泛的研究和安全干预。

Abstract: Crash narratives in crash reports provide crucial contextual information for traffic safety analysis. Yet, their broader use is hindered by the presence of personally identifiable information (PII), including names, home addresses, and license plate numbers. Because PII appears sparsely and inconsistently in crash narratives, manual detection is not scalable, and existing rule-based approaches often fail to capture context-dependent PII. This study develops and evaluates a locally deployable, agentic workflow for PII detection in crash narratives by leveraging large language models (LLMs). The workflow contains a Hybrid Extractor and a Verifier. The Hybrid Extractor routes structured PII (e.g., phone numbers and email addresses) to a rule-based model (i.e., Presidio) and context-dependent PII (e.g., names, home addresses, and alphanumeric identifiers) to a domain-adapted, fine-tuned LLM. To address ambiguity in challenging categories, the workflow incorporates ensemble LLM extraction and an agentic verification step that filters false detections through evidence-based reasoning. Evaluated on a real-world crash dataset, the agentic workflow achieves strong performance with a precision of 0.82, a recall of 0.94, an F1 of 0.87, and an accuracy of 0.96, outperforming multiple baseline methods. Moreover, the ablation results suggest that ensemble LLM extraction and Verifier offer improved detection for home addresses and alphanumeric identifiers. The workflow runs locally, supporting privacy-sensitive operational settings where external APIs are restricted. This work offers a practical and robust path for scalable, privacy-preserving crash data processing, enabling broader research and safety interventions while safeguarding individual privacy.

</details>


### [49] [TopFeaRe: Locating Critical State of Adversarial Resilience for Graphs Regarding Topology-Feature Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15370)
*Xinxin Fan,Wenxiong Chen,Quanliang Jing,Chi Lin,Shaoye Luo,Wenbo Song,Yunfeng Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于复杂动态系统平衡点理论的图对抗防御方法，通过定位图的对抗弹性临界状态来防御拓扑和特征攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法未能揭示图对抗攻击中拓扑和特征两个维度为何需要以及如何充分融合来共同学习图表示的内在原因

Method: 1) 将图映射到复杂动态系统，用系统振荡建模对抗扰动行为；2) 设计二维拓扑-特征纠缠函数表示扰动图；3) 利用平衡点理论定位图的对抗弹性临界状态

Result: 在五个常用真实数据集上的多面实验验证了方法的有效性，结果显示在四种代表性图对抗攻击下显著优于现有基线方法

Conclusion: 通过复杂动态系统理论为图对抗防御提供了新视角，成功定位图的对抗弹性临界状态，实现了拓扑和特征维度的有效融合防御

Abstract: Graph adversarial attacks are usually produced from the two perspectives of topology/structure and node feature, both of them represent the paramount characteristics learned by today's deep learning models. Although some defense countermeasures are proposed at present, they fails to disclose the intrinsic reasons why these two aspects necessitate and how they are adequately fused to co-learn the graph representation. Towards this question, we in this paper propose an adversarial defense approach through locating the graph's critical state of adversarial resilience, resorting to the equilibrium-point theory in the discipline of complex dynamic system (CDS). In brief, our work has three novelties: i) Adversarial-Attack Modeling, i.e. map a graph regime into CDS, and use the oscillation of dynamic system to model the behavior of adversarial perturbation; ii) 2D Topology-Feature-Entangled Function Design for Perturbed Graph, i.e. project graph topology and node feature as two characteristic spaces, and define two-dimensional entangled perturbation functions to represent the dynamic variance under adversarial attacks; and iii) Location of Critical State of Adversarial Resilience, i.e. utilize the equilibrium-point theory to locate the graph's critical state of attack resilience resorting to the perturbation-reflected 2D function. Finally, multi-facet experiments on five commonly-used realistic datasets validate the effectiveness of our proposed approach, and the results show our approach can significantly outperform the state-of-the-art baselines under four representative graph adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [50] [The Synthetic Media Shift: Tracking the Rise, Virality, and Detectability of AI-Generated Multimodal Misinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15372)
*Zacharias Chrysidis,Stefanos-Iordanis Papadopoulos,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CONVEX是一个包含15万+多模态帖子的虚假信息数据集，分析显示AI生成内容虽然传播更广但主要是被动参与，检测器性能随时间下降


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI的发展，真实与合成媒体的界限日益模糊，挑战在线信息的完整性，需要研究多模态虚假信息的传播动态和检测方法

Method: 构建CONVEX大规模多模态虚假信息数据集，包含错误标注、编辑和AI生成的视觉内容，分析传播性、参与度和共识动态，评估专门检测器和视觉语言模型性能

Result: AI生成内容获得不成比例的传播性但主要是被动参与；虽然初始报告较慢，但一旦被标记能更快达成社区共识；检测器性能随时间持续下降

Conclusion: 数字信息环境快速演变，需要持续监控和自适应策略来应对合成媒体带来的挑战

Abstract: As generative AI advances, the distinction between authentic and synthetic media is increasingly blurred, challenging the integrity of online information. In this study, we present CONVEX, a large-scale dataset of multimodal misinformation involving miscaptioned, edited, and AI-generated visual content, comprising over 150K multimodal posts with associated notes and engagement metrics from X's Community Notes. We analyze how multimodal misinformation evolves in terms of virality, engagement, and consensus dynamics, with a focus on synthetic media. Our results show that while AI-generated content achieves disproportionate virality, its spread is driven primarily by passive engagement rather than active discourse. Despite slower initial reporting, AI-generated content reaches community consensus more quickly once flagged. Moreover, our evaluation of specialized detectors and vision-language models reveals a consistent decline in performance over time in distinguishing synthetic from authentic images as generative models evolve. These findings highlight the need for continuous monitoring and adaptive strategies in the rapidly evolving digital information environment.

</details>


### [51] [LinuxArena: A Control Setting for AI Agents in Live Production Software Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15384)
*Tyler Tracy,Ram Potham,Nick Kuhn,Myles Heller,Anshul Khandelwal,Cody Rushing,Henri Lemoine,Miguel Brandao,Tomas Turlik,Adam Hanson,Josh Hills,Amy Ngo,Ram Rachum,Nik Mitchell,Falko Galperin,Oscar Sykes,Pip Arnott,Samuel Prieto Lima,Carlos Giudice,Matt Goldwater,Daniel Popp,Drew de Wet,Ruben Castaing,Qi Guo,Douw Marx,Benjamin Shaffrey,Justin Shenk,Martin Milbradt,Hannah Meagher,Shaheen Ahmed-Chowdhury,Daniel O'Connell,Chris Canal,Buck Shlegeris,Aryan Bhatt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LinuxArena是一个在真实多服务生产环境中运行的智能体控制测试平台，包含20个环境、1671个主要任务和184个安全侧任务，是目前最大最全面的软件工程控制测试环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要建立一个能够真实反映生产环境复杂性的控制测试平台，用于评估智能体在软件工程任务中的安全性和控制能力，特别是检测攻击者能否在完成正常任务的同时进行恶意活动。

Method: 创建LinuxArena平台，包含真实的多服务生产环境、大量软件工程任务和安全侧任务。通过破坏性评估（测量攻击者完成侧任务的能力）和监控评估（测量监控模型检测破坏尝试的能力）来验证平台的有效性。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6在GPT-5-nano监控器（1%步级误报率）下实现了约23%的未检测破坏成功率。同时发布的LaStraj数据集显示，人工制作的攻击轨迹比任何模型生成的攻击具有更高的规避监控成功率。

Conclusion: LinuxArena为攻击者和防御者都提供了有意义的改进空间，使其成为开发和评估未来控制协议的强大测试平台，表明当前攻击策略尚未饱和该平台的能力。

Abstract: We introduce LinuxArena, a control setting in which agents operate directly on live, multi-service production environments. LinuxArena contains 20 environments, 1,671 main tasks representing legitimate software engineering work, and 184 side tasks representing safety failures such as data exfiltration and backdooring, making it the largest and most diverse control setting for software engineering to date. We validate LinuxArena is useful for control research by running sabotage evaluations, which measure whether attackers can complete side tasks while working on main tasks, and monitor evaluations, which measure a monitor model's ability to detect sabotage attempts. Against a GPT-5-nano trusted monitor at a 1\% step-wise false positive rate, Claude Opus 4.6 achieves roughly a 23% undetected sabotage success rate. We additionally release LaStraj, a dataset of human-crafted attack trajectories that evade monitors at substantially higher rates than any model-generated attacks we elicited, showing that current attack policies do not saturate LinuxArena. These results suggest that LinuxArena has meaningful headroom for both attackers and defenders, making it a strong testbed for developing and evaluating future control protocols.

</details>


### [52] [Graded Symbolic Verification with a Fuzzy Dolev-Yao Attacker Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15402)
*Murat Moran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种考虑累积侧信道泄漏的协议验证方法，使用分级攻击者视图和T-范数泄漏更新，发现传统二元知识模型会遗漏某些安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Dolev-Yao符号协议验证使用二元攻击者知识模型（已知/未知），这种抽象无法捕捉累积侧信道场景，其中重复的噪声观测会逐步提高攻击者知识。需要更精细的模型来评估协议在累积侧信道攻击下的安全性。

Method: 使用分级攻击者视图μ_K∈[0,1]建模攻击者知识，采用乘积T-范数进行泄漏更新，在Modified Murphi中实现有限网格显式状态执行。方法通过精确概念格属性约简进行优化，并暴露阈值驱动的安全到失败转换。

Result: 在对称和非对称协议（包括Needham-Schroeder-Lowe）上的执行结果表明，在清晰语义下通过基线模型的协议，一旦启用累积侧信道泄漏就可能失败。该方法揭示了传统二元运行中未表示的阈值驱动安全到失败转换。

Conclusion: 累积侧信道泄漏对协议安全性有显著影响，传统二元知识模型不足以捕捉这种威胁。提出的分级攻击者视图和T-范数泄漏更新方法能够更准确地评估协议在现实侧信道攻击下的安全性，揭示了传统验证方法遗漏的安全漏洞。

Abstract: Classical symbolic protocol verification under Dolev--Yao uses binary attacker knowledge (known/unknown). This abstraction misses cumulative side-channel settings, where repeated noisy observations progressively improve attacker knowledge. We model this process with a graded attacker view \(μ_K\in[0,1]\), product T-norm leak updates, and finite-grid explicit-state execution in Modified Murphi.
  The method is optimised with exact concept-lattice attribute reducts and exposes threshold-driven safe-to-fail transitions that are not represented in corresponding binary runs under the same bounded assumptions. Executed results on symmetric and asymmetric protocols, including Needham--Schroeder--Lowe (NSL), show that baseline models passing under crisp semantics can fail once cumulative side-channel leakage is enabled.

</details>


### [53] [CSLE: A Reinforcement Learning Platform for Autonomous Security Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15590)
*Kim Hammar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CSLE是一个用于自主安全管理的强化学习平台，通过仿真和模拟系统结合，在接近真实操作环境的条件下实现近乎最优的安全管理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习在安全管理中的应用大多局限于仿真环境，缺乏在实际操作系统中泛化能力的验证，需要构建能够连接理论与实际操作的实验平台

Method: CSLE平台包含两个核心系统：1) 仿真系统 - 在虚拟化环境中复制目标系统关键组件，收集测量数据和日志，识别系统模型（如马尔可夫决策过程）；2) 模拟系统 - 通过系统模型模拟高效学习安全策略，然后在仿真系统中评估和优化策略，缩小理论与实际操作性能差距

Result: 通过四个用例（流量控制、复制控制、分段控制和恢复控制）展示了CSLE能够在近似操作系统的环境中实现近乎最优的安全管理

Conclusion: CSLE平台成功解决了强化学习在安全管理中理论与实际操作的脱节问题，为自主安全管理提供了有效的实验平台，能够在接近真实环境的条件下实现高性能的安全管理策略

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a promising approach to autonomous and adaptive security management in networked systems. However, current reinforcement learning solutions for security management are mostly limited to simulation environments and it is unclear how they generalize to operational systems. In this paper, we address this limitation by presenting CSLE: a reinforcement learning platform for autonomous security management that enables experimentation under realistic conditions. Conceptually, CSLE encompasses two systems. First, it includes an emulation system that replicates key components of the target system in a virtualized environment. We use this system to gather measurements and logs, based on which we identify a system model, such as a Markov decision process. Second, it includes a simulation system where security strategies are efficiently learned through simulations of the system model. The learned strategies are then evaluated and refined in the emulation system to close the gap between theoretical and operational performance. We demonstrate CSLE through four use cases: flow control, replication control, segmentation control, and recovery control. Through these use cases, we show that CSLE enables near-optimal security management in an environment that approximates an operational system.

</details>


### [54] [Privacy, Prediction, and Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15596)
*Ben Jacobsen,Nitin Kohli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究在差分隐私约束下，基于算法预测的稀缺资源分配系统，分析个体级与单元级分配策略在隐私、效率和目标精度之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法预测越来越多地用于稀缺资源分配，但最近研究表明简单的单元级分配策略可能优于个体级目标分配。同时，隐私问题也反对个体级目标分配，导致单元级分配成为隐私和效用双重推荐方案。作者希望全面理解隐私与目标层级之间的相互作用。

Method: 将差分隐私与经济学援助分配模型相结合，研究个体级和单元级分配策略的私有化变体，在随机和无分布设置下，考虑数据可用性的各种约束条件。

Result: 通过分析提供了清晰、可解释的界限，描述了分配中隐私、效率和目标精度之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统性地将差分隐私与援助分配模型相结合，为理解隐私约束下的资源分配策略提供了理论框架和分析工具。

Abstract: Algorithmic predictions are increasingly used to inform the allocation of scarce resources. The promise of these methods is that, through machine learning, they can better identify the people who would benefit most from interventions. Recently, however, several works have called this assumption into question by demonstrating the existence of settings where simple, unit-level allocation strategies can meet or even exceed the performance of those based on individual-level targeting. Separately, other works have objected to individual-level targeting on privacy grounds, leading to an unusual situation where a single solution, unit-level targeting, is recommended for reasons of both privacy and utility. Motivated by the desire to fully understand the interplay of privacy and targeting levels, we initiate the study of aid allocation systems that satisfy differential privacy, synthesizing existing works on private optimization with the economic models of aid allocation used in the non-private literature. To this end, we investigate private variants of both individual and unit-level allocation strategies in both stochastic and distribution-free settings under a range of constraints on data availability. Through this analysis, we provide clean, interpretable bounds characterizing the tradeoffs between privacy, efficiency, and targeting precision in allocation.

</details>


### [55] [Too Private to Tell: Practical Token Theft Attacks on Apple Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15637)
*Haoling Zhou,Shixuan Zhao,Chao Wang,Zhiqiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究人员发现了苹果智能（Apple Intelligence）AI服务中的Serpent攻击漏洞，这是一种跨设备令牌重放攻击，允许攻击者窃取受害者的访问令牌并在其他设备上使用，所有使用量都会计入受害者的配额。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 苹果智能声称通过两阶段认证授权设计和匿名访问令牌特别关注用户安全和隐私。本研究旨在通过流量分析、逆向工程和与苹果公开文档的交叉比对，揭示该令牌发放机制中可能存在的漏洞。

Method: 采用流量分析、逆向工程和与苹果公开文档的交叉比对方法，研究苹果智能的令牌发放机制。具体提出了Serpent攻击，这是首个针对苹果智能的实用跨设备令牌重放攻击。

Result: 成功在最新的macOS 26 Tahoe上实施攻击，证明即使攻击者自身配额已用完，也能立即重新获得苹果智能服务访问权限。已向厂商负责任地披露漏洞，并获得苹果确认，分配了CVE编号和漏洞赏金。

Conclusion: 匿名化身份本身并不能确保AI服务的安全性；要强制执行不可转移性，需要将令牌与合法用户进行密码学绑定。这一发现为内置AI服务提供了重要安全教训。

Abstract: Apple Intelligence is a generative AI (GenAI) service provided by Apple on its devices. While offering a similar set of features as other similar GenAI services, Apple Intelligence is claimed to be designed with an extra focus on user security and privacy through a two-stage authentication and authorization design using anonymous access tokens. In this paper, we present our investigation into this token issuance mechanism with a goal to reveal possible vulnerabilities using traffic analysis, reverse engineering, and cross comparison with Apple's public documentation. Specifically, we present the Serpent attack, the first practical cross-device token replay attack against Apple Intelligence that allows the attacker to steal the access tokens from the victim's device and utilise them on a different device, with all usage rate-limited against the victim. We have achieved successful attacks on the latest macOS 26 Tahoe and demonstrated that an attacker, who even has used up its own allowance, can immediately regain access to Apple Intelligence service. We have responsibly disclosed the vulnerabilities to the vendors and received confirmation from Apple with CVE assigned and bounty given. Our results highlight a general lesson for built-in AI services: Anonymising identity does not by itself make the AI service secure; Enforcing non-transferability requires cryptographic binding to the rightful user.

</details>


### [56] [Half-Moon Cookie: Private, Similarity-Based Blocklisting with TOCTOU-Attack Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15641)
*Xinyuan Zhang,Anrin Chakraborti,Michael K. Reiter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Half-Moon Cookie：一种私有黑名单框架，允许客户端检查项目是否接近服务器持有的专有黑名单中的元素，同时保护客户端查询隐私和黑名单机密性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑名单技术存在隐私泄露问题：要么需要公开黑名单，要么客户端需要向服务器暴露查询内容。需要一种既能保护客户端隐私又能保护服务器专有黑名单机密性的解决方案

Method: 提出Half-Moon Cookie框架，将嵌入步骤与黑名单检查分离，使性能取决于两者的和而非乘积。支持两种检查模式：完整的黑名单检查（用于初始验证）和高效的验证检查（用于确认先前结果），防止TOCTOU攻击

Result: 该框架可实例化为基于相似性的恶意软件检测系统，能够有效识别恶意可执行文件，同时不泄露客户端输入和底层黑名单内容

Conclusion: Half-Moon Cookie提供了一种实用的私有黑名单检查方案，平衡了隐私保护、性能效率和安全性需求，特别适用于需要保护专有检测模型和用户隐私的恶意软件检测场景

Abstract: Blocklisting is a common technique for preventing the use of known malicious content. However, conventional blocklisting infrastructures require either the blocklist to be public or clients to reveal their queries to the blocklist server. In this work, we introduce a private blocklisting framework, Half-Moon Cookie, by which a client can check an item against a proprietary blocklist held by a server, to determine whether the item is close to any blocklist element in a metric space. Critically, our design separates the embedding step from the blocklist check, so that performance degrades with their sum and not their product. Still, this check might be too costly to perform on the critical path of using the item, and so our design also supports a very efficient check that an item previously passed the blocklist check. In doing so, we support applications where one client can perform the blocklist check on the item before sending it, and recipients can more efficiently confirm the previous result before using the item, thereby avoiding TOCTOU attacks. We demonstrate how Half-Moon Cookie can be instantiated for similarity-based malware detection, enabling effective identification of malicious executables without revealing client inputs or disclosing the underlying blocklist.

</details>


### [57] [DPDSyn: Improving Differentially Private Dataset Synthesis for Model Training by Downstream Task Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15660)
*Mingxuan Jia,Wen Huang,Weixin Zhao,Xingyi Wang,Jian Peng,Zhishuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DPDSyn方法，通过训练满足差分隐私的下游任务AI模型来合成数据集，在保护隐私的同时提升数据效用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私数据集合成方法通过选择原始数据的低维分布来近似原始分布，但难以选择合适的低维分布，导致合成数据集的数据效用降低。需要一种既能保护隐私又能保持数据效用的合成方法。

Method: 首先在原始私有数据集上训练满足差分隐私的AI模型用于下游任务，然后利用该模型合成数据集。模型满足差分隐私确保隐私保护，同时模型为完成下游任务而训练，保留了完成任务的关键信息。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，DPDSyn方法在准确率上最大提升2.40倍，合成效率最大提升333.73倍，优于八个最先进的基线方法。进一步实验验证了DPDSyn在不同数据规模下具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过训练差分隐私AI模型并利用其合成数据集的方法，能够在保护隐私的同时显著提升合成数据集的数据效用和合成效率，为解决差分隐私数据集合成问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: How to synthesize a dataset while achieving differential privacy for AI model training is a meaningful but challenging problem. To address this problem, state-of-the-art methods first select original private dataset's multiple low-dimensional distributions that have the potential to approximate the distribution of original private dataset with high precision, and then synthesize a dataset obeying all selected low-dimensional distributions as the synthetic dataset. However, it is difficult to select suitable low-dimensional distributions, which in turn degrades the data utility of resulting synthetic dataset. To improve differentially private dataset synthesis, we propose to train a differentially private AI model for downstream tasks on the original private dataset and utilize the trained model to synthesize datasets. In particular, on the one hand, the AI model satisfies differential privacy so no matter how to use the model does not disclose private information of original private dataset. On the other hand, the AI model is trained to complete the downstream task so the AI model preserves critical information for completing downstream tasks. We utilize the AI model to synthesize datasets to achieve the goal of improving data utility while preserving privacy. Empirical evaluations on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed DPDSyn consistently outperforms eight state-of-the-art baselines with a maximum improvement of 2.40x in accuracy and 333.73x in synthesis efficiency. Further experiments also validate that DPDSyn has strong scalability across varying data scales.

</details>


### [58] [Privacy-Preserving LLMs Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15728)
*Xidong Wu,Yukuan Zhang,Yuqiong Ji,Reza Shirkavand,Qian Lou,Shangqian Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PPRoute是一个保护隐私的LLM路由框架，通过MPC友好操作、多步模型训练算法和O(1)通信复杂度的Top-k算法，在保证路由质量的同时显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM路由在用户和LLM之间增加了中间层，带来了新的隐私风险，而现有的隐私保护技术如MPC在协议设计和实现方面研究不足，且朴素实现通常带来过高的计算开销

Method: 1. 使用MPC友好操作加速编码器推理；2. 采用多步模型训练算法在加密域约束下保持路由质量；3. 提出具有O(1)通信复杂度的未排序Top-k算法用于安全模型搜索

Result: 在不同数据集上，PPRoute达到了明文对应方案的性能，同时相比朴素MPC实现实现了约20倍的加速

Conclusion: PPRoute成功解决了LLM路由中的隐私保护问题，通过优化的MPC实现显著降低了计算开销，为隐私保护的LLM路由提供了可行的解决方案

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as a critical strategy to balance model performance and cost-efficiency by dynamically selecting services from various model providers. However, LLM routing adds an intermediate layer between users and LLMs, creating new privacy risks to user data. These privacy risks have not been systematically studied. Although cryptographic techniques such as Secure Multi-Party Computation (MPC) enable privacy-preserving computation, their protocol design and implementation remain under-explored, and naïve implementations typically incur prohibitive computational overhead. To address this, we propose a privacy-preserving LLM routing framework (PPRoute). PPRoute includes multiple strategies to speed up encoder inference and nearest neighbor search under the MPC and maintain the quality of LLM routing. First, PPRoute uses MPC-friendly operations to boost the encoder inference. Second, PPRoute uses a multiple-step model training algorithm to maintain routing quality despite the constraints of the encrypted domain. Third, PPRoute proposes an unsorted Top-k algorithm with $O(1)$ communication complexity for secure sorting in model search, significantly reducing communication latency. Across different datasets, PPRoute achieves the performance of plaintext counterparts, while achieving approximately a 20$\times$ speedup over naïve MPC implementations.

</details>


### [59] [Secure Authentication in Wireless IoT: Hamming Code Assisted SRAM PUF as Device Fingerprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15810)
*Florian Lehn,Pascal Ahr,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 基于SRAM PUF的IIoT设备阈值认证方案，通过HC纠错和TMV将认证后BER控制在1%以下，建立可靠性-安全性权衡的设计空间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对受限工业物联网设备，利用SRAM PUF的硬件固有特性进行认证，需要解决SRAM PUF固有的不可靠性问题，同时平衡资源约束与安全要求

Method: 提出阈值认证方案，结合汉明码纠错和时序多数投票来稳定PUF响应，量化可靠性与安全性约束之间的阈值差距，将其重构为设计预算

Result: 方案能将认证后比特错误率可靠控制在1%以下；增加HC冗余或TMV计数可降低BER但收益递减且计算开销增加；较大响应长度使可靠性优化逐渐过时

Conclusion: 建立了PUF纠错的综合设计空间，指导未来实现平衡纠错质量与计算需求、功耗、实现复杂度等资源约束，支持资源感知的阈值校准

Abstract: Static Random Access Memory (SRAM) Physically Unclonable Functions (PUFs) make use of intrinsic manufacturing variations in memory cells to derive device-unique responses. Employing such hardware-rooted fingerprints for authentication, this work demonstrates a threshold-based authentication proof of concept for constrained Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) devices. The proposed scheme can reliably cap the the post-authentication bit error rate (BER) below 1 %. Inherent SRAM PUF unreliability is addressed by a resource-efficient combination of Hamming code (HC) Error Correction (EC) and Temporal Majority Voting (TMV). Increasing HC redundancy or TMV count significantly reduces the BER, albeit with diminishing returns and increasingly prohibitive computational overhead. Furthermore, this work quantifies the threshold gap between strict reliability and security constraints. This gap is reframed as a design budget which enables the resource-aware calibration of the acceptance threshold, PUF response length, and stabilization technique, without violating designed-for error limits. Larger responses make reliability optimizations increasingly obsolete. This comparative analysis establishes a comprehensive design space for PUF EC, guiding future implementations in balancing EC quality against resource constraints such as computational demand, power consumption, and implementation complexity.

</details>


### [60] [QUACK! Making the (Rubber) Ducky Talk: A Systematic Study of Keystroke Dynamics for HID Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15845)
*Alessandro Lotto,Francesco Marchiori,Mauro Conti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统性地研究了基于击键动态的人类与机器输入区分方法，提出了一种轻量级、保护隐私的检测方案，仅使用时序特征即可有效识别自动化USB HID攻击，无需访问输入内容或用户画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算系统默认信任人类输入设备，这为自动化攻击创造了可乘之机。USB HID仿真攻击（如USB Rubber Ducky）利用这一假设注入任意击键序列，绕过传统防御。现有基于打字速度或时序规律性的启发式方法容易被随机化规避。击键动态分析虽更鲁棒，但先前研究多聚焦于行为认证（验证特定用户），而非检测自动化注入。连续监控方法需访问输入内容，引发隐私担忧。

Method: 通过五个研究问题指导，系统分析击键动态用于人机区分。采用轻量级模型，仅使用时序特征，无需输入内容访问或用户画像。研究攻击者复杂度与规避能力的关系，分析模型鲁棒性对多样化生成策略的依赖程度。量化检测及时性与可靠性在不同击键序列长度下的权衡。

Result: 研究表明：1）仅使用时序特征的轻量级模型可实现鲁棒且保护隐私的检测；2）攻击者复杂度并不单调转化为更好的规避能力；3）模型鲁棒性依赖于接触结构多样化的生成策略，而非增加模型复杂度；4）确定了不同击键序列长度下检测及时性与可靠性的权衡关系，识别出早期有效拦截攻击的实际操作点。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统性地将击键动态应用于人机区分而非用户认证，证明了仅使用时序特征的轻量级模型可实现隐私保护的自动化攻击检测。攻击者复杂度并非决定性因素，模型鲁棒性关键在于接触多样化的攻击策略。研究为早期有效拦截USB HID仿真攻击提供了实用解决方案，平衡了检测及时性与可靠性。

Abstract: Modern computing systems inherently trust human input devices, creating an exploitable attack surface for adversarial automation. USB Human Interface Device (HID) emulation attacks, such as those enabled by the USB Rubber Ducky, exploit this assumption to inject arbitrary keystroke sequences while bypassing traditional defenses. Existing countermeasures rely on simple heuristics based on typing speed or timing regularity, which can be easily evaded through basic randomization. Keystroke dynamics analysis offers a more robust alternative by modeling temporal typing behavior. However, prior work frames this problem as behavioral authentication, verifying whether input originates from a specific user rather than detecting automated injection. An alternative approach is continuous monitoring via keylogging integrated with intrusion detection systems, but this requires access to input content, raising significant privacy concerns.
  In this paper, we provide the first systematic characterization of keystroke dynamics for human-vs-machine discrimination, independent of user identity. Guided by five research questions, we show that robust, privacy-preserving detection is achievable using lightweight models operating solely on timing features, eliminating the need for content access or user profiling. Our analysis reveals that attacker sophistication does not monotonically translate into improved evasion. Instead, robustness depends on exposure to structurally diverse generation strategies rather than increased model complexity. Finally, we quantify the trade-off between detection timeliness and reliability across varying keystroke sequence lengths, identifying practical operating points for early and effective attack interception.

</details>


### [61] [Module Lattice Security (Part I): Unconditional Verification of Weber's Conjecture for $k \le 12$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15858)
*Ming-Xing Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次为k≤12提供了Weber猜想的无条件证明，无需依赖广义黎曼假设


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weber猜想在格基密码学中至关重要，涉及主理想问题可解性、整数环上模的自由性以及Ring-LWE和Module-LWE最坏情况到平均情况归约的紧致性。现有验证（k≥9）依赖广义黎曼假设，需要无条件证明。

Method: 结合Fukuda-Komatsu计算筛法、分圆Z₂-塔的归纳结构以及Herbrand定理

Result: 首次为k≤12提供了Weber猜想的无条件证明，无需依赖广义黎曼假设

Conclusion: 该方法为格基密码学基础问题提供了无条件验证，减少了对未证明假设的依赖

Abstract: Weber's conjecture (1886) governs three aspects of lattice-based cryptography: the solvability of the Principal Ideal Problem, the freeness of modules over rings of integers, and the tightness of worst-case-to-average-case reductions in Ring-LWE (R-LWE) and Module-LWE (MLWE). Existing verifications for $k \ge 9$ rely on Generalized Riemann Hypothesis (GRH). In this paper, we present the first unconditional proof for $k \le 12$. Our method combines the Fukuda-Komatsu computational sieve, inductive structure of the cyclotomic $\mathbb{Z}_2$-tower, and Herbrand's theorem.

</details>


### [62] [Low-Stack HAETAE for Memory-Constrained Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15868)
*Gustavo Banegas,Kim Youngbeom,Seo Seog Chung,Vredendaal Christine Van*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对8-16kB SRAM微控制器的HAETAE模块格签名方案的低栈实现，通过多项优化技术将签名栈从71-141kB降至5.8-6.0kB


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内存受限的微控制器上，峰值栈使用通常是关键约束。HAETAE的超球采样器、大型瞬态多项式向量和变长签名载荷（提示和高位数组）对这类设备构成了特殊挑战，需要降低栈使用量以适应8-16kB SRAM环境。

Method: 采用三项关键技术：(1)拒绝感知的传递分解，将编码隔离到接受后路径；(2)组件级早期拒绝，当部分范数已超过界限时短路响应计算；(3)使用范围非对称数字系统(rANS)的逆序流式熵编码，消除完整的提示和高位暂存缓冲区。结合流式矩阵生成、带流式高斯后端的双通超球采样器以及行流式验证。

Result: 将签名栈从参考实现的71-141kB降至5.8-6.0kB，密钥生成降至4.7-5.7kB，验证降至4.7-4.8kB。在三个安全级别上分别减少了密钥生成的75%、86%和8%，签名生成的92%、95%和24%，验证的85%、91%和22%。验证在所有安全级别上都适合8kB RAM（签名缓冲区+栈），比ML-DSA m4fstack快2.34-3.34倍。

Conclusion: 提出的优化技术成功将HAETAE签名方案适配到内存受限的微控制器环境，显著降低了栈使用量，同时保持了可接受的性能开销。验证性能甚至有所提升，证明了该低栈实现的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: We present a low-stack implementation of the module-lattice signature scheme HAETAE, targeting microcontrollers with 8 kB-16 kB of available SRAM. On such devices, peak stack usage is often the binding constraint, and HAETAE's hyperball-based sampler, large transient polynomial vectors, and variable-length signature payloads (hint and high-bits arrays) pose a particular challenge. To address this we introduce (i) Rejection-aware pass decomposition, which isolates encoding to the post-acceptance path; (ii) Component-level early rejection, which short-circuits the response computation when a partial norm already exceeds the bound; and (iii) Reverse-order streaming entropy coding using range Asymmetric Numeral Systems (rANS), which eliminates full hint and high-bits staging buffers. Combined with streamed matrix generation, a two-pass hyperball sampler with streaming Gaussian backend, and row-streamed verification, these techniques bring Signing stack from 71 kB-141 kB in the reference implementation down to 5.8 kB-6.0 kB, key generation to 4.7 kB-5.7 kB, and verification to 4.7 kB-4.8 kB across all three security levels. Our pure C implementation covers all three security levels (HAETAE-2/3/5), whose optimization paths differ due to the public-key domain (d>0 vs. d=0) and rejection structure. We implement our optimization on a Nucleo-L4R5ZI and compare to the reference pqm4 (for HAETAE-2 and -3) and a recently published memory-optimized implementation (targeting HAETAE-5 only). We reduce HAETAE-2, -3, and -5 stack by respectively 75, 86 and 8 % for key generation, 92, 95 and 24 % for signature generation, and 85, 91 and 22 % for verification. Depending on the parameter set, this impacts performance by at most a factor 1.8 and 3.4 for key and signature generation respectively, while even offering a performance improvement up to 18 % for verification. Verification at all security levels fits within 8 kB of RAM (signature buffer + stack) and is 2.34-3.34x faster than ML-DSA m4fstack at each comparable security level. We additionally validate portability under RIOT-OS on ARM Cortex-M4 and RISC-V targets.

</details>


### [63] [A Case Study on the Impact of Anonymization Along the RAG Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15958)
*Andreea-Elena Bodea,Stephen Meisenbacher,Florian Matthes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在RAG系统中匿名化处理的位置选择问题，比较了在数据集层面和生成答案层面进行匿名化对隐私-效用权衡的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管检索增强生成(RAG)技术具有很大潜力，但在实际应用中可能引发隐私问题。虽然使用匿名化技术去除个人身份信息(PII)是RAG管理员的合理选择，但现有研究未考虑匿名化在RAG流程中的位置安排问题。

Method: 通过案例研究，系统且实证地测量了在RAG流程中两个关键点进行匿名化的影响：数据集层面和生成答案层面。

Result: 研究表明，根据匿名化发生的位置不同，可以观察到隐私-效用权衡的差异，这证明了隐私风险缓解措施在RAG中的位置安排具有重要意义。

Conclusion: 匿名化在RAG流程中的位置选择对隐私保护效果和系统效用有重要影响，需要根据具体应用场景权衡隐私风险缓解措施的部署位置。

Abstract: Despite the considerable promise of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), many real-world use cases may create privacy concerns, where the purported utility of RAG-enabled insights comes at the risk of exposing private information to either the LLM or the end user requesting the response. As a potential mitigation, using anonymization techniques to remove personally identifiable information (PII) and other sensitive markers in the underlying data represents a practical and sensible course of action for RAG administrators. Despite a wealth of literature on the topic, no works consider the placement of anonymization along the RAG pipeline, i.e., asking the question, where should anonymization happen? In this case study, we systematically and empirically measure the impact of anonymization at two important points along the RAG pipeline: the dataset and generated answer. We show that differences in privacy-utility trade-offs can be observed depending on where anonymization took place, demonstrating the significance of privacy risk mitigation placement in RAG.

</details>


### [64] [TwoHamsters: Benchmarking Multi-Concept Compositional Unsafety in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15967)
*Chaoshuo Zhang,Yibo Liang,Mengke Tian,Chenhao Lin,Zhengyu Zhao,Le Yang,Chong Zhang,Yang Zhang,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了多概念组合不安全（MCCU）这一新型漏洞，即单个概念安全但组合后产生不安全语义的问题，并创建了包含17.5k提示的TwoHamsters基准来评估现有模型和防御机制在此漏洞上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像（T2I）模型的安全对齐主要关注显性恶意概念，忽略了组合语义带来的微妙但关键的风险。作者发现当前安全机制对多概念组合不安全（MCCU）漏洞存在严重忽视，需要系统性地识别和评估这一新型安全威胁。

Method: 1. 形式化定义了多概念组合不安全（MCCU）漏洞，即单个概念安全但组合后产生不安全语义的问题；2. 创建了TwoHamsters基准，包含17.5k个精心策划的提示来探测MCCU漏洞；3. 对10个最先进的T2I模型和16种防御机制进行了严格评估。

Result: 评估揭示了8个关键发现：1. 当前T2I模型面临严重的MCCU风险，FLUX在TwoHamsters上的MCCU生成成功率高达99.52%；2. 现有防御机制效果有限，LLaVA-Guard仅达到41.06%的召回率；3. 当前管理危险组合生成的范式存在严重局限性。

Conclusion: 多概念组合不安全（MCCU）是T2I模型安全中的一个关键但被忽视的漏洞，现有模型和防御机制对此存在严重不足。TwoHamsters基准为评估和解决这一漏洞提供了重要工具，需要开发新的方法来有效管理组合语义带来的安全风险。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable synthesis capabilities of text-to-image (T2I) models, safeguarding them against content violations remains a persistent challenge. Existing safety alignments primarily focus on explicit malicious concepts, often overlooking the subtle yet critical risks of compositional semantics. To address this oversight, we identify and formalize a novel vulnerability: Multi-Concept Compositional Unsafety (MCCU), where unsafe semantics stem from the implicit associations of individually benign concepts. Based on this formulation, we introduce TwoHamsters, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 17.5k prompts curated to probe MCCU vulnerabilities. Through a rigorous evaluation of 10 state-of-the-art models and 16 defense mechanisms, our analysis yields 8 pivotal insights. In particular, we demonstrate that current T2I models and defense mechanisms face severe MCCU risks: on TwoHamsters, FLUX achieves an MCCU generation success rate of 99.52%, while LLaVA-Guard only attains a recall of 41.06%, highlighting a critical limitation of the current paradigm for managing hazardous compositional generation.

</details>


### [65] [Where Does MEV Really Come From? Revisiting CEXDEX Arbitrage on Ethereum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15973)
*Bence Ladóczk,Miklós Rásonyi,János Tapolcai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文通过改进AMM模型，证明CEX-DEX套利是MEV收入的主要来源，传统BS模型因忽略价格跳跃而低估了套利规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究以太坊生态中MEV收入的来源，特别是CEX-DEX套利的重要性。传统理论模型显著低估了这些套利的规模，而实证研究虽然指出了其重要性，但仍受限于各种启发式假设的限制。

Method: 提出了一个扩展的离散时间AMM模型，价格过程由扩散分量和具有任意噪声分布的随机跳跃组成。使用一般离散时间SDE，通过函数迭代计算平稳概率分布。证明错价过程是遍历马尔可夫链。用C++实现模型，从以太坊区块链收集现货价格和AMM交易数据，并将模型参数拟合到观测价格。

Result: CEX-DEX套利需要与主要流动性池总活动相当的交易量，产生的利润与MEV相当。模型估计与实证观察高度匹配，为区块链生态中的几个基本问题提供了自然理论解释。

Conclusion: 传统BS模型因忽略价格跳跃而低估了CEX-DEX套利利润。提出的扩展AMM模型能更准确地捕捉价格动态，证明CEX-DEX套利是MEV收入的主要来源，为理解区块链生态系统提供了更好的理论框架。

Abstract: A central question of the Ethereum ecosystem is where Maximal Extractable Value (MEV)revenue originates and to what extent it stems from harming unsuspecting users. It is acceptable if MEV arises from arbitrages between centralised and decentralised exchanges (CEX-DEX). Yet theoretical models have significantly underestimated the scale of these arbitrages, while empirical studies have highlighted their importance - though these remain conservative estimates, constrained by numerous debatable heuristic assumptions. Revisiting the theoretical model, we found that CEX-DEX arbitrages require trading volumes on the order of the total activity of major liquidity pools and yield profits comparable to MEV. Most prior AMM models utilised the Black-Scholes (BS) stochastic differential equation (SDE) - i.e., geometric Brownian motion - and assumed continuous price trajectories where asset prices move in small increments only.We argue that BS underestimates arbitrage profits by ignoring price jumps, which are precisely the points at which arbitrage opportunities tend to arise. To address this gap, we present an extended discrete-time AMM model in which the price process is the sum of a diffusive component and stochastic jumps that can have arbitrary noise distributions. Although mathematically more involved this framework allows us to employ a general discrete-time SDE and compute the stationary probability distribution via function iteration with geometric convergence. We further prove that the resulting mispricing process is an ergodic Markov chain. We implement our model in C++, collect spot prices and AMM exchange data from the Ethereum blockchain and fit the model parameters to the observed prices. The estimates derived from our model closely match empirical observations and provide a natural theoretical explanation for several fundamental questions in the blockchain ecosystem.

</details>


### [66] [MATRIX: Multi-Layer Code Watermarking via Dual-Channel Constrained Parity-Check Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16001)
*Yuqing Nie,Chong Wang,Guosheng Xu,Guoai Xu,Chenyu Wang,Haoyu Wang,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MATRIX是一个新颖的代码水印框架，通过约束奇偶校验矩阵方程进行水印编码，采用双通道水印（变量命名和语义保持变换）来提高覆盖范围和鲁棒性，结合BCH纠错码和解决方案空间多样性，在多个Code LLM生成的Python代码上实现了99.20%的平均检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码水印方法存在根本性局限：黑盒方法要么具有可检测的语法模式易受统计分析攻击，要么依赖隐式神经嵌入行为削弱了可解释性、可审计性和精确控制；白盒方法缺乏代码感知能力可能损害功能。当前单层水印方案无法满足日益复杂的来源追踪需求，如多级归属和版本跟踪。

Method: MATRIX将水印编码建模为求解约束奇偶校验矩阵方程。采用双通道水印机制：通过变量命名和语义保持变换实现水印嵌入，增强水印覆盖范围并确保相互备份的鲁棒性。结合BCH纠错码和解决方案空间多样性来提高对抗统计分析的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个Code LLM生成的Python代码上评估显示：平均水印检测准确率达到99.20%，功能损失极小（0-0.14%），对各种攻击的鲁棒性提高7.70-26.67%，水印适用性比现有方法提高2-6倍。

Conclusion: MATRIX为复杂代码来源追踪场景提供了有效解决方案，在可检测性、保真度和鲁棒性之间取得了良好平衡，解决了现有水印方法的关键局限性。

Abstract: Code Large Language Models (Code LLMs) have revolutionized software development but raised critical concerns regarding code provenance, copyright protection, and security. Existing code watermarking approaches suffer from two fundamental limitations: black-box methods either exhibit detectable syntactic patterns vulnerable to statistical analysis or rely on implicit neural embedding behaviors that weaken interpretability, auditability, and precise control, while white-box methods lack code-aware capabilities that may compromise functionality. Moreover, current single-layer watermarking schemes fail to address increasingly complex provenance requirements such as multi-level attribution and version tracking.
  We present MATRIX, a novel code watermarking framework that formulates watermark encoding as solving constrained parity-check matrix equations. MATRIX employs dual-channel watermarking through variable naming and semantic-preserving transformations, enhancing watermark coverage across a wider range of code while ensuring mutual backup for robustness. By integrating BCH error-correction codes with solution space diversity, our approach achieves robustness against statistical analysis. Extensive evaluation on Python code generated by multiple Code LLMs demonstrates that MATRIX achieves an average watermark detection accuracy of 99.20% with minimal functionality loss (0-0.14%), improves robustness by 7.70-26.67% against various attacks, and increases watermarking applicability by 2-6x compared with existing methods. These results establish MATRIX as an effective solution for complex code provenance scenarios while balancing among detectability, fidelity, and robustness.

</details>


### [67] [ProcRoute: Process-Scoped Authorization of Split-Tunnel Routes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16080)
*Arul Thileeban Sagayam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ProcRoute是一个系统，通过将路由访问建模为访问控制问题，限制内部路由只能被明确授权的应用程序使用，防止恶意进程滥用VPN/ZTNA中的内部路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大多数分割隧道VPN/ZTNA部署中，安装内部路由会授权整个设备而非特定应用程序使用该路由。这使得非特权恶意进程能够通过重用企业应用程序的路由访问内部服务，存在安全风险。

Method: ProcRoute将路由访问建模为访问控制问题：应用程序身份作为主体，带端口和协议约束的目标前缀作为资源，通过默认拒绝的决策函数来仲裁每个到内部目标的connect()和UDP sendmsg()调用。系统基于Linux cgroup v2和eBPF套接字地址钩子构建原型。

Result: 在双机WireGuard部署中，ProcRoute性能与WireGuard基线相当，比nftables cgroup匹配配置快13%，p50连接延迟为93μs（比基线增加3.6μs）。策略可扩展到5000个前缀，撤销时间低于毫秒。单机环回微基准测试显示内部允许路径开销为2.7μs，成功阻止82/82次未经授权的pivot尝试，策略重载期间120万次连接尝试中零次瞬时允许。

Conclusion: ProcRoute提供了一种有效的机制，通过应用程序级别的路由访问控制来增强分割隧道VPN/ZTNA的安全性，防止恶意进程滥用内部路由，同时保持低开销和良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: In most split-tunnel VPN/ZTNA deployments, installing an internal route authorizes the entire device, not a specific application, to use it. An unprivileged malicious process can therefore reach internal services by reusing routes intended for corporate applications. We present ProcRoute, a system that restricts internal-route access to explicitly authorized applications. ProcRoute models route access as an access-control problem: application identities are principals, destination prefixes with port and protocol constraints are resources, and a total, default-deny decision function mediates every connect() and UDP sendmsg() to an internal destination. Processes without a grant retain external access but are denied internal routes under our threat model. We describe ProcRoute's formal model, a Linux prototype built on cgroup v2 and eBPF socket-address hooks, and two complementary evaluations. In a two-machine WireGuard deployment, ProcRoute matches the WireGuard baseline and 13% faster than an nftables cgroup-matching configuration, with a p50 connect latency of 93 $μ$s (+3.6 $μ$s over baseline), flat policy scaling to 5,000 prefixes, and sub-millisecond revocation. Single-machine loopback microbenchmarks confirm low hook overhead: 2.7 $μ$s on the internal-allow path, 82/82 unauthorized pivot attempts blocked, and zero transient allows across 1.2 million connection attempts during policy reload.

</details>


### [68] [PolicyGapper: Automated Detection of Inconsistencies Between Google Play Data Safety Sections and Privacy Policies Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16128)
*Luca Ferrari,Billel Habbati,Meriem Guerar,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PolicyGapper：基于LLM的自动化工具，用于检测Google Play数据安全部分与隐私政策之间的不一致性，在330个热门应用中发现2689个遗漏披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Google Play要求开发者提供数据安全部分披露数据实践，但缺乏自动化工具验证这些披露与隐私政策的一致性，且研究表明近80%热门应用存在不完整或误导性声明。

Method: 提出四阶段LLM方法：爬取DSS和隐私政策、预处理、基于LLM的分析、后处理，无需访问应用二进制文件。在2025年第三季度收集的330个跨33类热门应用上进行评估。

Result: 识别出2689个遗漏披露（2040个数据收集相关，649个数据共享相关）。在10%分层子集上进行人工验证，三次独立运行平均精确率0.75、召回率0.77、准确率0.69、F1分数0.76。

Conclusion: PolicyGapper能有效自动检测DSS与隐私政策间的不一致性，为应用商店监管和开发者合规提供工具支持。已发布完整复制包促进可重复性。

Abstract: Mobile application developers are required to disclose how they collect, use, and share user data in compliance with privacy regulations. To support transparency, major app marketplaces have introduced standardized disclosure mechanisms. In 2022, Google mandated the Data Safety Section (DSS) on Google Play, requiring developers to summarize their data practices. However, compiling accurate DSS disclosures is challenging, as they must remain consistent with the corresponding privacy policy (PP), and no automated tool currently verifies this alignment. Prior studies indicate that nearly 80% of popular apps contain incomplete or misleading DSS declarations. We present PolicyGapper, an LLM-based methodology for automatically detecting discrepancies between DSS disclosures and privacy policies. PolicyGapper operates in four stages: scraping, pre-processing, analysis, and post-processing, without requiring access to application binaries. We evaluate PolicyGapper on a dataset of 330 top-ranked apps spanning all 33 Google Play categories, collected in Q3 2025. The approach identifies 2,689 omitted disclosures, including 2,040 related to data collection and 649 to data sharing. Manual validation on a stratified 10% subset, repeated across three independent runs, yields an average Precision of 0.75, Recall of 0.77, Accuracy of 0.69, and F1-score of 0.76. To support reproducibility, we release a complete replication package, including the dataset, prompts, source code, and results available at https://github.com/Mobile-IoT-Security-Lab/PolicyGapper and https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19628493.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [69] [InfoChess: A Game of Adversarial Inference and a Laboratory for Quantifiable Information Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15373)
*Kieran A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: InfoChess是一个对称对抗游戏，将竞争性信息获取作为主要目标，通过棋子改变可见性而非捕获，玩家根据对对手国王位置的推理概率得分


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一个专门研究多智能体在部分可观测环境下推理的测试平台，消除传统棋类中棋子捕获带来的物质激励混淆，专注于信息获取竞争

Method: 1) 设计InfoChess游戏规则：无棋子捕获，棋子用于改变可见性，基于对手国王位置推理概率得分；2) 构建启发式智能体层次结构；3) 训练强化学习智能体；4) 使用信息论指标分析游戏：信念熵、oracle交叉熵、动作诱导观察通道下的预测对数得分

Result: 强化学习智能体优于启发式基线，信息论指标能够解耦认知不确定性、校准不匹配和对抗移动引起的不确定性，为研究部分可观测下的多智能体推理提供了有效测试平台

Conclusion: InfoChess成功创建了一个专门研究竞争性信息获取的对称对抗游戏环境，其信息论分析框架能够深入理解多智能体推理过程，为后续研究提供了有价值的工具和基准

Abstract: We propose InfoChess, a symmetric adversarial game that elevates competitive information acquisition to the primary objective. There is no piece capture, removing material incentives that would otherwise confound the role of information. Instead, pieces are used to alter visibility. Players are scored on their probabilistic inference of the opponent's king location over the duration of the game. To explore the space of strategies for playing InfoChess, we introduce a hierarchy of heuristic agents defined by increasing levels of opponent modeling, and train a reinforcement learning agent that outperforms these baselines. Leveraging the discrete structure of the game, we analyze gameplay through natural information-theoretic characterizations that include belief entropy, oracle cross entropy, and predictive log score under the action-induced observation channel. These measures disentangle epistemic uncertainty, calibration mismatch, and uncertainty induced by adversarial movement. The design of InfoChess renders it a testbed for studying multi-agent inference under partial observability. We release code for the environment and agents, and a public interface to encourage further study.

</details>


### [70] [Scalable Algorithms with Provable Optimality Bounds for the Multiple Watchman Route Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15610)
*Srikar Gouru,Ariel Felner,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出针对多看守员路径问题（MWRP）的优化算法，包括状态空间缩减方法、最优规划器MWRP-CP3、次优算法及其变体，显著提升求解效率和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多看守员路径问题（MWRP）需要为M个看守员规划路径，确保地图上每个位置至少被一个看守员看到。现有方法在状态空间搜索效率和可扩展性方面存在局限，需要更高效的算法来处理更大规模的地图。

Method: 1. 提出多种状态空间缩减方法，通过剪枝保证在前往其他区域途中必然被看到的区域；2. 开发最优规划器MWRP-CP3，结合状态空间缩减和改进的启发式方法；3. 提出具有解质量保证的次优算法，包括适用于完工时间问题的加权A*变体MxWA*；4. 开发次优算法的随时变体；5. 提出通过求解多个分解子问题改进现有次优解的技术。

Result: 1. MWRP-CP3能将搜索空间减少95%以上，在2D网格地图上比现有最优算法快200倍以上；2. 次优算法能求解比MWRP-CP3可处理地图大3倍的地图；3. 提供了开源代码库和视频演示。

Conclusion: 该研究为多看守员路径问题提供了高效的最优和次优求解方法，显著提升了搜索效率、计算速度和问题规模的可扩展性，为实际应用提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we tackle the Multiple Watchman Route Problem (MWRP), which aims to find a set of paths that M watchmen can follow such that every location on the map can be seen by at least one watchman. First, we propose multiple methods to reduce the state space over which a search needs to be conducted by pruning map areas that are guaranteed to be seen en route to other areas. Next, we introduce MWRP-CP3, an efficient optimal planner that combines these methods with techniques that improve the quality and calculation time of existing heuristics. We present several suboptimal algorithms with bounds on solution quality, including MxWA*, a general variant of weighted A* for makespan problems. We also present anytime variations of our suboptimal algorithms, as well as techniques to improve an existing suboptimal solution by solving multiple decomposed sub-problems. We show that MWRP-CP3 can reduce the search space by more than 95% and runs more than 200x faster than existing optimal algorithms on 2D grid maps. We also show that our suboptimal algorithms solve maps 3x larger than those solvable by MWRP-CP3. See mwrp-cp3.github.io for the open source codebase and video demonstrations.

</details>


### [71] [AstroVLM: Expert Multi-agent Collaborative Reasoning for Astronomical Imaging Quality Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16024)
*Yaohui Han,Tianshuo Wang,Zixi Zhao,Zhengchun Zhu,Shuo Ren,Yiru Wang,Rongliang Fu,Tinghuan Chen,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: AstroVLM：一个用于天文图像质量诊断的协作多智能体系统，基于视觉语言模型解决复杂多过程任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文成像是一个涉及多学科知识和多个子任务的复杂问题，现有视觉语言模型在这一领域研究不足。天文成像过程复杂，各环节之间存在强相关性，导致图像质量诊断和错误定位极具挑战性，需要大量专家时间和精力。

Method: 提出AstroVLM，一个协作多智能体系统，专门用于天文图像质量诊断。该系统基于视觉语言模型构建，通过多个智能体协作来处理复杂的多过程任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，AstroVLM在真实世界天文成像质量诊断任务中优于所有基线方法，为语言模型处理复杂多过程任务提供了参考。

Conclusion: AstroVLM成功解决了天文图像质量诊断的复杂问题，展示了视觉语言模型在复杂多过程任务中的应用潜力，为类似复杂领域的问题解决提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have been applied to several specific domains and have shown strong problem-solving capabilities. However, astronomical imaging, a quite complex problem involving multidisciplinary knowledge and several subtasks, has not been adequately studied. Due to the complexity of the astronomical imaging process, both world-class astronomical organizations, such as NASA, and expert enthusiasts devote a great deal of time and effort. This is because the processes in astronomical imaging have complex underlying correlations that significantly influence one another, making the quality diagnosis and error localization of astronomical images challenging. To address this problem, we propose AstroVLM, a collaborative multi-agent system for diagnosing the quality of astronomical images. Experiment results show that AstroVLM outperforms all baselines on real-world astronomical imaging quality diagnosis tasks, providing a reference for language models to handle complicated multi-process tasks.

</details>


### [72] [Veritas-RPM: Provenance-Guided Multi-Agent False Positive Suppression for Remote Patient Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16081)
*Aswini Misro,Vikash Sharma,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Veritas-RPM是一个包含五个处理层的溯源引导多智能体架构，用于远程患者监测中的假阳性场景检测，在530个合成患者时段上评估性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决远程患者监测(RPM)中假阳性警报过多的问题，通过多智能体架构提高警报验证的准确性和效率

Method: 构建包含五个处理层的多智能体架构：VeritasAgent（真实情况组装）、SentinelLayer（异常检测）、DirectorAgent（专家路由）、六个领域专家智能体、MetaSentinelAgent（冲突解决和最终决策）；基于98个假阳性场景的合成分类法生成530个合成患者时段进行评估

Result: 使用真实抑制率(TSR)、假升级率(FER)和不确定率(INDR)三个指标报告性能，所有案例都有已知的真实标签

Conclusion: Veritas-RPM架构通过溯源引导的多智能体方法，为远程患者监测中的假阳性检测提供了一种系统化的解决方案

Abstract: We present Veritas-RPM, a provenance-guided multi-agent architecture comprising five processing layers: VeritasAgent (ground-truth assembly), SentinelLayer (anomaly detection), DirectorAgent (specialist routing), six domain Specialist Agents, and MetaSentinelAgent (conflict resolution and final decision). We construct a 98-case synthetic taxonomy of false-positive scenarios derived from documented RPM patterns. Synthetic patient epochs (n = 530) were generated directly from taxonomy parameters and processed through the pipeline. Ground-truth labels are known for all cases. Performance is reported as True Suppression Rate (TSR), False Escalation Rate (FER), and Indeterminate Rate (INDR).

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [73] [Iterated Invariant EKF for Quadruped Robot Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15449)
*Hilton Marques Souza Santana,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Sven Goffin,Ylenia Nisticò,Silvère Bonnabel,Claudio Semini,Marco Antonio Meggiolaro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于迭代不变扩展卡尔曼滤波(IterIEKF)的开源腿式机器人状态估计算法，利用足部接触约束和本体感知测量，在精度和一致性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卡尔曼滤波在机器人状态估计中依赖线性系统和高斯噪声假设，难以在实际非线性系统中应用。虽然IEKF解决了系统动力学的非线性问题，但测量模型的非线性处理仍有待改进。本文旨在将IterIEKF应用于腿式机器人状态估计，提升在复杂环境下的性能。

Method: 提出基于IterIEKF的开源状态估计算法，仅使用本体感知测量，利用足部接触时的速度约束和基座速度信息。算法通过迭代更新步骤处理非线性测量模型，继承了经典卡尔曼滤波的兼容性特性。

Result: 通过大量数值仿真和真实数据集评估，IterIEKF在精度和一致性方面均优于原始IEKF、基于SO(3)的卡尔曼滤波及其迭代变体。算法对环境条件具有固有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于IterIEKF的腿式机器人状态估计算法有效解决了非线性测量模型问题，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了状态估计性能，为实际应用提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Kalman filter-based algorithms are fundamental for mobile robots, as they provide a computationally efficient solution to the challenging problem of state estimation. However, they rely on two main assumptions that are difficult to satisfy in practice: (a) the system dynamics must be linear with Gaussian process noise, and (b) the measurement model must also be linear with Gaussian measurement noise. Previous works have extended assumption (a) to nonlinear spaces through the Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (IEKF), showing that it retains properties similar to those of the classical Kalman filter when the system dynamics are group-affine on a Lie group. More recently, the counterpart of assumption (b) for the same nonlinear setting was addressed in [1]. By means of the proposed Iterated Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (IterIEKF), the authors of that work demonstrated that the update step exhibits several compatibility properties of the classical linear Kalman filter. In this work, we introduce a novel open-source state estimation algorithm for legged robots based on the IterIEKF. The update step of the proposed filter relies solely on proprioceptive measurements, exploiting kinematic constraints on foot velocity during contact and base-frame velocity, making it inherently robust to environmental conditions. Through extensive numerical simulations and evaluation on real-world datasets, we demonstrate that the IterIEKF outperforms the vanilla IEKF, the SO(3)-based Kalman Filter, and its iterated variant in terms of both accuracy and consistency.

</details>


### [74] [Contact-Aware Planning and Control of Continuum Robots in Highly Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15638)
*Aedan Mangan,Kehan Long,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Miheer Potdar,Nikolay Atanasov,Tania K. Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种接触感知的连续体机器人路径规划方法，能够在受限解剖环境中区分有益和有害接触，实现安全导航


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人在血管或腔内解剖等受限环境中导航时，与周围结构的接触不可避免。有益的接触可辅助运动，但有害的接触会降低可控性、引发运动学奇点或带来安全风险，因此需要区分接触质量并规划安全轨迹

Method: 开发接触感知规划方法，评估接触质量，惩罚危险交互同时允许良性接触；生成运动学可行轨迹和接触感知雅可比矩阵，用于硬件闭环控制；在患者扫描的解剖模型上验证集成系统

Result: 在三种常见解剖环境中均生成有效规划；硬件实验中连续体机器人100%成功到达目标并避免危险尖端接触；平均跟踪误差分别为1.9±0.5mm、1.2±0.1mm和1.7±0.2mm；消融研究表明惩罚末端段接触提高了可操作性并防止硬件故障

Conclusion: 该方法实现了在高度受限环境中的可靠、接触感知导航，通过区分接触质量并规划安全轨迹，确保连续体机器人在解剖环境中的安全操作

Abstract: Continuum robots are well suited for navigating confined and fragile environments, such as vascular or endoluminal anatomy, where contact with surrounding structures is often unavoidable. While controlled contact can assist motion, unfavorable contact can degrade controllability, induce kinematic singularities, or introduce safety risks. We present a contact-aware planning approach that evaluates contact quality, penalizing hazardous interactions, while permitting benign contact. The planner produces kinematically feasible trajectories and contact-aware Jacobians which can be used for closed-loop control in hardware experiments. We validate the approach by testing the integrated system (planning, control, and mechanical design) on anatomical models from patient scans. The planner generates effective plans for three common anatomical environments, and, in all hardware trials, the continuum robot was able to reach the target while avoiding dangerous tip contact (100% success). Mean tracking errors were 1.9 +/- 0.5 mm, 1.2 +/- 0.1 mm, and 1.7 +/- 0.2 mm across the three different environments. Ablation studies showed that penalizing end-of-continuum-segment (ECS) contact improved manipulability and prevented hardware failures. Overall, this work enables reliable, contact-aware navigation in highly constrained environments.

</details>


### [75] [One-Shot Cross-Geometry Skill Transfer through Part Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15455)
*Skye Thompson,Ondrej Biza,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于语义部件分解的技能迁移方法，通过生成式形状模型将交互点从演示对象部件转移到新对象，实现单次演示即可适应多种几何形状的技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能迁移方法在处理形状陌生的物体时往往失败，而组合建模方法显示出更好的迁移效果，因此希望通过分解物体为语义部件来改进技能迁移能力。

Method: 利用数据高效的生成式形状模型，将交互点从演示对象的部件准确转移到新对象；自主构建优化目标，对齐技能相关物体部件上的这些点。

Result: 该方法比现有工作能泛化到更广泛的物体几何形状，在模拟和真实环境中，通过单次演示即可成功迁移多种技能和物体。

Conclusion: 基于语义部件分解的方法显著提升了机器人技能迁移的泛化能力，能够适应形状陌生的物体，实现高效的单次演示技能迁移。

Abstract: Given a demonstration, a robot should be able to generalize a skill to any object it encounters-but existing approaches to skill transfer often fail to adapt to objects with unfamiliar shapes. Motivated by examples of improved transfer from compositional modeling, we propose a method for improving transfer by decomposing objects into their constituent semantic parts. We leverage data-efficient generative shape models to accurately transfer interaction points from the parts of a demonstration object to a novel object. We autonomously construct an objective to optimize the alignment of those points on skill-relevant object parts. Our method generalizes to a wider range of object geometries than existing work, and achieves successful one-shot transfer for a range of skills and objects from a single demonstration, in both simulated and real environments.

</details>


### [76] [NeuroMesh: A Unified Neural Inference Framework for Decentralized Multi-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15475)
*Yang Zhou,Yash Shetye,Long Quang,Devon Super,Jesse Milzman,Manohari Goarin,Aditya Azad,Devang Sunil Dhake,Jeffery Mao,Carlos Nieto-Granda,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeuroMesh：面向异构机器人的多域跨平台模块化去中心化神经推理框架，统一了观测编码、消息传递、聚合和任务解码流程，支持混合GPU/CPU推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于硬件异构性、通信约束和缺乏统一执行栈，将学习到的多机器人模型部署在异构机器人上仍然具有挑战性

Method: 提出NeuroMesh框架，采用双聚合范式（基于归约和广播的信息融合）和并行化架构（解耦周期时间与端到端延迟），基于C++高性能实现，使用Zenoh进行机器人间通信，支持混合GPU/CPU推理

Result: 在包含空中和地面机器人的异构团队上验证了NeuroMesh在协作感知、去中心化控制和任务分配等任务中的鲁棒性，能够适应不同任务结构和有效载荷大小

Conclusion: NeuroMesh为异构多机器人系统提供了标准化、高性能的神经推理框架，计划开源以促进社区发展

Abstract: Deploying learned multi-robot models on heterogeneous robots remains challenging due to hardware heterogeneity, communication constraints, and the lack of a unified execution stack. This paper presents NeuroMesh, a multi-domain, cross-platform, and modular decentralized neural inference framework that standardizes observation encoding, message passing, aggregation, and task decoding in a unified pipeline. NeuroMesh combines a dual-aggregation paradigm for reduction- and broadcast-based information fusion with a parallelized architecture that decouples cycle time from end-to-end latency. Our high-performance C++ implementation leverages Zenoh for inter-robot communication and supports hybrid GPU/CPU inference. We validate NeuroMesh on a heterogeneous team of aerial and ground robots across collaborative perception, decentralized control, and task assignment, demonstrating robust operation across diverse task structures and payload sizes. We plan to release NeuroMesh as an open-source framework to the community.

</details>


### [77] [Trajectory Planning for Safe Dual Control with Active Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15507)
*Kaleb Ben Naveed,Manveer Singh,Devansh R. Agrawal,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dual-gatekeeper框架：在安全约束和任务成本预算下，集成鲁棒规划与主动探索，仅在可验证改善时进行探索，平衡即时任务性能与长期不确定性降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在模型不确定性下的轨迹规划存在局限性：鲁棒规划过于保守，忽略不确定性降低；而主动探索方法通常通过加权探索项来权衡，但缺乏对探索何时有益的正式考虑，且安全保证不完善。

Method: 提出Dual-gatekeeper框架，将鲁棒规划与主动探索集成，在安全约束和任务成本预算下进行决策。框架核心思想是：仅当探索能提供可验证的改善且不损害安全或违反预算时才进行探索。提供了基于不同安全机制的两种实现。

Result: 在四旋翼导航和自动驾驶赛车案例研究中验证了框架性能，展示了在参数不确定性下平衡任务性能与不确定性降低的能力。

Conclusion: Dual-gatekeeper框架为双控制问题提供了原则性解决方案，在保证安全和预算可行性的前提下，实现了即时任务性能与长期不确定性降低的平衡。

Abstract: Planning safe trajectories under model uncertainty is a fundamental challenge. Robust planning ensures safety by considering worst-case realizations, yet ignores uncertainty reduction and leads to overly conservative behavior. Actively reducing uncertainty on-the-fly during a nominal mission defines the dual control problem. Most approaches address this by adding a weighted exploration term to the cost, tuned to trade off the nominal objective and uncertainty reduction, but without formal consideration of when exploration is beneficial. Moreover, safety is enforced in some methods but not in others. We study a budget-constrained dual control problem, where uncertainty is reduced subject to safety and a mission-level cost budget that limits the allowable degradation in task performance due to exploration. In this work, we propose Dual-gatekeeper, a framework that integrates robust planning with active exploration under formal guarantees of safety and budget feasibility. The key idea is that exploration is pursued only when it provides a verifiable improvement without compromising safety or violating the budget, enabling the system to balance immediate task performance with long-term uncertainty reduction in a principled manner. We provide two implementations of the framework based on different safety mechanisms and demonstrate its performance on quadrotor navigation and autonomous car racing case studies under parametric uncertainty.

</details>


### [78] [ShapeGen: Robotic Data Generation for Category-Level Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15569)
*Yirui Wang,Xiuwei Xu,Angyuan Ma,Bingyao Yu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ShapeGen是一种免模拟器、3D方式生成形状多样化操作数据的方法，通过形状库构建和功能感知生成两阶段提升策略的类别内形状泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中部署的操作策略面临日常物体在类别内几何多样性带来的挑战，需要类别级泛化能力。传统方法依赖形状多样化的训练数据，但手动收集此类数据因需要大量人工劳动和多样物体集合而不可行。

Method: ShapeGen采用两阶段方法：1) 形状库构建阶段：训练形状间的空间扭曲映射，将功能对应的点映射到点，并将3D模型与扭曲映射聚合到即插即用的形状库中；2) 功能感知生成阶段：设计一个利用已有库的流程，仅需最少人工标注即可生成物理合理且功能正确的新演示数据。

Result: 真实世界实验证明ShapeGen能有效提升策略的类别内形状泛化能力。

Conclusion: ShapeGen提供了一种免模拟器、3D方式生成形状多样化操作数据的方法，解决了手动收集多样化训练数据的可行性问题，显著提升了操作策略在现实世界中的类别级泛化能力。

Abstract: Manipulation policies deployed in uncontrolled real-world scenarios are faced with great in-category geometric diversity of everyday objects. In order to function robustly under such variations, policies need to work in a category-level manner, i.e. knowing how to interact with any object in a certain category, instead of only a specific one seen during training.
