This in-category generalizability is usually nurtured with shape-diversified training data; however, manually collecting such a corpus of data is infeasible due to the requirement of intense human labor and large collections of divergent objects at hand. In this paper, we propose ShapeGen, a data generation method that aims at generating shape-variated manipulation data in a simulator-free and 3D manner. ShapeGen decomposes the process into two stages: Shape Library curation and Function-Aware Generation. In the first stage, we train spatial warpings between shapes mapping points to points that correspond functionally, and aggregate 3D models along with the warpings into a plug-and-play Shape Library. In the second stage, we design a pipeline that, leveraging established Libraries, requires only minimal human annotation to generate physically plausible and functionally correct novel demonstrations. Experiments in the real world demonstrate the effectiveness of ShapeGen to boost policies' in-category shape generalizability. Project page: https://wangyr22.github.io/ShapeGen/.

</details>


### [79] [GaussianFlow SLAM: Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM Guided by GaussianFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15612)
*Dong-Uk Seo,Jinwoo Jeon,Eungchang Mason Lee,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianFlow SLAM：一种利用光流作为几何感知线索的单目3D高斯溅射SLAM系统，通过高斯投影运动与光流对齐来正则化场景重建和位姿估计，提升渲染质量和跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目SLAM中应用高斯溅射技术面临几何线索不足的挑战，缺乏几何监督会导致映射和跟踪陷入局部最小值，产生结构退化和不准确问题。

Method: 提出GaussianFlow SLAM，利用光流作为几何感知线索指导场景结构和相机位姿优化；引入高斯流概念（高斯投影运动），使其与光流对齐；采用基于归一化误差的致密化和剪枝模块来优化非活跃和不稳定的高斯。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法在渲染质量和跟踪精度方面优于现有最先进算法。

Conclusion: 通过光流作为几何正则化线索，GaussianFlow SLAM成功解决了单目高斯溅射SLAM中的几何监督不足问题，实现了高质量的场景重建和准确的相机跟踪。

Abstract: Gaussian splatting has recently gained traction as a compelling map representation for SLAM systems, enabling dense and photo-realistic scene modeling. However, its application to monocular SLAM remains challenging due to the lack of reliable geometric cues from monocular input. Without geometric supervision, mapping or tracking could fall in local-minima, resulting in structural degeneracies and inaccuracies. To address this challenge, we propose GaussianFlow SLAM, a monocular 3DGS-SLAM that leverages optical flow as a geometry-aware cue to guide the optimization of both the scene structure and camera poses. By encouraging the projected motion of Gaussians, termed GaussianFlow, to align with the optical flow, our method introduces consistent structural cues to regularize both map reconstruction and pose estimation. Furthermore, we introduce normalized error-based densification and pruning modules to refine inactive and unstable Gaussians, thereby contributing to improved map quality and pose accuracy. Experiments conducted on public datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior rendering quality and tracking accuracy compared with state-of-the-art algorithms. The source code is available at: https://github.com/url-kaist/gaussianflow-slam.

</details>


### [80] [Factor Graph-Based Shape Estimation for Continuum Robots via Magnus Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15619)
*Lorenzo Ticozzi,Patricio A. Vela,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合低维几何变量应变参数化和因子图推理的新方法，用于从稀疏噪声传感器数据重建连续体机械臂形状，实现紧凑状态表示和概率不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在参数化方法（紧凑但缺乏概率结构）和Cosserat杆因子图推理（提供不确定性量化但状态维度随空间离散化增长）之间存在权衡，需要结合两者优势。

Method: 在因子图框架内估计低维几何变量应变参数化的系数，使用基于应变场Magnus展开的新型运动学因子，将GVS应变系数与骨干位姿变量连接作为先验约束。

Result: 在0.4米长肌腱驱动连续体机器人仿真中，三种测量配置下平均位置误差均低于2毫米，当仅有位置测量时，与高斯过程回归基线相比方向误差减少六倍。

Conclusion: 该方法结合了参数化方法的紧凑状态表示和因子图推理的概率处理优势，实现了可直接用于基于模型控制的紧凑状态向量，同时保持了模块化、概率处理和计算效率。

Abstract: Reconstructing the shape of continuum manipulators from sparse, noisy sensor data is a challenging task, owing to the infinite-dimensional nature of such systems. Existing approaches broadly trade off between parametric methods that yield compact state representations but lack probabilistic structure, and Cosserat rod inference on factor graphs, which provides principled uncertainty quantification at the cost of a state dimension that grows with the spatial discretization. This letter combines the strength of both paradigms by estimating the coefficients of a low-dimensional Geometric Variable Strain (GVS) parameterization within a factor graph framework. A novel kinematic factor, derived from the Magnus expansion of the strain field, encodes the closed-form rod geometry as a prior constraint linking the GVS strain coefficients to the backbone pose variables. The resulting formulation yields a compact state vector directly amenable to model-based control, while retaining the modularity, probabilistic treatment and computational efficiency of factor graph inference. The proposed method is evaluated in simulation on a 0.4 m long tendon-driven continuum robot under three measurement configurations, achieving mean position errors below 2 mm for all three scenarios and demonstrating a sixfold reduction in orientation error compared to a Gaussian process regression baseline when only position measurements are available.

</details>


### [81] [Fuzzy Logic Theory-based Adaptive Reward Shaping for Robust Reinforcement Learning (FARS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15772)
*Hürkan Şahin,Van Huyen Dang,Erdi Sayar,Alper Yegenoglu,Erdal Kayacan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模糊逻辑的奖励塑形方法，将人类直觉融入强化学习奖励设计，通过模糊规则编码专家知识，在自主无人机竞速任务中实现更稳定的学习和更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在处理高维状态空间和长时域任务时面临探索困难，稀疏或固定奖励导致学习缓慢并陷入局部最优。需要将人类专家直觉融入奖励设计以改善学习效率和稳定性。

Method: 提出基于模糊逻辑的奖励塑形方法，将专家知识编码为自适应且可解释的模糊规则。这些规则根据智能体状态动态调整奖励贡献，实现快速运动与精确控制之间的平滑过渡。

Result: 在自主无人机竞速基准测试中，该方法展现出稳定的学习行为和一致的任务性能。在更具挑战性的环境中，相比非模糊奖励公式，收敛速度更快，训练种子间的性能变异性降低，成功率提升约5%。

Conclusion: 模糊逻辑奖励塑形通过将人类直觉融入强化学习奖励设计，有效解决了高维状态空间和长时域任务中的探索困难，实现了更稳定、更高效的学习性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) often struggles in real-world tasks with high-dimensional state spaces and long horizons, where sparse or fixed rewards severely slow down exploration and cause agents to get trapped in local optima. This paper presents a fuzzy logic based reward shaping method that integrates human intuition into RL reward design. By encoding expert knowledge into adaptive and interpreable terms, fuzzy rules promote stable learning and reduce sensitivity to hyperparameters. The proposed method leverages these properties to adapt reward contributions based on the agent state, enabling smoother transitions between fast motion and precise control in challenging navigation tasks. Extensive simulation results on autonomous drone racing benchmarks show stable learning behavior and consistent task performance across scenarios of increasing difficulty. The proposed method achieves faster convergence and reduced performance variability across training seeds in more challenging environments, with success rates improving by up to approximately 5 percent compared to non fuzzy reward formulations.

</details>


### [82] [From Seeing to Simulating: Generative High-Fidelity Simulation with Digital Cousins for Generalizable Robot Learning and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15805)
*Jasper Lu,Zhenhao Shen,Yuanfei Wang,Shugao Liu,Shengqiang Xu,Shawn Xie,Jingkai Xu,Feng Jiang,Jade Yang,Chen Xie,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出生成式框架建立真实全景图到高保真仿真场景的映射，通过语义和几何编辑合成多样化场景，支持交互式操作任务和大规模环境构建，用于可泛化的机器人学习与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界机器人策略学习需要多样化数据增强，但真实数据收集成本高昂（需要物理资产和环境重配置）。通过将真实场景增强到仿真中成为高效学习和评估的实用方法。

Method: 1) 建立生成式真实到仿真映射框架，从真实全景图生成高保真仿真场景；2) 通过语义和几何编辑合成多样化"表亲场景"；3) 结合高质量物理引擎和真实资产支持交互式操作任务；4) 采用多房间拼接构建一致的大规模环境，支持复杂布局的长时程导航。

Result: 实验显示：1) 仿真到真实的强相关性验证了平台保真度；2) 大规模数据生成显著提高了对未见场景和物体变化的泛化能力；3) 证明了Digital Cousins方法对可泛化机器人学习和评估的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的生成式框架能够高效创建多样化仿真场景，支持机器人策略学习，通过大规模数据生成显著提升泛化能力，为可泛化的机器人学习和评估提供了有效平台。

Abstract: Learning robust robot policies in real-world environments requires diverse data augmentation, yet scaling real-world data collection is costly due to the need for acquiring physical assets and reconfiguring environments. Therefore, augmenting real-world scenes into simulation has become a practical augmentation for efficient learning and evaluation. We present a generative framework that establishes a generative real-to-sim mapping from real-world panoramas to high-fidelity simulation scenes, and further synthesize diverse cousin scenes via semantic and geometric editing. Combined with high-quality physics engines and realistic assets, the generated scenes support interactive manipulation tasks. Additionally, we incorporate multi-room stitching to construct consistent large-scale environments for long-horizon navigation across complex layouts. Experiments demonstrate a strong sim-to-real correlation validating our platform's fidelity, and show that extensively scaling up data generation leads to significantly better generalization to unseen scene and object variations, demonstrating the effectiveness of Digital Cousins for generalizable robot learning and evaluation.

</details>


### [83] [Limits of Lamarckian Evolution Under Pressure of Morphological Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15854)
*Jed R Muff,Karine Miras,A. E. Eiben*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 拉马克遗传在机器人形态-控制器协同进化中加速优化，但其优势依赖于亲子形态相似性；当进化压力转向形态多样性时，拉马克系统的性能下降比达尔文系统更显著，揭示了基于遗传的利用与多样性驱动的探索之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拉马克遗传在机器人形态-控制器协同进化中的局限性。虽然拉马克遗传（子代继承父母学习到的控制器）已被证明能加速优化过程，但其有效性依赖于亲子形态相似性。本研究旨在探究当进化过程被驱动向高形态多样性时，拉马克遗传的表现如何，特别是与达尔文进化相比。

Method: 使用模块化机器人系统，研究机器人形态和控制器的协同进化与学习。比较达尔文进化（仅遗传先天控制器）和拉马克进化（遗传学习后的控制器）在两种选择压力下的表现：1）纯任务性能优化（运动任务）；2）多目标压力（同时奖励形态新颖性）。分析亲子形态相似性与性能之间的关系。

Result: 1）当仅优化任务性能时，拉马克进化优于达尔文进化；2）引入形态多样性选择压力后，两种系统的性能都下降，但拉马克系统的下降幅度更大；3）促进多样性会降低亲子相似性，从而削弱继承父母学习控制器的益处；4）揭示了拉马克遗传的局限性：基于遗传的利用与多样性驱动的探索之间存在根本性权衡。

Conclusion: 拉马克遗传在机器人形态-控制器协同进化中的优势高度依赖于亲子形态相似性。当进化压力转向促进形态多样性时，亲子相似性降低，导致继承学习控制器的益处减少，拉马克系统的性能下降比达尔文系统更显著。这揭示了进化算法设计中利用与探索之间的基本权衡，为未来机器人进化系统的设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Lamarckian inheritance has been shown to be a powerful accelerator in systems where the joint evolution of robot morphologies and controllers is enhanced with individual learning. Its defining advantage lies in the offspring inheriting controllers learned by their parents. The efficacy of this option, however, relies on morphological similarity between parent and offspring. In this study, we examine how Lamarckian inheritance performs when the search process is driven toward high morphological variance, potentially straining the requirement for parent-offspring similarity. Using a system of modular robots that can evolve and learn to solve a locomotion task, we compare Darwinian and Lamarckian evolution to determine how they respond to shifting from pure task-based selection to a multi-objective pressure that also rewards morphological novelty. Our results confirm that Lamarckian evolution outperforms Darwinian evolution when optimizing task-performance alone. However, introducing selection pressure for morphological diversity causes a substantial performance drop, which is much greater in the Lamarckian system. Further analyses show that promoting diversity reduces parent-offspring similarity, which in turn reduces the benefits of inheriting controllers learned by parents. These results reveal the limits of Lamarckian evolution by exposing a fundamental trade-off between inheritance-based exploitation and diversity-driven exploration.

</details>


### [84] [Environment-Adaptive Solid-State LiDAR-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15864)
*Zhi Zhang,Chalermchon Satirapod,Bingtao Ma,Changjun Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种环境自适应的固态激光雷达-惯性SLAM方法，通过局部法向量约束和退化感知的地图维护来提升极端环境下的定位精度和地图一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态激光雷达-惯性SLAM在速度和鲁棒性方面具有优势，但在极端环境中由于严重的几何退化和不可靠观测，常导致优化病态和地图不一致，难以实现精确建图。

Method: 提出环境自适应的固态激光雷达-惯性里程计，集成局部法向量约束与退化感知的地图维护。具体包括：1）引入局部法向量约束提升状态估计稳定性，抑制退化场景中的定位漂移；2）设计退化引导的地图更新策略以提高地图精度。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在极端和感知退化环境中实现了优越的建图精度和鲁棒性，相比基线方法平均RMSE降低高达12.8%。

Conclusion: 通过局部法向量约束和退化感知地图维护，该方法有效解决了固态激光雷达-惯性SLAM在极端环境中的几何退化和地图一致性问题，显著提升了定位和建图性能。

Abstract: Solid-state LiDAR-inertial SLAM has attracted significant attention due to its advantages in speed and robustness. However, achieving accurate mapping in extreme environments remains challenging due to severe geometric degeneracy and unreliable observations, which often lead to ill-conditioned optimization and map inconsistencies. To address these challenges, we propose an environment-adaptive solid-state LiDAR-inertial odometry that integrates local normal-vector constraints with degeneracy-aware map maintenance to enhance localization accuracy. Specifically, we introduce local normal-vector constraints to improve the stability of state estimation, effectively suppressing localization drift in degenerate scenarios. Furthermore, we design a degeneration-guided map update strategy to improve map precision. Benefiting from the refined map representation, localization accuracy is further enhanced in subsequent estimation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior mapping accuracy and robustness in extreme and perceptually degraded environments, with an average RMSE reduction of up to 12.8% compared to the baseline method.

</details>


### [85] [DTEA: A Dual-Topology Elastic Actuator Enabling Real-Time Switching Between Series and Parallel Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15865)
*Vishal Ramesh,Aman Singh,Shishir Kolathaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型双拓扑弹性执行器（DTEA），能够在运行过程中实时在串联弹性执行器（SEA）和并联弹性执行器（PEA）拓扑之间动态切换，并通过实验验证了其可行性、鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 串联弹性执行器（SEA）和并联弹性执行器（PEA）具有互补但相互排斥的优势，但目前没有执行器能够在运行过程中实现这两种拓扑之间的实时切换。本研究旨在填补这一空白，开发一种能够在SEA和PEA拓扑之间动态切换的新型执行器。

Method: 提出双拓扑弹性执行器（DTEA）设计，开发了概念验证原型来展示拓扑切换机制的可行性。通过实验评估切换机制在运行条件下的鲁棒性和切换时间，并进行静态刚度和扰动抑制性能测试来表征SEA和PEA两种模式下的性能。

Result: 执行器在负载下成功完成了324次拓扑切换循环而无损坏，证明了机制的鲁棒性。SEA和PEA模式之间的切换时间小于33.33毫秒。静态刚度测试显示PEA模式比SEA模式硬1.53倍（KSEA=5.57±0.02 Nm/rad，KPEA=8.54±0.02 Nm/rad）。扰动抑制实验表明SEA模式的平均峰值偏转比PEA模式大2.26倍（5.2度 vs 2.3度），平均稳定时间长3.45倍（1380毫秒 vs 400毫秒）。

Conclusion: 双拓扑弹性执行器（DTEA）成功实现了在运行过程中SEA和PEA拓扑之间的动态切换，实验结果表明该机制具有鲁棒性且切换速度快。两种模式下的性能特征与传统SEA和PEA执行器一致，验证了DTEA执行器两种模式的功能性。这项研究为需要动态适应不同工作条件的机器人系统提供了新的执行器设计可能性。

Abstract: Series and parallel elastic actuators offer complementary but mutually exclusive advantages, yet no existing actuator enables real-time transition between these topologies during operation. This paper presents a novel actuator design called the Dual-Topology Elastic Actuator (DTEA), which enables dynamic switching between SEA and PEA topologies during operation. A proof-of-concept prototype of the DTEA is developed to demonstrate the feasibility of the topology-switching mechanism. Experiments are conducted to evaluate the robustness and timing of the switching mechanism under operational conditions. The actuator successfully performed 324 topology-switching cycles under load without damage, demonstrating the robustness of the mechanism. The measured switching time between SEA and PEA modes is under 33.33 ms. Additional experiments are conducted to characterize the static stiffness and disturbance rejection performance in both SEA and PEA modes. Static stiffness tests show that the PEA mode is 1.53x stiffer than the SEA mode, with KSEA = 5.57 +/- 0.02 Nm/rad and KPEA = 8.54 +/- 0.02 Nm/rad. Disturbance rejection experiments show that the mean peak deflection in SEA mode is 2.26x larger than in PEA mode (5.2 deg vs. 2.3 deg), while the mean settling time is 3.45x longer (1380 ms vs. 400 ms). The observed behaviors are consistent with the known characteristics of conventional SEA and PEA actuators, validating the functionality of both modes in the DTEA actuator.

</details>


### [86] [Robust Fleet Sizing for Multi-UAV Inspection Missions under Synchronized Replacement Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15890)
*Vishal Ramesh,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对多无人机巡检任务中的机队规模问题，提出了一个考虑同步电池耗尽的最坏情况机队规模规则，相比传统基于统计独立假设的Erlang-B方法显著提高了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机机队规模规划方法存在两个主要问题：一是假设稳态运行条件，不适用于有限时间任务；二是将替换请求视为统计独立事件，这种基于单次请求阻塞保证的方法在需求聚集时无法保证任务级可靠性。研究发现，高效路由策略会给每架无人机分配相似的工作负载，导致电池同步耗尽和替换需求爆发，即使在平均容量足够的情况下也可能耗尽备用无人机池。

Method: 论文推导出一个闭式充分机队规模规则：k = m(ceil(R) + 1)，其中m是活跃无人机数量，R是恢复时间与活跃时间之比。这个额外的m架备用无人机缓冲区可以吸收恢复周期边界处最坏情况的同步需求。通过蒙特卡洛模拟在五种场景下进行验证（m在[2,10]范围内，R在[0.87,3.39]范围内，每种场景1000次试验）。

Result: 蒙特卡洛验证显示，使用每请求阻塞目标ε=0.01的Erlang-B规模规划方法在R=3.39时任务成功率降至69.9%，95%的备用耗尽事件集中在需求最高的5分钟窗口内。相比之下，提出的规则在所有测试条件下（包括风速变异系数CV=0.30）都保持了99.8%的成功率（Wilson 95%置信下限99.3%），在最苛刻场景下仅需额外增加4架无人机。

Conclusion: 论文提出的机队规模规则通过考虑同步电池耗尽的最坏情况，为有限时间多无人机巡检任务提供了可靠的任务级保证。相比传统基于统计独立假设的方法，该方法显著提高了任务成功率，同时增加的硬件成本有限，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Multi-UAV inspection missions require spare drones to replace active drones during recharging cycles. Existing fleet-sizing approaches often assume steady-state operating conditions that do not apply to finite-horizon missions, or they treat replacement requests as statistically independent events. The latter provides per-request blocking guarantees that fail to translate to mission-level reliability when demands cluster. This paper identifies a structural failure mode where efficient routing assigns similar workloads to each UAV, leading to synchronized battery depletion and replacement bursts that exhaust the spare pool even when average capacity is sufficient.
  We derive a closed-form sufficient fleet-sizing rule, k = m(ceil(R) + 1), where m is the number of active UAVs and R is the recovery-to-active time ratio. This additive buffer of m spares absorbs worst-case synchronized demand at recovery-cycle boundaries and ensures mission-level reliability even when all UAVs deplete simultaneously. Monte Carlo validation across five scenarios (m in [2, 10], R in [0.87, 3.39], 1000 trials each) shows that Erlang-B sizing with a per-request blocking target epsilon = 0.01 drops to 69.9% mission success at R = 3.39, with 95% of spare exhaustion events concentrated in the top-decile 5-minute demand windows. In contrast, the proposed rule maintains 99.8% success (Wilson 95% lower bound 99.3%) across all tested conditions, including wind variability up to CV = 0.30, while requiring only four additional drones in the most demanding scenario.

</details>


### [87] [A Reconfigurable Pneumatic Joint Enabling Localized Selective Stiffening and Shape Locking in Vine-Inspired Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15907)
*Ayodele James Oyejide,Ustaz A. Yaqub,Samir Erturk,Eray A. Baran,Fabio Stroppa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可重构气动关节（RPJ）架构，通过离散压力可调刚度增强藤蔓机器人结构性能，实现自由空间中更好的形状保持和负载能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统藤蔓机器人在自由空间中存在轴向刚度低、负载能力差、转向时形状无法保持等根本限制，需要一种在不影响连续生长能力的同时增强结构性能的解决方案

Method: 设计可重构气动关节（RPJ）模块，包含对称分布的气动腔室，通过加压实现局部弯曲刚度增加；将RPJ集成到肌腱驱动转向的软体生长机器人中，并开发紧凑的基座站用于空中外翻

Result: 系统表征和实验验证显示：外翻所需压力适中，局部刚化性能与层压机制相当；机器人实现了弯曲时更好的形状保持、负载下重力偏转减小、级联收缩以及高达202g的可靠有效载荷运输

Conclusion: RPJ机制为结构自适应藤蔓机器人提供了实用途径，适用于物体分类和自适应探索等操作导向任务，在无约束环境中具有应用潜力

Abstract: Vine-inspired robots achieve large workspace coverage through tip eversion, enabling safe navigation in confined and cluttered environments. However, their deployment in free space is fundamentally limited by low axial stiffness, poor load-bearing capacity, and the inability to retain shape during and after steering. In this work, we propose a reconfigurable pneumatic joint (RPJ) architecture that introduces discrete, pressure-tunable stiffness along the robot body without compromising continuous growth. Each RPJ module comprises symmetrically distributed pneumatic chambers that locally increase bending stiffness when pressurized, enabling decoupling between global compliance and localized rigidity. We integrate the RPJs into a soft growing robot with tendon-driven steering and develop a compact base station for mid-air eversion. System characterization and experimental validation demonstrate moderate pressure requirements for eversion, as well as comparable localized stiffening and steering performance to layer-jamming mechanisms. Demonstrations further show that the proposed robot achieves improved shape retention during bending, reduced gravitational deflection under load, cascading retraction, and reliable payload transport up to 202 g in free space. The RPJ mechanism establishes a practical pathway toward structurally adaptive vine robots for manipulation-oriented tasks such as object sorting and adaptive exploration in unconstrained environments.

</details>


### [88] [DENALI: A Dataset Enabling Non-Line-of-Sight Spatial Reasoning with Low-Cost LiDARs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16201)
*Nikhil Behari,Diego Rivero,Luke Apostolides,Suman Ghosh,Paul Pu Liang,Ramesh Raskar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了DENALI数据集，这是首个大规模真实世界的低成本LiDAR时空直方图数据集，用于数据驱动的非视距感知研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级LiDAR内部记录包含直接和多反射光返回的完整时间分辨直方图，这些多反射返回编码了丰富的非视距线索，但传统方法难以利用这些信息。需要探索数据驱动的方法来实现低成本LiDAR的非视距感知。

Method: 创建DENALI数据集，采集72,000个隐藏物体场景的时间分辨LiDAR直方图，涵盖不同物体形状、位置、光照条件和空间分辨率。使用该数据集进行数据驱动的非视距感知研究。

Result: 研究表明消费级LiDAR能够实现准确的数据驱动非视距感知。识别了限制性能的关键场景和建模因素，以及当前模拟到真实转换存在的保真度差距。

Conclusion: 该工作为使用消费级LiDAR实现可扩展的非视距视觉提供了基础，指出了未来研究方向，特别是解决模拟到真实转换的保真度问题。

Abstract: Consumer LiDARs in mobile devices and robots typically output a single depth value per pixel. Yet internally, they record full time-resolved histograms containing direct and multi-bounce light returns; these multi-bounce returns encode rich non-line-of-sight (NLOS) cues that can enable perception of hidden objects in a scene. However, severe hardware limitations of consumer LiDARs make NLOS reconstruction with conventional methods difficult. In this work, we motivate a complementary direction: enabling NLOS perception with low-cost LiDARs through data-driven inference. We present DENALI, the first large-scale real-world dataset of space-time histograms from low-cost LiDARs capturing hidden objects. We capture time-resolved LiDAR histograms for 72,000 hidden-object scenes across diverse object shapes, positions, lighting conditions, and spatial resolutions. Using our dataset, we show that consumer LiDARs can enable accurate, data-driven NLOS perception. We further identify key scene and modeling factors that limit performance, as well as simulation-fidelity gaps that hinder current sim-to-real transfer, motivating future work toward scalable NLOS vision with consumer LiDARs.

</details>


### [89] [Semantic Area Graph Reasoning for Multi-Robot Language-Guided Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16263)
*Ruiyang Wang,Hao-Lun Hsu,Jiwoo Kim,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGR框架使用LLM通过语义区域图协调多机器人系统进行语义搜索，相比传统几何探索方法显著提升语义目标搜索效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多机器人协调策略（如前沿覆盖或信息增益）无法融入高层任务意图（如搜索特定房间类型的关联对象），需要语义推理能力

Method: 提出语义区域图推理（SAGR）分层框架：从语义占据图增量构建语义区域图，编码房间实例、连通性、前沿可用性和机器人状态；LLM基于空间结构和任务上下文进行高层语义房间分配，确定性前沿规划和局部导航处理几何执行

Result: 在Habitat-Matterport3D数据集的100个场景实验中，SAGR在保持与最先进探索方法竞争力的同时，持续提升语义目标搜索效率，在大型环境中提升达18.8%

Conclusion: 结构化语义抽象作为LLM推理与多机器人协调的有效接口，在复杂室内环境中具有重要价值

Abstract: Coordinating multi-robot systems (MRS) to search in unknown environments is particularly challenging for tasks that require semantic reasoning beyond geometric exploration. Classical coordination strategies rely on frontier coverage or information gain and cannot incorporate high-level task intent, such as searching for objects associated with specific room types. We propose \textit{Semantic Area Graph Reasoning} (SAGR), a hierarchical framework that enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to coordinate multi-robot exploration and semantic search through a structured semantic-topological abstraction of the environment. SAGR incrementally constructs a semantic area graph from a semantic occupancy map, encoding room instances, connectivity, frontier availability, and robot states into a compact task-relevant representation for LLM reasoning. The LLM performs high-level semantic room assignment based on spatial structure and task context, while deterministic frontier planning and local navigation handle geometric execution within assigned rooms. Experiments on the Habitat-Matterport3D dataset across 100 scenarios show that SAGR remains competitive with state-of-the-art exploration methods while consistently improving semantic target search efficiency, with up to 18.8\% in large environments. These results highlight the value of structured semantic abstractions as an effective interface between LLM-based reasoning and multi-robot coordination in complex indoor environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [Verification Modulo Tested Library Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15533)
*Abhishek Uppar,Omar Muhammad,Sumanth Prabhu,Deepak D'Souza,Madhusudan P,Adithya Murali*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种验证客户端程序使用复杂库的方法：通过合成库方法的模块化契约，这些契约既能证明客户端正确性，又能通过测试引擎验证，同时引入上下文契约概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化验证使用复杂库的客户端程序面临挑战，因为需要同时确保库方法的契约既足够证明客户端正确性，又能在实际测试中通过验证。

Method: 提出反例引导的学习框架，通过合成器与约束求解器和测试引擎交互，推断模块化/上下文方法契约及客户端的归纳不变量，使用基于ICE学习算法的通用CHC求解器作为主要合成引擎。

Result: 实现了名为vmtlc的工具，并在客户端调用大型库的基准测试中展示了其有效性。

Conclusion: 验证模块测试库契约的方法能够有效自动化验证使用复杂库的客户端程序，上下文契约比传统模块化契约更简单易推断，反例引导学习框架结合CHC求解器和测试引擎是可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider the problem of \emph{verification modulo tested library
  contracts} as a step towards automating the verification of client programs that use complex libraries. We formulate this problem as the synthesis of modular contracts for the library methods used by the client that are adequate to prove the client correct, and that also pass the scrutiny of a testing engine that tests the library against these contracts. We also consider a new form of method contracts called \emph{contextual contracts} that arise in this setting that hold in the context of the client program, and can often be simpler and easier to infer than classical modular contracts. We provide a counterexample-guided learning framework to solve this problem, in which the synthesizer interacts with a constraint solver as well as the testing engine in order to infer adequate modular/contextual method contracts and inductive invariants for the client. The main synthesis engines we use are generalizing CHC solvers that are realized using ICE learning algorithms. We realize this framework in a tool called \vmtlc and show its efficacy on benchmarks where clients call large libraries.

</details>


### [91] [Synthesizing Backward Error Bounds, Backward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15633)
*Laura Zielinski,Justin Hsu*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出自动化后向误差分析框架，通过范畴论方法建模稳定数值程序，开发工具eggshel自动证明后向稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后向稳定性是数值算法的重要属性，但现有自动化工具主要针对前向误差分析，缺乏有效的后向误差分析工具。需要开发能够处理变量重用等挑战的自动化后向稳定性证明方法。

Method: 1) 提出新的后向稳定性定义，具有组合性和灵活性；2) 建立范畴Shel，其中态射建模稳定数值程序；3) 开发工具eggshel，在Shel的语法子范畴中自动搜索后向稳定性证明；4) 算法能处理变量重用等挑战性问题。

Result: 实现了eggshel工具，能够自动合成先前超出自动化分析能力的程序的后向误差界，证明了算法的正确性，并在多个程序上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一个形式化框架，支持广泛数值程序的自动化后向误差分析，通过范畴论方法实现了组合性分析，开发的工具能够处理复杂程序并超越现有自动化分析能力。

Abstract: Backward stability is a desirable property for a well-designed numerical algorithm: given an input, a backward stable floating-point program produces the exact output for a nearby input. While automated tools for bounding the forward error of a numerical program are well-established, few existing tools target backward error analysis. We present a formal framework that enables sound, automated backward error analysis for a broad class of numerical programs. First, we propose a novel generalization of the definition of backward stability that is both compositional and flexible, satisfied by a wide range of floating-point operations. Second, based on this generalization, we develop the category Shel where morphisms model stable numerical programs, and show that structures in Shel support a rich variety of backward error analyses. Third, we implement a tool, eggshel, that automatically searches within a syntactic subcategory of Shel to prove backward stability for a given program. Our algorithm handles many programs with variable reuse, a known challenge in backward error analysis. We prove soundness of our algorithm and use our tool to synthesize backward error bounds for a suite of programs that were previously beyond the reach of automated analysis.

</details>


### [92] [jMT: Testing Correctness of Java Memory Models (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15978)
*Lukas Panneke,Heike Wehrheim*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: jMT是一个用于构建并发Java程序多执行语义的新工具，通过因果检查从单执行模型构建有效的多执行语义，支持评估Java内存模型提案并测试其与编译方案的符合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Java内存模型存在缺陷，现有修复方案要么发现新的意外行为，要么禁止标准编译器优化。由于缺乏自动验证Java内存模型新提案的工具，需要开发能够构建多执行语义的工具来评估内存模型提案。

Method: jMT基于定义良好执行图的单执行模型，通过因果检查构建有效的多执行语义。该工具支持按程序评估Java内存模型提案，并测试内存模型与现有编译方案和编译器的符合性。

Result: 在169个litmus测试上评估jMT，揭示了现有Java内存模型的一些有趣见解，验证了工具的有效性。

Conclusion: jMT填补了Java内存模型验证工具的空白，能够有效评估新提案并测试与编译方案的符合性，为Java内存模型研究提供了重要工具支持。

Abstract: Folklore is often saying "The Java memory model is broken." Therefore, several approaches have proposed repairs, only to find new programs exhibiting unexpected, unintuitive behavior or the model forbidding standard compiler optimizations. The complexity of defining a memory model for concurrent Java lies in the fact that it requires a multi-execution model. Multi-execution models need to inspect the many potential executions of a program in order to find the valid ones. Tools automatically validating novel proposals of Java memory models are, however, largely lacking. To alleviate this problem, we introduce jMT, a novel tool for constructing multi-execution semantics for concurrent Java programs. jMT relies on single-execution models defining well-formed execution graphs, based on which it builds valid multi-execution semantics via causality checking. Thereby, jMT supports evaluating new proposals of Java memory models (JMMs) on a per-program basis. jMT can furthermore be employed for testing the conformance of JMMs to existing compilation schemes and compilers. Our evaluation of jMT on 169 litmus tests reveals a number of interesting insights into existing JMMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [The Spectral Geometry of Thought: Phase Transitions, Instruction Reversal, Token-Level Dynamics, and Perfect Correctness Prediction in How Transformers Reason](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15350)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现大语言模型在推理与事实回忆任务中，其隐藏激活空间表现出谱相变现象，揭示了推理的谱理论框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型在推理与事实回忆任务中隐藏激活空间的谱特性差异，理解模型内部表示几何结构如何反映不同认知过程

Method: 对11个模型（涵盖5种架构家族）进行系统谱分析，包括Qwen、Pythia、Phi、Llama、DeepSeek-R1，识别七种核心现象：推理谱压缩、指令微调谱反转、架构依赖生成分类、谱缩放定律、令牌级谱级联、推理步骤谱标点、谱正确性预测

Result: 发现9/11模型在推理任务中α值显著降低；指令微调模型反转了基模型的关系；建立了α_reasoning ∝ -0.074 ln N的缩放定律；谱α值在最终答案生成前就能预测正确性（Qwen2.5-7B的AUC=1.000，6个模型平均AUC=0.893）

Conclusion: 建立了Transformer推理的全面谱理论，揭示思维几何结构在方向上具有普适性，在动力学上具有架构特异性，并能预测任务结果

Abstract: We discover that large language models exhibit \emph{spectral phase transitions} in their hidden activation spaces when engaging in reasoning versus factual recall. Through systematic spectral analysis across \textbf{11 models} spanning \textbf{5 architecture families} (Qwen, Pythia, Phi, Llama, DeepSeek-R1), we identify \textbf{seven} core phenomena: (1)~\textbf{Reasoning Spectral Compression} -- 9/11 models show significantly lower $α$ for reasoning ($p < 0.05$), with larger effects in stronger models; (2)~\textbf{Instruction Tuning Spectral Reversal} -- base models show reasoning $α< $ factual $α$, while instruction-tuned models reverse this relationship; (3)~\textbf{Architecture-Dependent Generation Taxonomy} -- prompt-to-response shifts partition into expansion, compression, and equilibrium regimes; (4)~\textbf{Spectral Scaling Law} -- $α_\text{reasoning} \propto -0.074 \ln N$ across 4 Qwen base models ($R^2 = 0.46$); (5)~\textbf{Token-Level Spectral Cascade} -- per-token alpha tracking reveals local synchronization that decays exponentially with layer distance, and is weaker for reasoning than factual tasks; (6)~\textbf{Reasoning Step Spectral Punctuation} -- phase-transition signatures align with reasoning step boundaries; and (7)~\textbf{Spectral Correctness Prediction} -- spectral $α$ alone achieves AUC $= 1.000$ (Qwen2.5-7B, late layers) and mean AUC $= 0.893$ across 6 models in predicting correctness \emph{before} the final answer is generated. Together, these findings establish a comprehensive \emph{spectral theory of reasoning} in transformers, revealing that the geometry of thought is universal in direction, architecture-specific in dynamics, and predictive of outcome.

</details>


### [94] [Mapping High-Performance Regions in Battery Scheduling across Data Uncertainty, Battery Design, and Planning Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15360)
*Jaime de Miguel Rodriguez,Artjom Vargunin,Brigitta Robin Raudne,David Solis Martin,Yaroslava Mykhailenko,Kaarel Oja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过三元分析探讨了多阶段模型预测控制下储能系统的运行，揭示了数据特征、预测不确定性、规划时域和电池倍率之间的相互作用，发现了有效时域的存在，为工业储能运行提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储能系统在模型预测控制下的运行优化需要考虑多个因素的复杂相互作用，包括数据特征、预测不确定性、规划时域长度和电池倍率特性。现有研究缺乏对这些因素系统性关系的理解，导致计算成本高且难以确定最优规划时域。

Method: 采用三元分析方法，生成合成数据集来系统探索数据剖面和不确定性的变化。通过参数化方法构建数据特征与最优时域长度之间的关系映射，研究不同电池类型、不确定性水平和数据剖面的组合。

Result: 研究发现存在有效时域，即超过该时域后额外预测信息对运行效益有限。确定了不同电池类型、不确定性水平和数据剖面下的最优时域长度，量化了预测不确定性导致的收入损失，表明即使对快速电池，预测误差也会影响性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为工业储能运行提供了实用指导，通过考虑有效时域可以降低计算成本同时保持最优性能。提出的框架为未来机器学习方法奠定了基础，支持在不进行大量计算的情况下实现工业环境中的持续优化。

Abstract: This study presents a triadic analysis of energy storage operation under multi-stage model predictive control, investigating the interplay between data characteristics, forecast uncertainty, planning horizon, and battery c-rate. Synthetic datasets are generated to systematically explore variations in data profiles and uncertainty, enabling parametrization and the construction of relationships that map these characteristics to optimal horizon length. Results reveal the presence of an effective horizon, defined as the look-ahead length beyond which additional forecast information provides limited operational benefit. Accounting for this horizon can reduce computational costs while maintaining optimal performance. The study provides optimal horizon lengths across a broad range of combinations of battery types, uncertainty levels, and data profiles, offering practical guidance for industrial storage operation. It also quantifies revenue losses due to forecast uncertainty, showing that errors can impact performance even for fast batteries. Finally, the framework lays the groundwork for future machine learning approaches that map dataset parametrization to optimal horizons, supporting continuous optimization in industrial settings without heavy computation.

</details>


### [95] [Sequential KV Cache Compression via Probabilistic Language Tries: Beyond the Per-Vector Shannon Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15356)
*Gregory Magarshak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种两层的顺序KV压缩架构，利用语言模型的结构特性，通过概率前缀去重和预测差分编码，在理论上实现了比现有方法TurboQuant高约914,000倍的压缩比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存量化方法（如TurboQuant）已接近每个向量压缩的香农熵极限，但这针对的是一个比实际重要问题更弱的问题：将KV缓存作为序列进行压缩。KV缓存中的标记不是任意的浮点数据，而是模型训练所用形式语言的样本，模型本身是该语言的近乎最优预测器。

Method: 提出顺序KV压缩的两层架构：1）概率前缀去重：使用概率语言字典树（PLTs）的度量d_T(s, s') = -log_2 P_M(s ^ s')，识别跨会话中语义等价的共享前缀；2）预测差分编码：仅存储每个新KV向量与模型自身预测之间的残差，实现每个标记的熵界H(KV_{i+1} | KV_{<=i}) <= H(token_{i+1} | token_{<=i})。

Result: 理论分析表明，在典型语言模型困惑度（约10-20）下，该方法平均每个标记位置为3.3-4.3比特，而TurboQuant为每个向量分量3比特（典型注意力头有64-128个分量）。理论压缩比相对于TurboQuant约为914,000倍。即使在熵底线上1000倍（悲观最坏情况开销），压缩比仍保持约914倍，且随着上下文长度增加，压缩效果会改善而非恶化。

Conclusion: 顺序KV压缩通过利用语言模型的结构特性，实现了比现有方法显著更高的压缩比。两层架构正交且可与包括TurboQuant在内的现有每向量量化方法组合使用，为KV缓存压缩提供了新的理论框架和实际改进方向。

Abstract: Recent work on KV cache quantization, culminating in TurboQuant, has approached the Shannon entropy limit for per-vector compression of transformer key-value caches. We observe that this limit applies to a strictly weaker problem than the one that actually matters: compressing the KV cache as a sequence. The tokens stored in a KV cache are not arbitrary floating-point data -- they are samples from the exact formal language the model was trained on, and the model is by construction a near-optimal predictor of that language. We introduce sequential KV compression, a two-layer architecture that exploits this structure. The first layer, probabilistic prefix deduplication, identifies semantically equivalent shared prefixes across sessions using the trie metric d_T(s, s') = -log_2 P_M(s ^ s') from Probabilistic Language Tries (PLTs). The second layer, predictive delta coding, stores only the residual of each new KV vector from the model's own prediction of it, achieving a per-token entropy bound of H(KV_{i+1} | KV_{<=i}) <= H(token_{i+1} | token_{<=i}). We prove that at typical language model perplexity -- approximately 10-20 for fluent English text -- this bound is 3.3-4.3 bits on average per token position, compared to TurboQuant's 3 bits per vector component (with typical attention heads having 64-128 components). The theoretical compression ratio over TurboQuant is approximately 914,000x at the Shannon limit. Even at 1000x above the entropy floor -- a deliberately pessimistic worst-case overhead, two orders of magnitude above the 2-5x typical of practical source coders -- the ratio remains approximately 914x over TurboQuant, with compression improving rather than degrading as context length grows. The two layers are orthogonal and compose with existing per-vector quantization methods including TurboQuant.

</details>


### [96] [Transfer Learning from Foundational Optimization Embeddings to Unsupervised SAT Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15448)
*Koyena Pal,Serdar Kadioglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将基础优化嵌入模型应用于布尔可满足性问题，证明其能跨领域迁移到约束满足问题，支持无监督任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基础优化嵌入模型是否能够泛化到优化问题之外的决策问题领域，特别是布尔可满足性问题，探索统一表示框架的可能性

Method: 将CNF公式映射到与MIP相同的二分约束-变量图表示，直接重用预训练的嵌入模型，无需架构修改或有监督微调

Result: 嵌入模型能够捕捉SAT实例的结构规律，支持实例聚类和分布识别等无监督任务，首次证明基础优化嵌入可迁移到约束满足领域

Conclusion: 基础优化嵌入模型可成功迁移到SAT问题，这是向优化和决策问题统一表示框架迈出的一步

Abstract: Foundational optimization embeddings have recently emerged as powerful pre-trained representations for mixed-integer programming (MIP) problems. These embeddings were shown to enable cross-domain transfer and reduce reliance on solver-generated labels. In this work, we investigate whether such representations generalize beyond optimization to decision problems, focusing on Boolean satisfiability (SAT). We adapt the foundational optimization architecture to SAT by mapping CNF formulas into the same bipartite constraint-variable graph representation used for MIPs. This allows direct reuse of the pre-trained embedding model without architectural changes or supervised fine-tuning. Our results show that these embeddings capture structural regularities in SAT instances and support unsupervised tasks such as instance clustering and distribution identification. We demonstrate, for the first time, that foundational optimization embeddings can transfer to constraint satisfaction domains. Our findings is a step toward a unified representational framework for both optimization and decision problems.

</details>


### [97] [Beyond Single-Model Optimization: Preserving Plasticity in Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15414)
*Lute Lillo,Nick Cheney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TeLAPA框架通过维护行为多样化的策略档案和共享潜在空间，解决了持续强化学习中单一策略保存的局限性，实现了更灵活的任务适应和重用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续强化学习方法依赖单一模型保存，存在"可塑性丧失"问题，即保留的策略在任务干扰后可能不再提供可靠的快速适应起点。需要从保留孤立解决方案转向维护技能对齐的邻域。

Method: 引入TeLAPA框架，基于质量多样性方法，为每个任务组织行为多样化的策略邻域档案，维护共享潜在空间使存档策略在非平稳漂移下保持可比性和可重用性。

Result: 在MiniGrid CL设置中，TeLAPA成功学习更多任务，在干扰后重新访问任务时恢复能力更快，在任务序列中保持更高性能。分析显示源最优策略通常不是转移最优策略。

Conclusion: 持续强化学习应围绕可重用且胜任的策略邻域重构，超越单一模型保存，通过保留和选择多个邻近替代方案而非压缩为单一代表，实现更具可塑性的终身智能体。

Abstract: Continual reinforcement learning must balance retention with adaptation, yet many methods still rely on \emph{single-model preservation}, committing to one evolving policy as the main reusable solution across tasks. Even when a previously successful policy is retained, it may no longer provide a reliable starting point for rapid adaptation after interference, reflecting a form of \emph{loss of plasticity} that single-policy preservation cannot address. Inspired by quality-diversity methods, we introduce \textsc{TeLAPA} (Transfer-Enabled Latent-Aligned Policy Archives), a continual RL framework that organizes behaviorally diverse policy neighborhoods into per-task archives and maintains a shared latent space so that archived policies remain comparable and reusable under non-stationary drift. This perspective shifts continual RL from retaining isolated solutions to maintaining \emph{skill-aligned neighborhoods} with competent and behaviorally related policies that support future relearning. In our MiniGrid CL setting, \textsc{TeLAPA} learns more tasks successfully, recovers competence faster on revisited tasks after interference, and retains higher performance across a sequence of tasks. Our analyses show that source-optimal policies are often not transfer-optimal, even within a local competent neighborhood, and that effective reuse depends on retaining and selecting among multiple nearby alternatives rather than collapsing them to one representative. Together, these results reframe continual RL around reusable and competent policy neighborhoods, providing a route beyond single-model preservation toward more plastic lifelong agents.

</details>


### [98] [M3R: Localized Rainfall Nowcasting with Meteorology-Informed MultiModal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15377)
*Sanjeev Panta,Rhett M Morvant,Xu Yuan,Li Chen,Nian-Feng Tzeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M3R是一种基于多模态注意力机制的气象学架构，通过融合视觉雷达图像和数值天气站数据，实现直接降雨预测，在准确性和效率方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的降雨临近预报对灾害缓解和水资源管理至关重要。当前深度学习方法在有效利用多样化多媒体数据源方面存在局限，需要更有效整合异构气象数据的方法。

Method: 提出M3R架构，结合NEXRAD雷达图像和PWS数值测量数据，使用专门的多模态注意力机制，以天气站时间序列作为查询来选择性关注空间雷达特征，提取降水特征。

Result: 在三个100km×100km的NEXRAD雷达站中心区域实验中，M3R在准确性、效率和降水检测能力方面显著优于现有方法，建立了多媒体降水临近预报的新基准。

Conclusion: M3R为业务天气预报系统提供了实用工具，通过多模态数据融合和注意力机制实现了更准确的降雨预测，源代码已公开。

Abstract: Accurate and timely rainfall nowcasting is crucial for disaster mitigation and water resource management. Despite recent advances in deep learning, precipitation prediction remains challenging due to limitations in effectively leveraging diverse multimedia data sources. We introduce M3R, a Meteorology-informed MultiModal attention-based architecture for direct Rainfall prediction that synergistically combines visual NEXRAD radar imagery with numerical Personal Weather Station (PWS) measurements, using a comprehensive pipeline for temporal alignment of heterogeneous meteorological data. With specialized multimodal attention mechanisms, M3R novelly leverages weather station time series as queries to selectively attend to spatial radar features, enabling focused extraction of precipitation signatures. Experimental results for three spatial areas of 100 km * 100 km centered at NEXRAD radar stations demonstrate that M3R outperforms existing approaches, achieving substantial improvements in accuracy, efficiency, and precipitation detection capabilities. Our work establishes new benchmarks for multimedia-based precipitation nowcasting and provides practical tools for operational weather prediction systems. The source code is available at https://github.com/Sanjeev97/M3Rain

</details>


### [99] [ECG-Lens: Benchmarking ML & DL Models on PTB-XL Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15822)
*Saloni Garg,Ukant Jadia,Amit Sagtani,Kamal Kant Hiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较了传统机器学习算法和深度学习模型在PTB-XL数据集上对12导联ECG信号的分类性能，发现复杂CNN模型（ECG-Lens）表现最佳，准确率达80%，ROC-AUC为90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电图（ECG）信号的自动分类是诊断和监测心血管疾病的重要工具。研究旨在比较传统机器学习算法与深度学习模型在ECG信号分类上的性能，为选择自动化ECG分类模型提供实践基准，并指明针对特定疾病模型开发的方向。

Method: 研究比较了三种传统机器学习算法（决策树分类器、随机森林分类器、逻辑回归）和三种深度学习模型（简单CNN、LSTM、复杂CNN-ECGLens）。使用PTB-XL数据集（包含正常患者和多种心脏疾病患者的12导联记录）。深度学习模型在原始ECG信号上训练，自动提取判别特征。应用平稳小波变换（SWT）进行数据增强以提高模型性能、增加训练样本多样性并保留ECG信号的基本特征。使用准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数和ROC-AUC等多种指标评估模型性能。

Result: ECG-Lens模型取得了最佳性能，分类准确率达到80%，ROC-AUC为90%。深度学习架构（特别是复杂CNN）在原始12导联ECG数据上显著优于传统机器学习方法。

Conclusion: 深度学习架构（尤其是复杂CNN）在原始12导联ECG信号分类任务上大幅超越传统机器学习方法。该研究为选择自动化ECG分类模型提供了实用基准，并为针对特定疾病条件的模型开发指明了方向。

Abstract: Automated classification of electrocardiogram (ECG) signals is a useful tool for diagnosing and monitoring cardiovascular diseases. This study compares three traditional machine learning algorithms (Decision Tree Classifier, Random Forest Classifier, and Logistic Regression) and three deep learning models (Simple Convolutional Neural Network (CNN), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), and Complex CNN (ECGLens)) for the classification of ECG signals from the PTB-XL dataset, which contains 12-lead recordings from normal patients and patients with various cardiac conditions. The DL models were trained on raw ECG signals, allowing them to automatically extract discriminative features. Data augmentation using the Stationary Wavelet Transform (SWT) was applied to enhance model performance, increase the diversity of training samples, and preserve the essential characteristics of the ECG signals. The models were evaluated using multiple metrics, including accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. The ECG-Lens model achieved the highest performance, with 80% classification accuracy and a 90% ROC-AUC. These findings demonstrate that deep learning architectures, particularly complex CNNs substantially outperform traditional ML methods on raw 12-lead ECG data, and provide a practical benchmark for selecting automated ECG classification models and identifying directions for condition-specific model development.

</details>


### [100] [Prototype-Grounded Concept Models for Verifiable Concept Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16076)
*Stefano Colamonaco,David Debot,Pietro Barbiero,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PGCMs通过视觉原型来锚定概念，提升概念瓶颈模型的可解释性和可干预性，同时保持预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型虽然通过人类可理解的概念来结构化预测，但无法验证学习到的概念是否与人类意图一致，这损害了可解释性

Method: 引入原型锚定的概念模型，将概念锚定在学习到的视觉原型上——这些原型是图像中作为概念明确证据的部分，支持在原型层面进行针对性的人工干预

Result: PGCMs在预测性能上与最先进的概念瓶颈模型相当，同时在透明度、可解释性和可干预性方面显著提升

Conclusion: 通过视觉原型锚定概念能够有效解决概念对齐问题，为深度学习模型提供了更可靠的可解释性框架

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) aim to improve interpretability in Deep Learning by structuring predictions through human-understandable concepts, but they provide no way to verify whether learned concepts align with the human's intended meaning, hurting interpretability. We introduce Prototype-Grounded Concept Models (PGCMs), which ground concepts in learned visual prototypes: image parts that serve as explicit evidence for the concepts. This grounding enables direct inspection of concept semantics and supports targeted human intervention at the prototype level to correct misalignments. Empirically, PGCMs match the predictive performance of state-of-the-art CBMs while substantially improving transparency, interpretability, and intervenability.

</details>


### [101] [Hallucination as Trajectory Commitment: Causal Evidence for Asymmetric Attractor Dynamics in Transformer Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15400)
*G. Aytug Akarlar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现自回归语言模型的幻觉是由早期轨迹承诺和不对称吸引子动力学控制的，幻觉轨迹在第一个生成token就与事实轨迹分离，纠正幻觉需要多步干预而引发幻觉只需单次扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自回归语言模型中幻觉产生的根本机制，特别是探索幻觉是否由特定的动力学模式控制，以及纠正幻觉与引发幻觉之间的不对称性。

Method: 使用相同提示分叉方法重复采样相同输入观察自发分叉，通过激活修补技术分析28个层的因果不对称性，采用窗口修补研究干预持续性，通过残差状态分析和无监督聚类识别提示编码阶段的模式。

Result: 44.3%的提示在第一个生成token就发生分叉；幻觉激活注入正确轨迹导致87.5%的输出损坏，而反向干预仅恢复33.3%；残差状态可预测幻觉率（r=0.776）；无监督聚类识别出五个类似机制组，其中鞍点相邻组集中了12/13的分叉错误前提提示。

Conclusion: 幻觉是局部稳定的吸引子盆地：进入是概率性和快速的，退出需要跨层和跨步的协调干预，相关盆地的选择由在步骤0即可识别的可聚类机制决定。

Abstract: We present causal evidence that hallucination in autoregressive language models is an early trajectory commitment governed by asymmetric attractor dynamics. Using same-prompt bifurcation, in which we repeatedly sample identical inputs to observe spontaneous divergence, we isolate trajectory dynamics from prompt-level confounds. On Qwen2.5-1.5B across 61 prompts spanning six categories, 27 prompts (44.3%) bifurcate with factual and hallucinated trajectories diverging at the first generated token (KL = 0 at step 0, KL > 1.0 at step 1). Activation patching across 28 layers reveals a pronounced causal asymmetry: injecting a hallucinated activation into a correct trajectory corrupts output in 87.5% of trials (layer 20), while the reverse recovers only 33.3% (layer 24); both exceed the 10.4% baseline (p = 0.025) and 12.5% random-patch control. Window patching shows correction requires sustained multi-step intervention, whereas corruption needs only a single perturbation. Probing the prompt encoding itself, step-0 residual states predict per-prompt hallucination rate at Pearson r = 0.776 at layer 15 (p < 0.001 against a 1000-permutation null); unsupervised clustering identifies five regime-like groups (eta^2 = 0.55) whose saddle-adjacent cluster concentrates 12 of the 13 bifurcating false-premise prompts, indicating that the basin structure is organized around regime commitments fixed at prompt encoding. These findings characterize hallucination as a locally stable attractor basin: entry is probabilistic and rapid, exit demands coordinated intervention across layers and steps, and the relevant basins are selected by clusterable regimes already discernible at step 0.

</details>


### [102] [Dispatch-Aware Ragged Attention for Pruned Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15408)
*Saif Mahmoud,Ahmad Almasri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个轻量级双向Triton注意力内核，解决了Vision Transformers中令牌剪枝后短序列的调度开销瓶颈问题，使剪枝带来的计算节省在实际运行时间中更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Vision Transformers的令牌剪枝方法虽然能减少注意力FLOPs，但在使用可变长度注意力API（如FlashAttention-2 varlen和PyTorch NestedTensor SDPA）时，实际运行时间并未相应减少。主要瓶颈在于调度开销：在剪枝后的短序列长度（≤197个令牌）下，矩阵运算只需微秒级时间，而主机端调度路径却消耗60-90微秒。

Method: 提出了一个轻量级双向Triton注意力内核，其调度开销降至40微秒，比FlashAttention-2 varlen低约1.5倍。该内核集成到完整的pack-attend-unpack流水线中，能够有效处理剪枝后的可变长度序列。

Result: 在完整的pack-attend-unpack流水线中，系统相比填充的PyTorch SDPA实现了高达2.24倍的端到端吞吐量提升，在四种剪枝算法（Threshold-L2、DynamicViT、EViT、ATS）上表现一致，支持DeiT-T/S/B模型扩展，并保持比特精确的分类预测（最大绝对对数差异<0.007）。

Conclusion: 通过降低注意力内核的调度开销，使Vision Transformers令牌剪枝的实际计算节省能够在实际运行时间中体现，解决了当前可变长度注意力API在短序列场景下的性能瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Token pruning methods for Vision Transformers (ViTs) promise quadratic reductions in attention FLOPs by dropping uninformative patches. Yet when pruned sequences are executed with state-of-the-art variable-length attention APIs -- including FlashAttention-2's varlen and PyTorch's NestedTensor SDPA-the wall-clock attention latency doesn't scale accordingly. We trace this to a dispatch-overhead bottleneck: at the short, post-pruning sequence lengths typical of ViTs (<=197 tokens), actual matrix arithmetic completes in single-digit microseconds while the host-side dispatch path consumes 60-90 us. We present a lightweight, bidirectional Triton attention kernel whose dispatch floor is 40 us roughly 1.5x lower than FlashAttention-2 varlen-allowing pruning savings to become more visible in wall-clock time. Integrated into a complete pack-attend-unpack pipeline, our system achieves up to 2.24x end-to-end throughput over padded PyTorch SDPA consistently across four pruning algorithms (Threshold-L2, DynamicViT, EViT, ATS), scales across DeiT-T/S/B, and maintains bit-exact classification predictions with <0.007 max absolute logit difference.

</details>


### [103] [StoSignSGD: Unbiased Structural Stochasticity Fixes SignSGD for Training Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15416)
*Dingzhi Yu,Rui Pan,Yuxing Liu,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出StoSignSGD算法，通过向sign算子注入结构性随机性解决SignSGD在非光滑目标上的发散问题，在凸优化中达到理论下界，在非凸非光滑优化中改进复杂度边界，在LLM训练中表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SignSGD等基于符号的优化算法在分布式学习和大型基础模型训练中表现出色，但在ReLUs、max-pools、混合专家等现代机器学习中普遍存在的非光滑目标上会发散，需要克服这一根本限制。

Method: 提出StoSignSGD算法，在保持无偏更新步骤的同时向sign算子注入结构性随机性；建立符号转换框架可将任何通用优化器转换为无偏的基于符号的对应版本；在凸优化和非凸非光滑优化中进行理论分析。

Result: 理论证明StoSignSGD解决了SignSGD的非收敛问题，在凸优化中达到尖锐收敛率匹配下界，在非凸非光滑优化中改进了已知复杂度边界；实证显示在FP8低精度预训练中比基线加速1.44-2.14倍，在7B LLM数学推理微调中优于AdamW和SignSGD。

Conclusion: StoSignSGD通过结构性随机性注入解决了SignSGD在非光滑目标上的发散问题，在理论和实证上都表现出优越性能，为基于符号的优化提供了稳健高效的解决方案，并通过符号转换框架为算法设计提供了系统分析工具。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in distributed learning and training large foundation models. Despite their empirical superiority, SignSGD is known to diverge on non-smooth objectives, which are ubiquitous in modern machine learning due to ReLUs, max-pools, and mixture-of-experts. To overcome this fundamental limitation, we propose \textbf{StoSignSGD}, an algorithm that injects structural stochasticity into the sign operator while maintaining an unbiased update step. In the regime of (online) convex optimization, our theoretical analysis shows that StoSignSGD rigorously resolves the non-convergence issues of SignSGD, achieving a sharp convergence rate matching the lower bound. For the more challenging non-convex non-smooth optimization, we introduce generalized stationary measures that encompass prior definitions, proving that StoSignSGD improves upon the best-known complexity bounds by dimensional factors. Empirically, StoSignSGD exhibits robust stability and superior efficiency across diverse large language model (LLM) training regimes. Notably, in low-precision FP8 pretraining -- a setting where AdamW fails catastrophically -- StoSignSGD remains highly stable and yields a remarkable 1.44$\times$ to 2.14$\times$ speedup relative to established baselines. Furthermore, when fine-tuning 7B LLMs on mathematical reasoning tasks, StoSignSGD delivers substantial performance gains over both AdamW and SignSGD. Finally, to dissect the mechanisms driving its success, we develop a sign conversion framework capable of transforming any general optimizer into its unbiased, sign-based counterpart. Utilizing this framework, we deconstruct the core components of StoSignSGD and present a comprehensive ablation study to empirically validate our algorithmic design choices.

</details>


### [104] [Harmonizing Multi-Objective LLM Unlearning via Unified Domain Representation and Bidirectional Logit Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15482)
*Yisheng Zhong,Sijia Liu,Zhuangdi Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多目标大语言模型遗忘框架，通过数据和优化协同设计，在移除有害信息的同时保持通用能力、避免过度拒绝相邻概念，并增强对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法主要关注遗忘效果和效用保持，忽视了对抗鲁棒性和边界行为。单纯扩展这些方法到多目标设置可能导致遗忘任务干扰，需要一种能同时满足多个挑战性目标的统一框架。

Method: 采用数据和优化协同设计：1）将训练语料标准化为统一数据表示以减少领域差距；2）引入双向蒸馏方法，从上下文指导的教师模型中引出期望行为，同时抑制学生模型中的不良行为。

Result: 理论和实证分析表明，该方法能对齐领域分布，将看似无关的遗忘任务转化为协同优化。评估显示在多样挑战性需求下实现了最先进的平衡可靠遗忘性能。

Conclusion: 提出的多目标遗忘框架通过统一数据表示和双向蒸馏，有效协调多个遗忘目标，实现了平衡可靠的遗忘性能，解决了现有方法忽视鲁棒性和边界行为的问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) unlearning is crucial for removing hazardous or privacy-leaking information from the model. Practical LLM unlearning demands satisfying multiple challenging objectives simultaneously: removing undesirable knowledge, preserving general utility, avoiding over-refusal of neighboring concepts, and, crucially, ensuring robustness against adversarial probing attacks. However, existing unlearning methods primarily focus on a limited subset of these goals, typically unlearning efficacy and utility preservation while overlooking robustness and boundary behaviors. Naively extending these methods to multi-objective settings may lead to unlearning task interference. We propose a novel multi-objective unlearning framework that harmonizes multiple unlearning objectives through a data and optimization co-design: We standardize training corpora into a unified data representation to reduce the domain gap, and then introduce a bidirectional distillation method that simultaneously elicits desired behavior from a context-instructed teacher while suppressing undesirable behavior in the student model. Theoretical and empirical analyses show that our method aligns domain distributions and converts seemingly irrelevant unlearning tasks into cooperative optimization. Evaluation demonstrates state-of-the-art performance, which enables balanced and reliable unlearning across diverse, challenging requirements.

</details>


### [105] [$π_{0.7}$: a Steerable Generalist Robotic Foundation Model with Emergent Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15483)
*Physical Intelligence,Bo Ai,Ali Amin,Raichelle Aniceto,Ashwin Balakrishna,Greg Balke,Kevin Black,George Bokinsky,Shihao Cao,Thomas Charbonnier,Vedant Choudhary,Foster Collins,Ken Conley,Grace Connors,James Darpinian,Karan Dhabalia,Maitrayee Dhaka,Jared DiCarlo,Danny Driess,Michael Equi,Adnan Esmail,Yunhao Fang,Chelsea Finn,Catherine Glossop,Thomas Godden,Ivan Goryachev,Lachlan Groom,Haroun Habeeb,Hunter Hancock,Karol Hausman,Gashon Hussein,Victor Hwang,Brian Ichter,Connor Jacobsen,Szymon Jakubczak,Rowan Jen,Tim Jones,Gregg Kammerer,Ben Katz,Liyiming Ke,Mairbek Khadikov,Chandra Kuchi,Marinda Lamb,Devin LeBlanc,Brendon LeCount,Sergey Levine,Xinyu Li,Adrian Li-Bell,Vladislav Lialin,Zhonglin Liang,Wallace Lim,Yao Lu,Enyu Luo,Vishnu Mano,Nandan Marwaha,Aikys Mongush,Liam Murphy,Suraj Nair,Tyler Patterson,Karl Pertsch,Allen Z. Ren,Gavin Schelske,Charvi Sharma,Baifeng Shi,Lucy Xiaoyang Shi,Laura Smith,Jost Tobias Springenberg,Kyle Stachowicz,Will Stoeckle,Jiaming Tang,Jimmy Tanner,Shalom Tekeste,Marcel Torne,Kyle Vedder,Quan Vuong,Anna Walling,Haohuan Wang,Jason Wang,XuDong Wang,Chris Whalen,Samuel Whitmore,Blake Williams,Charles Xu,Sukwon Yoo,Lili Yu,Wuming Zhang,Zhuoyang Zhang,Ury Zhilinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: π₀.₇是一个新的机器人基础模型，通过多样化的上下文条件训练，能够在未见过的环境中执行多样化语言指令，实现零样本跨具身泛化，并在多种任务上达到与专门RL微调模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个通用的机器人基础模型，能够在不进行特定任务微调的情况下，在各种场景中表现出色，包括多阶段任务、跨具身泛化和复杂操作任务，减少对专门化模型的需求。

Method: 采用多样化的上下文条件训练方法，模型不仅接受语言指令作为条件，还接受额外的多模态信息，包括任务性能元数据和子目标图像，从而能够精确控制模型以不同策略执行任务。这种方法使得模型能够利用非常多样化的数据，包括演示数据、可能次优的自主数据（包括失败数据）以及非机器人来源的数据。

Result: π₀.₇在多种任务和机器人平台上表现出色：能够遵循未见环境中的多样化语言指令（包括多阶段厨房任务）；实现零样本跨具身泛化（如从未见过的叠衣服任务）；在操作意式咖啡机等挑战性任务上，性能与专门RL微调模型相当。模型在需要速度和灵巧性、语言遵循以及组合任务泛化的任务上均表现良好。

Conclusion: 通过多样化的上下文条件训练，π₀.₇作为一个机器人基础模型，能够实现强大的开箱即用性能，在多种场景中表现出色，包括语言指令遵循、跨具身泛化和复杂操作任务，为通用机器人能力的发展提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: We present a new robotic foundation model, called $π_{0.7}$, that can enable strong out-of-the-box performance in a wide range of scenarios. $π_{0.7}$ can follow diverse language instructions in unseen environments, including multi-stage tasks with various kitchen appliances, provide zero-shot cross-embodiment generalization, for example enabling a robot to fold laundry without seeing the task before, and perform challenging tasks such as operating an espresso machine out of the box at a level of performance that matches much more specialized RL-finetuned models. The main idea behind $π_{0.7}$ is to use diverse context conditioning during training. This conditioning information, contained in the prompt, makes it possible to steer the model precisely to perform many tasks with different strategies. It is conditioned not just on a language command that describes what it should do, but on additional multimodal information that also describes the manner or strategy in which it should do it, including metadata about task performance and subgoal images. This enables $π_{0.7}$ to use very diverse data, including demonstrations, potentially suboptimal (autonomous) data including failures, and data from non-robot sources. Our experiments evaluate $π_{0.7}$ across numerous tasks with multiple robot platforms, on tasks that require speed and dexterity, language following, and compositional task generalization.

</details>


### [106] [FineSteer: A Unified Framework for Fine-Grained Inference-Time Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15488)
*Zixuan Weng,Jinghuai Zhang,Kunlin Cai,Ying Li,Peiran Wang,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FineSteer是一个新颖的推理时引导框架，通过条件引导和细粒度向量合成两阶段设计，实现对LLM行为的精细控制，在安全性和真实性任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时引导方法通常采用一刀切的设计，难以同时实现有效性、效用保持和训练效率。LLM常表现出安全违规和幻觉等不良行为，需要更精细的控制方法。

Method: FineSteer将推理时引导分解为两个互补阶段：1) 子空间引导的条件引导(SCS)机制，避免不必要的引导以保持模型效用；2) 混合引导专家(MoSE)机制，捕捉期望引导行为的多模态特性，生成查询特定的引导向量。

Result: 在安全和真实性基准测试中，FineSteer在整体性能上优于最先进方法，实现了更强的引导性能且效用损失最小。

Conclusion: FineSteer通过两阶段分解和专门设计，实现了对LLM推理时行为的精细控制，在保持通用查询性能的同时，针对特定输入自适应优化引导向量，具有训练效率高的优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often exhibit undesirable behaviors, such as safety violations and hallucinations. Although inference-time steering offers a cost-effective way to adjust model behavior without updating its parameters, existing methods often fail to be simultaneously effective, utility-preserving, and training-efficient due to their rigid, one-size-fits-all designs and limited adaptability. In this work, we present FineSteer, a novel steering framework that decomposes inference-time steering into two complementary stages: conditional steering and fine-grained vector synthesis, allowing fine-grained control over when and how to steer internal representations. In the first stage, we introduce a Subspace-guided Conditional Steering (SCS) mechanism that preserves model utility by avoiding unnecessary steering. In the second stage, we propose a Mixture-of-Steering-Experts (MoSE) mechanism that captures the multimodal nature of desired steering behaviors and generates query-specific steering vectors for improved effectiveness. Through tailored designs in both SCS and MoSE, FineSteer maintains robust performance on general queries while adaptively optimizing steering vectors for targeted inputs in a training-efficient manner. Extensive experiments on safety and truthfulness benchmarks show that FineSteer outperforms state-of-the-art methods in overall performance, achieving stronger steering performance with minimal utility loss. Code is available at https://github.com/YukinoAsuna/FineSteer

</details>


### [107] [Predicting Where Steering Vectors Succeed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15557)
*Jayadev Billa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出线性可访问性剖面(LAP)作为预测导向向量有效性的诊断工具，通过应用解嵌入矩阵到中间隐藏状态来评估概念的可线性访问性，无需训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前导向向量方法在某些概念和层上有效，但在其他情况下失败，且实践者无法在干预前预测哪种设置适用，需要一种预测导向向量有效性的诊断方法。

Method: 引入线性可访问性剖面(LAP)，将logit lens重新用作导向向量有效性的预测器。关键度量A_lin应用模型的解嵌入矩阵到中间隐藏状态，无需训练。通过24个受控二元概念族在五个模型上进行验证。

Result: 峰值A_lin预测导向有效性达到ρ=+0.86到+0.91，层选择预测达到ρ=+0.63到+0.92。提出三机制框架解释何时均值差导向有效、何时需要非线性方法、何时任何方法都无效。实体导向演示验证了预测效果。

Conclusion: LAP为预测导向向量有效性提供了可靠的诊断工具，能够指导层选择并解释导向机制，显著改进了当前启发式方法（如中间层选择）的效果。

Abstract: Steering vectors work for some concepts and layers but fail for others, and practitioners have no way to predict which setting applies before running an intervention. We introduce the Linear Accessibility Profile (LAP), a per-layer diagnostic that repurposes the logit lens as a predictor of steering vector effectiveness. The key measure, $A_{\mathrm{lin}}$, applies the model's unembedding matrix to intermediate hidden states, requiring no training. Across 24 controlled binary concept families on five models (Pythia-2.8B to Llama-8B), peak $A_{\mathrm{lin}}$ predicts steering effectiveness at $ρ= +0.86$ to $+0.91$ and layer selection at $ρ= +0.63$ to $+0.92$. A three-regime framework explains when difference-of-means steering works, when nonlinear methods are needed, and when no method can work. An entity-steering demo confirms the prediction end-to-end: steering at the LAP-recommended layer redirects completions on Gemma-2-2B and OLMo-2-1B-Instruct, while the middle layer (the standard heuristic) has no effect on either model.

</details>


### [108] [Reward Weighted Classifier-Free Guidance as Policy Improvement in Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15577)
*Alexander Peysakhovich,William Berman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出奖励加权分类器自由引导（RCFG）作为策略改进算子，可在测试时优化新奖励函数，无需重新训练


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习方法在奖励函数变化时需要重新训练，缺乏灵活性；需要一种能在测试时适应新奖励函数的方法

Method: 提出奖励加权分类器自由引导（RCFG），作为策略改进算子，近似通过Q函数倾斜采样分布；应用于分子生成，并作为教师策略蒸馏到基础策略中

Result: RCFG能在测试时优化新奖励函数；作为教师策略蒸馏到基础策略中可显著加速标准强化学习的收敛

Conclusion: RCFG提供了一种灵活的策略改进方法，可在测试时适应奖励函数变化，同时可作为强化学习的有效预热策略

Abstract: Consider an auto-regressive model that produces outputs x (e.g., answers to questions, molecules) each of which can be summarized by an attribute vector y (e.g., helpfulness vs. harmlessness, or bio-availability vs. lipophilicity). An arbitrary reward function r(y) encodes tradeoffs between these properties. Typically, tilting the model's sampling distribution to increase this reward is done at training time via reinforcement learning. However, if the reward function changes, re-alignment requires re-training. In this paper, we show that a reward weighted classifier-free guidance (RCFG) can act as a policy improvement operator in this setting, approximating tilting the sampling distribution by the Q function. We apply RCFG to molecular generation, demonstrating that it can optimize novel reward functions at test time. Finally, we show that using RCFG as a teacher and distilling into the base policy to serve as a warm start significantly speeds up convergence for standard RL.

</details>


### [109] [PAWN: Piece Value Analysis with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15585)
*Ethan Tang,Hasan Davulcu,Jia Zou,Zhongju Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用CNN自编码器提取棋盘潜在位置表示，结合MLP显著提升国际象棋棋子相对价值预测精度，相比无上下文系统减少16%验证MAE


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测国际象棋棋子在特定局面中的相对价值是一个开放挑战，因为棋子的贡献取决于其与棋盘上所有其他棋子的空间关系。传统方法缺乏对完整棋盘状态的考虑。

Method: 采用CNN自编码器提取完整棋盘的潜在位置表示，将其作为上下文信息输入MLP架构进行棋子价值预测。使用超过1200万个棋子-价值对数据集，标签由Stockfish 17生成。

Result: 增强的棋子价值预测器显著优于无上下文的MLP系统，验证集平均绝对误差减少16%，相对棋子价值预测误差约为0.65个兵的价值。

Conclusion: 将完整问题状态编码为上下文信息为预测任何个体组件的贡献提供了有用的归纳偏置，这一方法可推广到其他领域。

Abstract: Predicting the relative value of any given chess piece in a position remains an open challenge, as a piece's contribution depends on its spatial relationships with every other piece on the board. We demonstrate that incorporating the state of the full chess board via latent position representations derived using a CNN-based autoencoder significantly improves accuracy for MLP-based piece value prediction architectures. Using a dataset of over 12 million piece-value pairs gathered from Grandmaster-level games, with ground-truth labels generated by Stockfish 17, our enhanced piece value predictor significantly outperforms context-independent MLP-based systems, reducing validation mean absolute error by 16% and predicting relative piece value within approximately 0.65 pawns. More generally, our findings suggest that encoding the full problem state as context provides useful inductive bias for predicting the contribution of any individual component.

</details>


### [110] [Adapting in the Dark: Efficient and Stable Test-Time Adaptation for Black-Box Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15609)
*Yunbei Zhang,Shuaicheng Niu,Chengyi Cai,Feng Liu,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BETA框架通过轻量级本地白盒导向模型为黑盒API模型提供高效测试时适应，无需额外API调用，在ImageNet-C上显著提升性能并大幅降低查询成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒模型（仅通过API访问）的测试时适应（TTA）研究不足。现有方法如后处理输出细化适应能力有限，而零阶优化（ZOO）虽然支持输入空间适应但查询成本高且优化困难

Method: BETA采用轻量级本地白盒导向模型创建可处理的梯度路径，结合预测协调技术、一致性正则化和面向提示学习的过滤机制，实现稳定适应

Result: 在ImageNet-C上，BETA使ViT-B/16准确率提升+7.1%，CLIP提升+3.4%，超越TENT和TPT等强白盒/灰盒方法。在商业API上，以250倍低成本达到与ZOO相当性能，保持实时推理速度

Conclusion: BETA为现实世界黑盒TTA提供了实用高效解决方案，通过本地导向模型实现稳定适应，无需额外API调用，显著降低查询成本并保持实时性能

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) for black-box models accessible only via APIs remains a largely unexplored challenge. Existing approaches such as post-hoc output refinement offer limited adaptive capacity, while Zeroth-Order Optimization (ZOO) enables input-space adaptation but faces high query costs and optimization challenges in the unsupervised TTA setting. We introduce BETA (Black-box Efficient Test-time Adaptation), a framework that addresses these limitations by employing a lightweight, local white-box steering model to create a tractable gradient pathway. Through a prediction harmonization technique combined with consistency regularization and prompt learning-oriented filtering, BETA enables stable adaptation with no additional API calls and negligible latency beyond standard inference. On ImageNet-C, BETA achieves a +7.1% accuracy gain on ViT-B/16 and +3.4% on CLIP, surpassing strong white-box and gray-box methods including TENT and TPT. On a commercial API, BETA achieves comparable performance to ZOO at 250x lower cost while maintaining real-time inference speed, establishing it as a practical and efficient solution for real-world black-box TTA.

</details>


### [111] [Flexible Empowerment at Reasoning with Extended Best-of-N Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15614)
*Taisuke Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合赋能在强化学习中推理动作的新方法，通过扩展的BoN采样实现探索-利用困境的灵活平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将赋能作为内在奖励项引入强化学习，但这种方法需要学习包含赋能的策略，导致调整探索强调程度的延迟。BoN采样技术能够隐式获取修改后的策略而无需显式学习，有望实现更灵活的探索-利用平衡。

Method: 提出基于Tsalis统计量扩展的BoN采样方法，将赋能纳入动作推理过程。该方法通过扩展的BoN采样调整策略修改程度，在保持计算成本的同时实现通用化调整。

Result: 通过玩具问题验证了所提方法平衡探索-利用困境的能力，并在复杂运动任务中展示了强化学习性能的提升。

Conclusion: 基于Tsalis统计量扩展的BoN采样方法能够有效将赋能纳入强化学习推理过程，实现更灵活的探索-利用平衡，提升复杂任务的解决能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel method that incorporates empowerment when reasoning actions in reinforcement learning (RL), thereby achieving the flexibility of exploration-exploitation dilemma (EED). In previous methods, empowerment for promoting exploration has been provided as a bonus term to the task-specific reward function as an intrinsically-motivated RL. However, this approach introduces a delay until the policy that accounts for empowerment is learned, making it difficult to adjust the emphasis on exploration as needed. On the other hand, a trick devised for fine-tuning recent foundation models at reasoning, so-called best-of-N (BoN) sampling, allows for the implicit acquisition of modified policies without explicitly learning them. It is expected that applying this trick to exploration-promoting terms, such as empowerment, will enable more flexible adjustment of EED. Therefore, this paper investigates BoN sampling for empowerment. Furthermore, to adjust the degree of policy modification in a generalizable manner while maintaining computational cost, this paper proposes a novel BoN sampling method extended by Tsalis statistics. Through toy problems, the proposed method's cability to balance EED is verified. In addition, it is demonstrated that the proposed method improves RL performance to solve complex locomotion tasks.

</details>


### [112] [Majority Voting for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15618)
*Tim Launer,Jonas Hübotter,Marco Bagatella,Ido Hakimi,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FMV通过函数共识识别代码生成的代表性解决方案，显著提升LiveCodeBench性能；扩展应用于无标签测试时强化学习，提高pass@1但未突破基础模型性能上限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基于函数共识的代码生成方法，旨在通过运行时执行签名识别多个生成方案中的代表性解决方案，提升大语言模型在代码生成任务中的性能

Method: 提出Functional Majority Voting (FMV)方法：基于函数共识，通过测试输入上的运行时执行签名从多个生成方案中识别代表性解决方案；扩展应用于无标签测试时强化学习的聚合策略

Result: FMV作为测试时推理策略显著提升LiveCodeBench性能且计算开销小；作为无标签测试时强化学习聚合策略提高holdout任务的pass@1，但未实现超越基础模型性能上限的自我改进

Conclusion: FMV是有效的代码生成测试时推理方法，可扩展应用于强化学习聚合，但存在性能上限限制，未能实现真正的自我改进

Abstract: We investigate Functional Majority Voting (FMV), a method based on functional consensus for code generation with Large Language Models, which identifies a representative solution from multiple generations using their runtime execution signatures on test inputs. We find that FMV is an effective test-time inference strategy, substantially boosting performance on LiveCodeBench without a large compute overhead. Furthermore, we extend the utility of functional consensus and apply it as an aggregation strategy for label-free Test-Time Reinforcement Learning. We demonstrate that this increases pass@1 on holdout tasks, but find no evidence of self-improvement beyond the base model's performance ceiling.

</details>


### [113] [NK-GAD: Neighbor Knowledge-Enhanced Unsupervised Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15668)
*Zehao Wang,Lanjun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NK-GAD：一种基于邻居知识增强的无监督图异常检测框架，针对属性级异质性图设计，通过联合编码器、邻居重建模块、中心聚合模块和双解码器提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的无监督图异常检测方法大多依赖同质性假设，但现实图中的属性级异质性使得连接节点可能具有不相似属性。分析发现当前方法不实用：1）不同连接节点对类型的属性相似度分布几乎相同；2）异常在频谱能量分布的低频和高频部分引起一致变化趋势，而中频部分变化更不稳定。

Method: 提出NK-GAD框架，包含：1）联合编码器捕获相似和不相似邻居特征；2）邻居重建模块建模正常分布；3）中心聚合模块精炼节点特征；4）双解码器分别重建属性和结构。

Result: 在七个数据集上的实验表明，NK-GAD相比现有方法平均AUC提升3.29%。

Conclusion: NK-GAD通过有效处理属性级异质性，为无监督图异常检测提供了更实用的解决方案，在多个数据集上表现出显著性能提升。

Abstract: Graph anomaly detection aims to identify irregular patterns in graph-structured data. Most unsupervised GNN-based methods rely on the homophily assumption that connected nodes share similar attributes. However, real-world graphs often exhibit attribute-level heterophily, where connected nodes have dissimilar attributes. Our analysis of attribute-level heterophily graphs reveals two phenomena indicating that current approaches are not practical for unsupervised graph anomaly detection: 1) attribute similarities between connected nodes show nearly identical distributions across different connected node pair types, and 2) anomalies cause consistent variation trends between the graph with and without anomalous edges in the low- and high-frequency components of the spectral energy distributions, while the mid-part exhibits more erratic variations. Based on these observations, we propose NK-GAD, a neighbor knowledge-enhanced unsupervised graph anomaly detection framework. NK-GAD integrates a joint encoder capturing both similar and dissimilar neighbor features, a neighbor reconstruction module modeling normal distributions, a center aggregation module refining node features, and dual decoders for reconstructing attributes and structures. Experiments on seven datasets show NK-GAD achieves an average 3.29\% AUC improvement.

</details>


### [114] [Hierarchical Active Inference using Successor Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15679)
*Prashant Rangarajan,Rajesh P. N. Rao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于层次主动推理的规划模型，结合环境层次模型与后继表示，实现高效规划，并在多个规划与强化学习任务中验证性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动推理作为基于自由能原则的神经启发模型，是理解大脑感知、动作和学习的统一框架。虽然已用于导航和规划等生态重要任务，但扩展到解决现实世界复杂大规模问题仍具挑战。受大脑多尺度层次表征启发，需要开发层次主动推理模型以实现高效规划。

Method: 提出基于层次主动推理的规划模型，结合环境层次模型与后继表示。具体包括：(1) 使用低层后继表示学习高层抽象状态；(2) 基于低层主动推理的规划用于引导和学习高层抽象动作；(3) 利用学习到的高层抽象状态和动作实现高效规划。

Result: 在多个规划与强化学习问题上验证模型性能：四房间任务变体、基于钥匙的导航任务、部分可观测规划问题、Mountain Car问题以及具有连续状态和动作空间的PointMaze导航任务族。结果表明模型能有效学习层次状态和动作抽象，并实现高效规划。

Conclusion: 该研究首次将学习到的层次状态和动作抽象应用于基于自由能原则的大脑功能理论中的主动推理，为复杂大规模问题的层次规划提供了有效框架，展示了层次主动推理在解决现实世界复杂任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Active inference, a neurally-inspired model for inferring actions based on the free energy principle (FEP), has been proposed as a unifying framework for understanding perception, action, and learning in the brain. Active inference has previously been used to model ecologically important tasks such as navigation and planning, but scaling it to solve complex large-scale problems in real-world environments has remained a challenge. Inspired by the existence of multi-scale hierarchical representations in the brain, we propose a model for planning of actions based on hierarchical active inference. Our approach combines a hierarchical model of the environment with successor representations for efficient planning. We present results demonstrating (1) how lower-level successor representations can be used to learn higher-level abstract states, (2) how planning based on active inference at the lower-level can be used to bootstrap and learn higher-level abstract actions, and (3) how these learned higher-level abstract states and actions can facilitate efficient planning. We illustrate the performance of the approach on several planning and reinforcement learning (RL) problems including a variant of the well-known four rooms task, a key-based navigation task, a partially observable planning problem, the Mountain Car problem, and PointMaze, a family of navigation tasks with continuous state and action spaces. Our results represent, to our knowledge, the first application of learned hierarchical state and action abstractions to active inference in FEP-based theories of brain function.

</details>


### [115] [DPrivBench: Benchmarking LLMs' Reasoning for Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15851)
*Erchi Wang,Pengrun Huang,Eli Chien,Om Thakkar,Kamalika Chaudhuri,Yu-Xiang Wang,Ruihan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出DPrivBench基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在差分隐私推理方面的能力，发现现有模型在处理高级算法时存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私算法设计和验证需要专家级推理，对非专家从业者构成高门槛。现有方法要么依赖需要大量领域知识的专门验证语言，要么是半自动且需要人工指导。作者研究大语言模型是否能自动化差分隐私推理。

Method: 引入DPrivBench基准测试，每个实例询问在特定假设下函数或算法是否满足声明的差分隐私保证。基准设计涵盖广泛的DP主题、不同难度级别，并通过抵抗简单模式匹配来防止捷径推理。

Result: 实验显示最强模型能较好处理教科书机制，但所有模型在处理高级算法时都遇到困难，揭示了当前DP推理能力的显著差距。通过进一步分析研究和失败模式分析，确定了改进自动化DP推理的几个有前景方向。

Conclusion: DPrivBench为开发和评估自动化差分隐私推理方法提供了坚实基础，并补充了现有的数学推理基准。研究揭示了当前大语言模型在复杂DP推理方面的局限性，为未来改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) has a wide range of applications for protecting data privacy, but designing and verifying DP algorithms requires expert-level reasoning, creating a high barrier for non-expert practitioners. Prior works either rely on specialized verification languages that demand substantial domain expertise or remain semi-automated and require human-in-the-loop guidance. In this work, we investigate whether large language models (LLMs) can automate DP reasoning. We introduce DPrivBench, a benchmark in which each instance asks whether a function or algorithm satisfies a stated DP guarantee under specified assumptions. The benchmark is carefully designed to cover a broad range of DP topics, span diverse difficulty levels, and resist shortcut reasoning through trivial pattern matching. Experiments show that while the strongest models handle textbook mechanisms well, all models struggle with advanced algorithms, revealing substantial gaps in current DP reasoning capabilities. Through further analytic study and failure-mode analysis, we identify several promising directions for improving automated DP reasoning. Our benchmark provides a solid foundation for developing and evaluating such methods, and complements existing benchmarks for mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [116] [Graph self-supervised learning based on frequency corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15699)
*Haojie Li,Mengjiao Zhang,Guanfeng Liu,Qiang Hu,Yan Wang,Junwei Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FC-GSSL是一种基于频率破坏的图自监督学习方法，通过破坏低频贡献的节点和边来构建偏向高频信息的破坏图，使用自编码器重建低频和一般特征，并设计多种采样策略生成多样破坏图进行表示对齐，以融合多频带信息并提升泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图自监督学习方法往往未能充分利用高频信号，且容易过拟合到特定局部模式，这限制了表示质量和泛化能力。需要一种能够融合多频带信息并减少对特定高频成分依赖的方法。

Method: 提出频率破坏图自监督学习(FC-GSSL)：1)根据节点的低频贡献破坏节点和边，构建偏向高频信息的破坏图；2)使用自编码器以破坏图为输入，重建低频和一般特征作为监督目标；3)设计多种采样策略，从采样结果的交集和并集生成多样破坏图；4)对齐这些视图中的节点表示，发现有用的频率组合。

Result: 在14个数据集上的节点分类、图预测和迁移学习实验中，FC-GSSL一致地提升了性能和泛化能力，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: FC-GSSL通过频率破坏策略和多样视图对齐，能够有效融合多频带信息，减少对特定高频成分的依赖，提升图自监督学习的表示质量和泛化能力。

Abstract: Graph self-supervised learning can reduce the need for labeled graph data and has been widely used in recommendation, social networks, and other web applications.
